### **Contoh Output yang Akan Saya Berikan:**
```markdown
# MusGo-OS 2in1 - Browser Module Blueprint
## 1. Overview
- Deskripsi: Browser terintegrasi untuk MusGo-OS 2in1 dengan fitur AI-optimized.
- Tujuan: Menyediakan pengalaman browsing yang aman, cepat, dan terintegrasi dengan ekosistem MusGo-OS.

## 2. Struktur Folder
```
musgo-os-2in1/
├── modules/
│   ├── browser/
│   │   ├── src/
│   │   │   ├── core/          # Inti browser (rendering, networking)
│   │   │   ├── ui/            # Antarmuka pengguna
│   │   │   ├── extensions/    # Plugin/ekstensi
│   │   │   ├── ai/            # Integrasi AI (prompt optimization, chatbot)
│   │   │   └── config/        # Konfigurasi default
│   │   ├── assets/            # Static files (CSS, JS, icons)
│   │   ├── tests/             # Unit & integration tests
│   │   ├── docs/              # Dokumentasi teknis
│   │   └── CMakeLists.txt     # Build configuration (jika C++)
│   └── ...
├── configs/
│   └── browser_settings.json # Konfigurasi global browser
└── ...
```

## 3. Komponen Utama
### 3.1 Core Engine
- **Rendering:** Chromium Embedded Framework (CEF)
- **Networking:** Custom HTTP stack dengan proxy support
- **Security:** Sandboxing, Content Security Policy (CSP), anti-fingerprinting

### 3.2 UI/UX
- **Tab System:** Multi-tab dengan session management
- **AI Sidebar:** Integrasi MusGo untuk optimasi prompt
- **Theme:** Dark/light mode, customizable

## 4. Integrasi dengan MusGo-OS
- **Prompt Optimization:** Otomatis mengoptimalkan prompt sebelum dikirim ke AI
- **File System Access:** Browser dapat berinteraksi dengan file lokal (dengan izin)
- **System Notifications:** Pemberitahuan terintegrasi dengan OS

## 5. Security & Privacy
- **Sandboxing:** Setiap tab berjalan dalam sandbox terpisah
- **Data Encryption:** History & bookmark di-encrypt
- **Anti-Tracking:** Blokir tracker & cookie pihak ketiga

## 6. Performance
- **Lazy Loading:** Tab tidak aktif tidak memakan resource
- **Caching:** Cache agresif untuk static assets

## 7. Build & Deployment
- **Dependencies:** CEF, Qt (untuk UI), OpenSSL
- **Build System:** CMake (C++) / Webpack (JS)
- **Target Platform:** Windows, Linux, macOS

## 8. Testing
- **Unit Tests:** Core engine, UI components
- **Integration Tests:** Interaksi dengan modul MusGo-OS lain
- **E2E Tests:** User flow (buka tab, navigasi, AI integration)

## 9. Dokumentasi
- **Developer Guide:** Cara menambahkan ekstensi/plugin
- **User Guide:** Fitur-fitur browser
```

### **MusGo-OS 2in1 - Browser Module Blueprint**
*(Enterprise-Grade | Python Backend | TypeScript/Python SDK)*

---

#### **1. Overview**
- **Deskripsi:**
  Browser terintegrasi dalam **MusGo-OS 2in1** sebagai **app ecosystem** yang menyediakan pengalaman browsing dengan fitur **AI-optimized**, **security-hardened**, dan **cross-platform**.
- **Tujuan:**
  - Menyediakan browser yang **aman, cepat, dan terintegrasi** dengan sistem operasi.
  - Mendukung **ekosistem MusGo-OS** (misal: integrasi dengan AI agent, file system, notifications).
  - **Extensible** untuk plugin/ekstensi custom.

---

#### **2. Struktur Folder & Subfolder**
```
musgo-os-2in1/
├── apps/
│   └── browser/                          # Root folder untuk app browser
│       ├── src/
│       │   ├── core/                     # Inti browser (rendering, networking, security)
│       │   │   ├── engine/                # Rendering engine (CEF/WebKit wrapper)
│       │   │   │   ├── python/            # Backend logic (Python)
│       │   │   │   │   ├── rendering.py   # Handle rendering via CEF/WebKit
│       │   │   │   │   ├── networking.py   # HTTP/HTTPS stack, proxy, caching
│       │   │   │   │   ├── security.py     # Sandboxing, CSP, anti-tracking
│       │   │   │   │   └── ...
│       │   │   │   └── typescript/        # SDK (TypeScript) untuk integrasi frontend
│       │   │   │       ├── interfaces/    # Type definitions
│       │   │   │       ├── services/      # Browser services (tab, bookmark, dll.)
│       │   │   │       └── ...
│       │   │   ├── session/               # Session management (tabs, cookies, localStorage)
│       │   │   │   ├── python/
│       │   │   │   │   ├── tab_manager.py
│       │   │   │   │   ├── cookie_jar.py
│       │   │   │   │   └── ...
│       │   │   │   └── typescript/
│       │   │   │       ├── session.ts
│       │   │   │       └── ...
│       │   │   └── ai/                    # Integrasi AI (MusGo)
│       │   │       ├── python/
│       │   │       │   ├── prompt_optimizer.py  # Optimasi prompt sebelum dikirim ke AI
│       │   │       │   ├── chatbot_integration.py
│       │   │       │   └── ...
│       │   │       └── typescript/
│       │   │           ├── ai_client.ts   # SDK untuk berinteraksi dengan AI
│       │   │           └── ...

│       │   │   │   ├── TabBar.tsx
│       │   │   │   ├── Sidebar.tsx
│       │   │   │   ├── AIChatPanel.tsx
│       │   │   │   └── ...

│       │   │   ├── themes/               # Dark/light mode

```

---

#### **3. Komponen Utama & Detail Blueprint**
*(Setiap bagian dijelaskan dalam bahasa manusia, dengan logic enterprise-grade)*

---
##### **3.1 Core Engine**
**Deskripsi:**
Inti browser yang menangani **rendering, networking, dan security**.

**Subkomponen:**
1. **Rendering Engine (CEF/WebKit Wrapper)**
   - **Fungsi:**
     - Render halaman web menggunakan **Chromium Embedded Framework (CEF)** atau **WebKit**.
     - Mendukung **multi-tab** dengan isolasi proses.
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `rendering.py`:
       - Inisialisasi **CEF/WebKit**.
       - Manage **browser instances** per tab.
       - Handle **lifecycle** (create, destroy, reload).
     - `networking.py`:
       - Custom **HTTP/HTTPS stack** dengan dukungan **proxy**.
       - **Caching** untuk static assets (agresif).
       - **Rate limiting** untuk mencegah abuse.
   - **SDK (TypeScript):**
     - `browser_service.ts`:
       - Interface untuk berinteraksi dengan backend rendering.
       - Method: `createTab()`, `closeTab()`, `navigate(url)`.

2. **Security Layer**
   - **Fungsi:**
     - **Sandboxing:** Setiap tab berjalan dalam **sandbox terpisah**.
     - **Content Security Policy (CSP):** Blokir script berbahaya.
     - **Anti-Tracking:** Blokir **tracker, fingerprinting, dan cookie pihak ketiga**.
     - **Data Encryption:** Enkripsi **history, bookmark, dan session data**.
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `security.py`:
       - Implementasi **sandboxing** (menggunakan `pybox` atau `gVisor`).
       - **CSP parser** untuk header HTTP.
       - **Anti-tracking rules** (daftar domain yang diblokir).
     - **Integrity Guard:**
       - Verifikasi **hash** dari file-file kritis (misal: `settings.json`).
       - **Anti-tamper mechanism** untuk mencegah modifikasi ilegal.

---
##### **3.2 Session Management**
**Deskripsi:**
Manajemen **tabs, cookies, localStorage, dan session state**.

**Subkomponen:**
1. **Tab Manager**
   - **Fungsi:**
     - Buat, tutup, dan **switch tab**.
     - **Session restoration** (simpan/load state tab).
     - **Lazy loading** (tab tidak aktif tidak memakan resource).
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `tab_manager.py`:
       - Kelas `Tab` (id, url, status, process_id).
       - Method: `create_tab()`, `switch_tab()`, `close_tab()`.
       - **Resource enforcement:** Batas maksimal tab (misal: 50).
   - **SDK (TypeScript):**
     - `tab_service.ts`:
       - Event: `onTabCreated`, `onTabClosed`, `onTabSwitched`.

2. **Cookie & Storage**
   - **Fungsi:**
     - Manajemen **cookies, localStorage, dan sessionStorage**.
     - **Encrypted storage** untuk data sensitif.
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `cookie_jar.py`:
       - Simpan cookies per **domain**.
       - **Encryption/decryption** (menggunakan `cryptography` library).
       - **Validation:** Cek **expiry, domain, dan path**.

---
##### **3.3 AI Integration (MusGo)**
**Deskripsi:**
Integrasi dengan **MusGo AI** untuk **prompt optimization, chatbot, dan fitur AI lainnya**.

**Subkomponen:**
1. **Prompt Optimizer**
   - **Fungsi:**
     - **Auto-optimize** prompt sebelum dikirim ke AI.
     - Gunakan **metode 4-D** (Dekonstruksi, Diagnosa, Kembangkan, Sajikan).
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `prompt_optimizer.py`:
       - Method: `optimize_prompt(raw_prompt: str) -> str`.
       - **Multi-layer validation:**
         - Cek **ambiguitas**.
         - Cek **kelengkapan konteks**.
       - **Logging:** Simpan **original vs. optimized prompt**.

2. **Chatbot Integration**
   - **Fungsi:**
     - **Sidebar AI** untuk berinteraksi dengan user.
     - **Context-aware** (mengingat history percakapan).
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `chatbot_integration.py`:
       - Koneksi ke **MusGo AI API**.
       - **Session management** untuk chat.
   - **SDK (TypeScript):**
     - `ai_client.ts`:
       - Method: `sendMessage(message: string) -> Promise<string>`.
       - Event: `onAIResponse`.

---
##### **3.4 UI/UX**
**Deskripsi:**
Antarmuka pengguna yang **responsif, customizable, dan terintegrasi dengan AI**.

**Subkomponen:**
1. **Tab Bar**
   - **Fitur:**
     - **Multi-tab** dengan preview thumbnail.
     - **Drag & drop** untuk reorder tab.
   - **Frontend (TypeScript/React):**
     - `TabBar.tsx`:
       - State: `activeTabId`, `tabs: Tab[]`.
       - Event: `onTabClick`, `onTabClose`.

2. **AI Sidebar**
   - **Fitur:**
     - **Prompt optimization** (tombol "Optimize").
     - **Chatbot** (real-time).
   - **Frontend (TypeScript):**
     - `AIChatPanel.tsx`:
       - Method: `handlePromptSubmit(prompt: string)`.

3. **Themes**
   - **Fitur:**
     - **Dark/light mode**.
     - **Customizable colors**.
   - **Frontend (CSS/TypeScript):**
     - `themes/`:
       - File: `dark.css`, `light.css`.

---
##### **3.5 Extensions/Plugins**
**Deskripsi:**
Dukungan untuk **ekstensi custom** (misal: ad-blocker, translator).

**Subkomponen:**
1. **Extension Loader**
   - **Fungsi:**
     - Load/unload **ekstensi**.
     - **Sandboxing** untuk ekstensi.
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `extension_loader.py`:
       - Method: `load_extension(path: str)`.
       - **Validation:** Cek **manifest.json** (nama, versi, permissions).

2. **Extension API (SDK)**
   - **Fungsi:**
     - **TypeScript SDK** untuk developer ekstensi.
   - **SDK (TypeScript):**
     - `extension_api.ts`:
       - Interface: `BrowserExtension`.
       - Method: `registerContentScript()`, `addToolbarButton()`.

---
##### **3.6 Config & Settings**
**Deskripsi:**
Konfigurasi **default, security policies, dan user preferences**.

**File:**
- `default_settings.json`:
  ```json
  {
    "browser": {
      "homepage": "https://musgo-os.2in1.com",
      "max_tabs": 50,
      "default_search_engine": "Google",
      "enable_sandboxing": true,
      "enable_ai_integration": true
    },
    "security": {
      "csp_enabled": true,
      "anti_tracking": true,
      "encrypt_history": true
    }
  }
  ```
- `security_policies.json`:
  - Daftar **allowed/blocked domains**.
  - **CSP rules**.

---
##### **3.7 Security & Privacy (Detail)**
**1. Sandboxing:**
   - Setiap tab berjalan dalam **process terpisah** (menggunakan `multiprocessing`).
   - **Resource limits:** CPU, memory, disk I/O.

**2. Input Sanitization:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `validators.py`:
       - Method: `sanitize_url(url: str) -> str`.
       - **Regex-based validation** untuk URL, HTML, JS.

**3. Injection Prevention:**
   - **SQL Injection:** Gunakan **parameterized queries** (jika ada DB).
   - **XSS Prevention:** Escape **HTML/JS** di input user.

**4. Rate Limiting:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `rate_limiter.py`:
       - Batas **request per menit** (misal: 100 request/minute).

**5. Privilege Enforcement:**
   - **Role-based access** (misal: `admin`, `user`).
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `access_control.py`:
       - Method: `check_permission(user: User, action: str) -> bool`.

---
##### **3.8 Performance Optimization**
**1. Lazy Loading:**
   - Tab **tidak aktif** tidak memuat **CSS/JS/images**.

**2. Caching:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `cache_manager.py`:
       - **LRU Cache** untuk static assets.
       - **TTL (Time-to-Live)** untuk cache.

**3. Preloading:**
   - **Preload** halaman yang **sering dikunjungi**.

---
##### **3.9 Event Broker Interaction**
*(Jika browser berinteraksi dengan modul lain via event broker)*
**Fungsi:**
- **Publish event** (misal: `tab_opened`, `ai_prompt_optimized`).
- **Subscribe event** (misal: `system_notification`).

**Backend (Python):**
- `event_broker.py`:
  - Method: `publish(event: str, data: dict)`.
  - **Retry strategy:** Coba 3x sebelum **dead-letter queue**.

---
##### **3.10 Transaction Control**
*(Jika ada operasi yang memerlukan atomicity)*
**Contoh:**
- **Simpan bookmark + history** dalam **1 transaksi**.
- **Backend (Python):**
  - `transaction_manager.py`:
    - Method: `begin_transaction()`, `commit()`, `rollback()`.

---
##### **3.11 Error Handling & Logging**
**1. Structured Error Model:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `exceptions.py`:
       - Kelas: `BrowserError`, `SecurityError`, `NetworkError`.

**2. Logging:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `logger.py`:
       - Level: `INFO`, `WARNING`, `ERROR`.
       - Format: `[TIMESTAMP] [LEVEL] [MODULE] message`.

**3. No Silent Failure:**
   - Setiap error **dilog** dan **ditampilkan ke user** (jika perlu).

---
##### **3.12 Multi-Layer Validation**
**1. Input Validation:**
   - Cek **format URL**, **panjang input**, dll.

**2. Business Validation:**
   - Cek **apakah tab sudah terbuka** sebelum buat tab baru.

**3. Constraint Validation:**
   - Cek **batasan resource** (misal: max tabs).

**4. State Validation:**
   - Cek **apakah browser sudah diinisialisasi**.

**5. Permission Validation:**
   - Cek **apakah user punya hak** untuk aksi tertentu.

---
##### **3.13 Integrity Guard**
**1. State Consistency Check:**
   - Cek **apakah state tab konsisten** (misal: URL valid).

**2. Duplicate Prevention:**
   - Cek **apakah tab dengan URL yang sama sudah ada**.

**3. Replay Prevention:**
   - **Nonce** untuk request kritis (misal: delete history).

---
##### **3.14 Build & Deployment**
**1. Dependencies:**
   - **Python:**
     - `cefpython3` (untuk CEF).
     - `pybox` (untuk sandboxing).
     - `cryptography` (untuk encryption).
     - `fastapi` (untuk API backend).
   - **TypeScript:**
     - `react`, `typescript`, `webpack`.

**2. Build System:**
   - **Python:** `pyproject.toml` ( Poetry).
   - **TypeScript:** `package.json` (npm/yarn).

**3. Target Platform:**
   - **Windows, Linux, macOS** (cross-platform).

**4. Deployment:**
   - **Docker** untuk containerization.
   - **CI/CD Pipeline** (GitHub Actions/GitLab CI).

---
##### **3.15 Testing**
**1. Unit Tests:**
   - **Python:** `pytest` (untuk backend).
   - **TypeScript:** `jest` (untuk frontend).

**2. Integration Tests:**
   - Test **interaksi antara backend & frontend**.

**3. E2E Tests:**
   - **Selenium/Playwright** untuk simulasi user flow.

---
##### **3.16 Dokumentasi**
**1. Developer Guide:**
   - Cara **menambahkan ekstensi**.
   - Cara **mengintegrasikan AI**.

**2. API Reference:**
   - Daftar **endpoint backend** (Python).
   - Daftar **SDK methods** (TypeScript).

**3. User Guide:**
   - Cara menggunakan **fitur browser**.

---

#### **4. Integrasi dengan MusGo-OS 2in1**
1. **File System Access:**
   - Browser bisa **baca/tulis file** di MusGo-OS (dengan izin user).
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `file_access.py`:
       - Method: `read_file(path: str) -> bytes`.
       - **Validation:** Cek **izin akses**.

2. **System Notifications:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `notification_service.py`:
       - Method: `send_notification(title: str, message: str)`.

3. **Inter-App Communication:**
   - Browser bisa **berkomunikasi** dengan app lain di MusGo-OS (misal: **File Explorer**).

---
#### **5. Security Hardening (Tambahan)**
1. **Anti-Tampering:**
   - **Checksum** untuk file-file kritis (misal: `browser.exe`).
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `integrity_checker.py`:
       - Method: `verify_checksum(file_path: str) -> bool`.

2. **Fail-Safe Mechanism:**
   - Jika **sandbox crash**, tab **auto-reload**.
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `fail_safe.py`:
       - Method: `handle_crash(tab_id: str)`.

3. **Privilege Escalation Prevention:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `privilege_guard.py`:
       - Cek **apakah proses mencoba akses resource terlarang**.

---
#### **6. Advanced Behavior Engine**
*(Untuk logic yang kompleks, misal: keputusan otomatis)*
1. **Decision Layers:**
   - **Layer 1:** Cek **izin user**.
   - **Layer 2:** Cek **resource availability**.
   - **Layer 3:** Cek **security policies**.

2. **Evaluation Model:**
   - **Risk Scoring:** Beri skor risiko untuk **URL/ekstensi**.
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `risk_scoring.py`:
       - Method: `calculate_risk(url: str) -> float`.

3. **Enforcement Engine:**
   - **Block/Allow** berdasarkan **risk score**.
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `enforcement_engine.py`:
       - Method: `enforce_policy(action: str, risk_score: float) -> bool`.

4. **Policy Override:**
   - **Admin** bisa **override** keputusan otomatis.

---
---
### **7. Contoh Alur Eksekusi (End-to-End)**
**Skenario:** User membuka tab baru ke `https://example.com`

1. **User Action:**
   - Klik tombol **"New Tab"** di UI.

2. **Frontend (TypeScript):**
   - `TabBar.tsx` memanggil `tab_service.createTab("https://example.com")`.

3. **SDK (TypeScript):**
   - `tab_service.ts` mengirim request ke **backend Python** via **FastAPI**.

4. **Backend (Python):**
   - `tab_manager.py`:
     - Cek **apakah URL valid** (`validators.py`).
     - Cek **apakah tab sudah ada** (duplicate prevention).
     - Cek **apakah resource cukup** (max tabs).
     - Buat **new Tab instance**.
   - `rendering.py`:
     - Inisialisasi **CEF/WebKit** untuk tab baru.
     - Load URL `https://example.com`.
   - `security.py`:
     - Terapkan **CSP** dan **sandboxing**.
     - Blokir **tracker** (jika ada).
   - `logger.py`:
     - Log: `[INFO] [TabManager] Tab created: id=123, url=https://example.com`.

5. **Event Broker:**
   - Publish event `tab_opened` ke **MusGo-OS event broker**.

6. **UI Update:**
   - `TabBar.tsx` menampilkan **tab baru**.

7. **AI Integration (Opsional):**
   - Jika user **mengaktifkan AI**, `prompt_optimizer.py` mengoptimalkan **prompt default**.

---
---
### **8. Inisiatif Tambahan (Untuk Menunjukkan Kemampuan)**
1. **Feature Flag System:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `feature_flags.py`:
       - Method: `is_feature_enabled(feature: str) -> bool`.
       - Contoh: `enable_ai_integration`, `enable_sandboxing`.

2. **A/B Testing Framework:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `ab_testing.py`:
       - Method: `get_variant(user_id: str, feature: str) -> str`.
       - Contoh: Test **UI tema** (dark vs. light).

3. **Telemetry & Analytics:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `telemetry.py`:
       - Method: `track_event(event: str, metadata: dict)`.
       - Data: **tab usage, AI interactions, errors**.

4. **Auto-Update Mechanism:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `auto_updater.py`:
       - Cek **versi terbaru** dari **MusGo-OS server**.
       - Download & install **update otomatis**.

5. **Cross-Platform Compatibility Layer:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `platform_utils.py`:
       - Method: `get_os() -> str` (Windows/Linux/macOS).
       - Handle **perbedaan path** (misal: `\` vs `/`).

6. **Plugin Marketplace Integration:**
   - **Backend (Python):**
     - `plugin_marketplace.py`:
       - Method: `fetch_plugins() -> List[Plugin]`.
       - **Validation:** Cek **signature** plugin sebelum install.

---
---

"""
MusGo-OS 2in1 - Browser Module Initialization
=============================================
Modul ini merupakan root package untuk aplikasi browser terintegrasi dalam MusGo-OS 2in1.
Modul ini bertanggung jawab untuk:
- Inisialisasi komponen inti browser (core, UI, AI, ekstensi)
- Integrasi dengan ekosistem MusGo-OS 2in1
- Manajemen dependensi internal
- Penegakan standar enterprise-grade
"""

import os
import sys
import logging
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
import hashlib
import json
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
import threading
import multiprocessing
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import re
from urllib.parse import urlparse, urljoin
import socket
import ssl
import base64
import zlib
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.kdf.pbkdf2 import PBKDF2HMAC
from cryptography.hazmat.backends import default_backend
import psutil
import platform
import subprocess
import tempfile
import shutil
from functools import wraps, lru_cache
import inspect
import traceback
from collections import defaultdict, deque
import weakref
import asyncio
import aiohttp
from dataclasses import asdict
from typing import TypeVar, Generic, Callable, Union, Set

# =============================================================================
# KONSTANTA GLOBAL & KONFIGURASI DEFAULT
# =============================================================================

# Versi modul
__VERSION__ = "1.0.0-enterprise"

# Path root modul
ROOT_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.absolute()

# Path konfigurasi
CONFIG_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "configs"
DEFAULT_SETTINGS_PATH = CONFIG_PATH / "browser_settings.json"
SECURITY_POLICIES_PATH = CONFIG_PATH / "security_policies.json"

# Path asset
ASSETS_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "assets"
ICONS_PATH = ASSETS_PATH / "icons"
FONTS_PATH = ASSETS_PATH / "fonts"
STYLES_PATH = ASSETS_PATH / "styles"
SCRIPTS_PATH = ASSETS_PATH / "scripts"

# Path dokumentasi
DOCS_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "docs"

# Path testing
TESTS_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "tests"
UNIT_TESTS_PATH = TESTS_PATH / "unit"
INTEGRATION_TESTS_PATH = TESTS_PATH / "integration"
E2E_TESTS_PATH = TESTS_PATH / "e2e"

# Path untuk SDK
SDK_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "sdk"
PYTHON_SDK_PATH = SDK_PATH / "python"
TYPESCRIPT_SDK_PATH = SDK_PATH / "typescript"

# Path untuk ekstensi
EXTENSIONS_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "extensions"

# Path untuk log
LOGS_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "logs"
LOGS_PATH.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# Path untuk cache
CACHE_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "cache"
CACHE_PATH.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# Path untuk session
SESSION_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "sessions"
SESSION_PATH.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# Path untuk temporary files
TEMP_PATH = ROOT_PATH / "temp"
TEMP_PATH.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# =============================================================================
# ENUM & DATA STRUCTURE
# =============================================================================

class BrowserMode(Enum):
    """Mode operasi browser."""
    STANDARD = auto()
    PRIVATE = auto()  # Incognito mode
    DEVELOPER = auto()
    KIOSK = auto()

class TabState(Enum):
    """State dari sebuah tab."""
    LOADING = auto()
    ACTIVE = auto()
    INACTIVE = auto()
    CRASHED = auto()
    FROZEN = auto()

class SecurityLevel(Enum):
    """Level keamanan browser."""
    LOW = auto()
    MEDIUM = auto()
    HIGH = auto()
    PARANOID = auto()

class Permission(Enum):
    """Daftar izin yang bisa dimiliki oleh ekstensi atau user."""
    READ_FILESYSTEM = auto()
    WRITE_FILESYSTEM = auto()
    NETWORK_ACCESS = auto()
    CAMERA_ACCESS = auto()
    MICROPHONE_ACCESS = auto()
    NOTIFICATION_ACCESS = auto()
    TAB_MANAGEMENT = auto()
    COOKIE_ACCESS = auto()
    HISTORY_ACCESS = auto()
    AI_INTEGRATION = auto()

@dataclass
class TabConfig:
    """Konfigurasi untuk sebuah tab."""
    tab_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    url: str = "about:blank"
    title: str = "New Tab"
    state: TabState = TabState.ACTIVE
    is_pinned: bool = False
    is_muted: bool = False
    zoom_level: float = 1.0
    last_accessed: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    parent_tab_id: Optional[str] = None
    window_id: Optional[str] = None
    session_id: Optional[str] = None
    permissions: Set[Permission] = field(default_factory=set)
    security_level: SecurityLevel = SecurityLevel.MEDIUM
    user_agent: Optional[str] = None
    custom_headers: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    proxy_settings: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    cache_enabled: bool = True
    javascript_enabled: bool = True
    images_enabled: bool = True
    cookies_enabled: bool = True

@dataclass
class BrowserSettings:
    """Konfigurasi global browser."""
    homepage: str = "https://musgo-os.2in1.com"
    default_search_engine: str = "https://www.google.com/search?q="
    max_tabs: int = 50
    max_windows: int = 10
    default_browser_mode: BrowserMode = BrowserMode.STANDARD
    default_security_level: SecurityLevel = SecurityLevel.MEDIUM
    enable_sandboxing: bool = True
    enable_csp: bool = True
    enable_anti_tracking: bool = True
    enable_ai_integration: bool = True
    enable_ad_blocker: bool = True
    enable_auto_update: bool = True
    enable_telemetry: bool = False
    cache_size_mb: int = 500
    session_timeout_minutes: int = 30
    language: str = "id-ID"
    theme: str = "system"  # system, light, dark
    font_size: int = 14
    user_agent: Optional[str] = None
    proxy_settings: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    allowed_domains: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    blocked_domains: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    csp_policies: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    default_permissions: Set[Permission] = field(default_factory=set)

@dataclass
class SecurityPolicy:
    """Kebijakan keamanan browser."""
    csp_directives: Dict[str, List[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    allowed_schemes: List[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ["http", "https", "data", "file", "about", "blob"])
    blocked_schemes: List[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ["javascript", "vbscript"])
    allowed_mime_types: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    blocked_mime_types: List[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ["text/html", "application/javascript"])
    max_script_length: int = 1000000  # 1MB
    max_style_length: int = 500000  # 500KB
    max_image_size: int = 10485760  # 10MB
    enable_hsts: bool = True
    hsts_max_age: int = 31536000  # 1 tahun
    enable_xss_protection: bool = True
    enable_frame_options: bool = True
    frame_options: str = "DENY"
    enable_content_security_policy: bool = True
    enable_referrer_policy: bool = True
    referrer_policy: str = "strict-origin-when-cross-origin"

@dataclass
class ExtensionManifest:
    """Manifest untuk ekstensi browser."""
    name: str
    version: str
    description: str
    author: str
    homepage: Optional[str] = None
    permissions: List[Permission] = field(default_factory=list)
    background_scripts: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    content_scripts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    browser_action: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    page_action: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    icons: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None
    default_locale: str = "en"
    manifest_version: int = 3

@dataclass
class AIConfig:
    """Konfigurasi integrasi AI."""
    api_endpoint: str = "https://api.musgo-os.2in1.com/v1"
    api_key: Optional[str] = None
    model: str = "musgo-optimized"
    timeout_seconds: int = 30
    max_tokens: int = 4096
    temperature: float = 0.7
    top_p: float = 0.9
    enable_prompt_optimization: bool = True
    enable_context_awareness: bool = True
    enable_autocomplete: bool = True
    prompt_optimization_level: int = 2  # 1: Basic, 2: Advanced, 3: Aggressive

@dataclass
class Event:
    """Struktur data untuk event internal browser."""
    event_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    event_type: str
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    source: str
    data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

@dataclass
class Transaction:
    """Struktur data untuk manajemen transaksi."""
    transaction_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    operations: List[Callable] = field(default_factory=list)
    status: str = "pending"  # pending, committed, rolled_back
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    committed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    rolled_back_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    error: Optional[Exception] = None

# =============================================================================
# EXCEPTION & ERROR HANDLING
# =============================================================================

class BrowserError(Exception):
    """Base exception untuk browser module."""
    def __init__(self, message: str, code: int = 500, details: Optional[Dict] = None):
        self.message = message
        self.code = code
        self.details = details or {}
        self.timestamp = datetime.now()
        super().__init__(self.message)

class SecurityError(BrowserError):
    """Exception untuk kesalahan keamanan."""
    def __init__(self, message: str, code: int = 403, details: Optional[Dict] = None):
        super().__init__(message, code, details)

class ValidationError(BrowserError):
    """Exception untuk kesalahan validasi."""
    def __init__(self, message: str, code: int = 400, details: Optional[Dict] = None):
        super().__init__(message, code, details)

class ResourceError(BrowserError):
    """Exception untuk kesalahan resource."""
    def __init__(self, message: str, code: int = 429, details: Optional[Dict] = None):
        super().__init__(message, code, details)

class NetworkError(BrowserError):
    """Exception untuk kesalahan jaringan."""
    def __init__(self, message: str, code: int = 502, details: Optional[Dict] = None):
        super().__init__(message, code, details)

class TabError(BrowserError):
    """Exception untuk kesalahan tab."""
    def __init__(self, message: str, code: int = 404, details: Optional[Dict] = None):
        super().__init__(message, code, details)

class ExtensionError(BrowserError):
    """Exception untuk kesalahan ekstensi."""
    def __init__(self, message: str, code: int = 400, details: Optional[Dict] = None):
        super().__init__(message, code, details)

# =============================================================================
# LOGGING CONFIGURATION
# =============================================================================

class BrowserLogger:
    """Logger khusus untuk modul browser."""

    def __init__(self, name: str = "MusGoBrowser"):
        self.name = name
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(name)
        self.logger.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

        # Handler untuk file log
        log_file = LOGS_PATH / f"{name}_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.log"
        file_handler = logging.FileHandler(log_file)
        file_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

        # Handler untuk console
        console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
        console_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)

        # Format log
        formatter = logging.Formatter(
            "%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(funcName)s:%(lineno)d | %(message)s"
        )
        file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        console_handler.setFormatter(formatter)

        self.logger.addHandler(file_handler)
        self.logger.addHandler(console_handler)

    def debug(self, message: str, **kwargs):
        self.logger.debug(message, extra={"data": kwargs})

    def info(self, message: str, **kwargs):
        self.logger.info(message, extra={"data": kwargs})

    def warning(self, message: str, **kwargs):
        self.logger.warning(message, extra={"data": kwargs})

    def error(self, message: str, **kwargs):
        self.logger.error(message, extra={"data": kwargs})

    def critical(self, message: str, **kwargs):
        self.logger.critical(message, extra={"data": kwargs})

    def exception(self, message: str, exc: Exception, **kwargs):
        self.logger.exception(message, extra={"data": kwargs, "exception": exc})

# Inisialisasi logger global
logger = BrowserLogger()

# =============================================================================
# UTILITY FUNCTIONS
# =============================================================================

class BrowserUtils:
    """Koleksi fungsi utility untuk browser."""

    @staticmethod
    def generate_id(prefix: str = "id") -> str:
        """Generate unique ID."""
        return f"{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex}"

    @staticmethod
    def sanitize_url(url: str) -> str:
        """Sanitize URL untuk mencegah injection."""
        if not url:
            raise ValidationError("URL cannot be empty")

        parsed = urlparse(url)
        if not parsed.scheme or not parsed.netloc:
            # Jika URL relatif, tambahkan scheme default
            if url.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
                return url
            elif url.startswith(("//")):
                return f"https:{url}"
            else:
                return f"https://{url}"

        # Validasi scheme
        allowed_schemes = ["http", "https", "data", "file", "about", "blob"]
        if parsed.scheme not in allowed_schemes:
            raise SecurityError(f"Unsupported URL scheme: {parsed.scheme}")

        # Reconstruct URL untuk menghindari encoding issues
        return parsed._replace(
            scheme=parsed.scheme.lower(),
            netloc=parsed.netloc.lower(),
            path=parsed.path,
            params=parsed.params,
            query=parsed.query,
            fragment=parsed.fragment
        ).geturl()

    @staticmethod
    def validate_url(url: str) -> bool:
        """Validasi format URL."""
        try:
            result = urlparse(url)
            return all([result.scheme, result.netloc])
        except:
            return False

    @staticmethod
    def is_allowed_domain(url: str, allowed_domains: List[str]) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah domain diizinkan."""
        if not allowed_domains:
            return True

        parsed = urlparse(url)
        domain = parsed.netloc.lower()

        for allowed in allowed_domains:
            if allowed.startswith("*."):
                if domain.endswith(allowed[1:]):
                    return True
            elif domain == allowed.lower():
                return True

        return False

    @staticmethod
    def is_blocked_domain(url: str, blocked_domains: List[str]) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah domain diblokir."""
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        domain = parsed.netloc.lower()

        for blocked in blocked_domains:
            if blocked.startswith("*."):
                if domain.endswith(blocked[1:]):
                    return True
            elif domain == blocked.lower():
                return True

        return False

    @staticmethod
    def generate_csp_header(policy: SecurityPolicy) -> str:
        """Generate Content-Security-Policy header."""
        directives = []
        for directive, values in policy.csp_directives.items():
            if values:
                directives.append(f"{directive} {' '.join(values)}")
        return "; ".join(directives)

    @staticmethod
    def hash_data(data: str, algorithm: str = "sha256") -> str:
        """Hash data menggunakan algoritma yang ditentukan."""
        if algorithm == "sha256":
            return hashlib.sha256(data.encode()).hexdigest()
        elif algorithm == "md5":
            return hashlib.md5(data.encode()).hexdigest()
        elif algorithm == "sha1":
            return hashlib.sha1(data.encode()).hexdigest()
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported hashing algorithm: {algorithm}")

    @staticmethod
    def encrypt_data(data: str, key: str) -> str:
        """Enkripsi data menggunakan Fernet."""
        if not key:
            raise ValueError("Encryption key cannot be empty")

        # Derive key dari string
        kdf = PBKDF2HMAC(
            algorithm=hashes.SHA256(),
            length=32,
            salt=b'salt-for-musgo-browser',  # Dalam produksi, gunakan salt random
            iterations=100000,
            backend=default_backend()
        )
        derived_key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(kdf.derive(key.encode()))
        fernet = Fernet(derived_key)
        return fernet.encrypt(data.encode()).decode()

    @staticmethod
    def decrypt_data(encrypted_data: str, key: str) -> str:
        """Dekripsi data menggunakan Fernet."""
        if not key:
            raise ValueError("Decryption key cannot be empty")

        kdf = PBKDF2HMAC(
            algorithm=hashes.SHA256(),
            length=32,
            salt=b'salt-for-musgo-browser',
            iterations=100000,
            backend=default_backend()
        )
        derived_key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(kdf.derive(key.encode()))
        fernet = Fernet(derived_key)
        return fernet.decrypt(encrypted_data.encode()).decode()

    @staticmethod
    def compress_data(data: str) -> bytes:
        """Kompres data menggunakan zlib."""
        return zlib.compress(data.encode())

    @staticmethod
    def decompress_data(compressed_data: bytes) -> str:
        """Dekompres data menggunakan zlib."""
        return zlib.decompress(compressed_data).decode()

    @staticmethod
    def get_system_info() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Dapatkan informasi sistem."""
        return {
            "os": platform.system(),
            "os_version": platform.version(),
            "architecture": platform.architecture(),
            "processor": platform.processor(),
            "hostname": platform.node(),
            "cpu_cores": psutil.cpu_count(logical=False),
            "cpu_logical_cores": psutil.cpu_count(logical=True),
            "memory_total_gb": round(psutil.virtual_memory().total / (1024 ** 3), 2),
            "memory_available_gb": round(psutil.virtual_memory().available / (1024 ** 3), 2),
            "disk_usage": psutil.disk_usage('/')._asdict(),
            "python_version": sys.version,
            "python_executable": sys.executable,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def check_resource_usage() -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Cek penggunaan resource sistem."""
        cpu_usage = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=1)
        memory_usage = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
        disk_usage = psutil.disk_usage('/').percent
        return {
            "cpu_usage_percent": cpu_usage,
            "memory_usage_percent": memory_usage,
            "disk_usage_percent": disk_usage,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def enforce_resource_limits(max_cpu: float = 80.0, max_memory: float = 80.0, max_disk: float = 90.0) -> bool:
        """Enforce batasan resource."""
        usage = BrowserUtils.check_resource_usage()
        if usage["cpu_usage_percent"] > max_cpu:
            logger.warning(f"CPU usage exceeded limit: {usage['cpu_usage_percent']}% > {max_cpu}%")
            return False
        if usage["memory_usage_percent"] > max_memory:
            logger.warning(f"Memory usage exceeded limit: {usage['memory_usage_percent']}% > {max_memory}%")
            return False
        if usage["disk_usage_percent"] > max_disk:
            logger.warning(f"Disk usage exceeded limit: {usage['disk_usage_percent']}% > {max_disk}%")
            return False
        return True

# =============================================================================
# SINGLETON MANAGER
# =============================================================================

class SingletonMeta(type):
    """Metaclass untuk implementasi Singleton pattern."""
    _instances = {}

    def __call__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if cls not in cls._instances:
            cls._instances[cls] = super().__call__(*args, **kwargs)
        return cls._instances[cls]

# =============================================================================
# CONFIGURATION MANAGER
# =============================================================================

class ConfigManager(metaclass=SingletonMeta):
    """Manager untuk konfigurasi browser."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.settings: BrowserSettings = self._load_settings()
        self.security_policy: SecurityPolicy = self._load_security_policy()
        self.ai_config: AIConfig = self._load_ai_config()
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._observers = weakref.WeakSet()

    def _load_settings(self) -> BrowserSettings:
        """Load settings dari file atau gunakan default."""
        try:
            if DEFAULT_SETTINGS_PATH.exists():
                with open(DEFAULT_SETTINGS_PATH, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    return BrowserSettings(**data)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load settings: {e}")
        return BrowserSettings()

    def _load_security_policy(self) -> SecurityPolicy:
        """Load security policy dari file atau gunakan default."""
        try:
            if SECURITY_POLICIES_PATH.exists():
                with open(SECURITY_POLICIES_PATH, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    return SecurityPolicy(**data)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load security policy: {e}")
        return SecurityPolicy()

    def _load_ai_config(self) -> AIConfig:
        """Load AI configuration dari file atau gunakan default."""
        try:
            ai_config_path = CONFIG_PATH / "ai_config.json"
            if ai_config_path.exists():
                with open(ai_config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    return AIConfig(**data)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load AI config: {e}")
        return AIConfig()

    def save_settings(self) -> None:
        """Simpan settings ke file."""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                DEFAULT_SETTINGS_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                with open(DEFAULT_SETTINGS_PATH, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(asdict(self.settings), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to save settings: {e}")
                raise

    def save_security_policy(self) -> None:
        """Simpan security policy ke file."""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                SECURITY_POLICIES_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                with open(SECURITY_POLICIES_PATH, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(asdict(self.security_policy), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to save security policy: {e}")
                raise

    def update_settings(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update settings dengan nilai baru."""
        with self._lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self.settings, key):
                    setattr(self.settings, key, value)
            self.save_settings()
            self._notify_observers("settings_updated", kwargs)

    def update_security_policy(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update security policy dengan nilai baru."""
        with self._lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self.security_policy, key):
                    setattr(self.security_policy, key, value)
            self.save_security_policy()
            self._notify_observers("security_policy_updated", kwargs)

    def add_observer(self, observer) -> None:
        """Tambahkan observer untuk perubahan konfigurasi."""
        self._observers.add(observer)

    def remove_observer(self, observer) -> None:
        """Hapus observer."""
        self._observers.discard(observer)

    def _notify_observers(self, event: str, data: Dict) -> None:
        """Beritahu semua observer tentang perubahan."""
        for observer in self._observers:
            if hasattr(observer, "on_config_change"):
                try:
                    observer.on_config_change(event, data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Observer error: {e}")

# =============================================================================
# EVENT BROKER (Internal)
# =============================================================================

class EventBroker(metaclass=SingletonMeta):
    """Internal event broker untuk komunikasi antar komponen browser."""

    def __init__(self):
        self._subscribers: Dict[str, List[Callable]] = defaultdict(list)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._event_queue = deque(maxlen=1000)
        self._running = False
        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None

    def start(self) -> None:
        """Start event broker thread."""
        if not self._running:
            self._running = True
            self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._process_events, daemon=True)
            self._thread.start()
            logger.info("EventBroker started")

    def stop(self) -> None:
        """Stop event broker thread."""
        self._running = False
        if self._thread:
            self._thread.join(timeout=5)
            self._thread = None
        logger.info("EventBroker stopped")

    def subscribe(self, event_type: str, callback: Callable) -> None:
        """Subscribe ke event tertentu."""
        with self._lock:
            self._subscribers[event_type].append(callback)
            logger.debug(f"Subscribed to event: {event_type}")

    def unsubscribe(self, event_type: str, callback: Callable) -> None:
        """Unsubscribe dari event tertentu."""
        with self._lock:
            if event_type in self._subscribers:
                if callback in self._subscribers[event_type]:
                    self._subscribers[event_type].remove(callback)
                    logger.debug(f"Unsubscribed from event: {event_type}")

    def publish(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Publish event ke semua subscriber."""
        with self._lock:
            self._event_queue.append(event)
            logger.debug(f"Published event: {event.event_type}")

    def _process_events(self) -> None:
        """Proses event di background thread."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                if self._event_queue:
                    event = self._event_queue.popleft()
                    self._deliver_event(event)
                else:
                    time.sleep(0.1)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error processing event: {e}")

    def _deliver_event(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Deliver event ke semua subscriber."""
        with self._lock:
            if event.event_type in self._subscribers:
                for callback in self._subscribers[event.event_type]:
                    try:
                        callback(event)
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"Error in event callback: {e}")

# =============================================================================
# VALIDATION LAYER
# =============================================================================

class Validator:
    """Kelas untuk validasi multi-layer."""

    @staticmethod
    def validate_tab_config(config: TabConfig) -> None:
        """Validasi konfigurasi tab."""
        if not config.tab_id:
            raise ValidationError("Tab ID cannot be empty")
        if not Validator.validate_url(config.url):
            raise ValidationError(f"Invalid URL: {config.url}")
        if config.zoom_level < 0.1 or config.zoom_level > 5.0:
            raise ValidationError("Zoom level must be between 0.1 and 5.0")
        if config.state not in TabState:
            raise ValidationError(f"Invalid tab state: {config.state}")

    @staticmethod
    def validate_browser_settings(settings: BrowserSettings) -> None:
        """Validasi settings browser."""
        if settings.max_tabs < 1 or settings.max_tabs > 200:
            raise ValidationError("max_tabs must be between 1 and 200")
        if settings.max_windows < 1 or settings.max_windows > 20:
            raise ValidationError("max_windows must be between 1 and 20")
        if settings.cache_size_mb < 10 or settings.cache_size_mb > 5000:
            raise ValidationError("cache_size_mb must be between 10 and 5000")
        if settings.session_timeout_minutes < 1 or settings.session_timeout_minutes > 1440:
            raise ValidationError("session_timeout_minutes must be between 1 and 1440")

    @staticmethod
    def validate_security_policy(policy: SecurityPolicy) -> None:
        """Validasi kebijakan keamanan."""
        if policy.max_script_length < 100 or policy.max_script_length > 10000000:
            raise ValidationError("max_script_length must be between 100 and 10MB")
        if policy.max_style_length < 100 or policy.max_style_length > 5000000:
            raise ValidationError("max_style_length must be between 100 and 5MB")
        if policy.max_image_size < 100 or policy.max_image_size > 50000000:
            raise ValidationError("max_image_size must be between 100 and 50MB")

    @staticmethod
    def validate_extension_manifest(manifest: ExtensionManifest) -> None:
        """Validasi manifest ekstensi."""
        if not manifest.name:
            raise ValidationError("Extension name cannot be empty")
        if not manifest.version:
            raise ValidationError("Extension version cannot be empty")
        if manifest.manifest_version not in [2, 3]:
            raise ValidationError("manifest_version must be 2 or 3")

    @staticmethod
    def validate_permissions(permissions: Set[Permission], required_permissions: Set[Permission]) -> None:
        """Validasi izin yang diminta."""
        if not required_permissions.issubset(permissions):
            missing = required_permissions - permissions
            raise ValidationError(f"Missing permissions: {missing}")

    @staticmethod
    def validate_input_length(input_str: str, max_length: int, field_name: str) -> None:
        """Validasi panjang input."""
        if len(input_str) > max_length:
            raise ValidationError(f"{field_name} exceeds maximum length of {max_length}")

    @staticmethod
    def validate_positive_number(value: Union[int, float], field_name: str) -> None:
        """Validasi angka positif."""
        if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value <= 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"{field_name} must be a positive number")

    @staticmethod
    def validate_path(path: str, field_name: str) -> None:
        """Validasi path file/direktori."""
        if not path:
            raise ValidationError(f"{field_name} cannot be empty")
        if ".." in path or path.startswith("/") or path.startswith("\\"):
            raise ValidationError(f"Invalid {field_name}: path traversal detected")

    @staticmethod
    def validate_regex(input_str: str, pattern: str, field_name: str) -> None:
        """Validasi input dengan regex."""
        if not re.match(pattern, input_str):
            raise ValidationError(f"Invalid {field_name}: does not match pattern {pattern}")

# =============================================================================
# INTEGRITY GUARD
# =============================================================================

class IntegrityGuard(metaclass=SingletonMeta):
    """Guard untuk memastikan integritas data dan state."""

    def __init__(self):
        self._checksums: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._initialized = False
        self._initialize()

    def _initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi checksum untuk file-file kritis."""
        if self._initialized:
            return

        critical_files = [
            DEFAULT_SETTINGS_PATH,
            SECURITY_POLICIES_PATH,
            ROOT_PATH / "__init__.py",
        ]

        for file_path in critical_files:
            if file_path.exists():
                self._checksums[str(file_path)] = self._calculate_checksum(file_path)

        self._initialized = True
        logger.info("IntegrityGuard initialized")

    def _calculate_checksum(self, file_path: Path) -> str:
        """Hitung checksum SHA-256 untuk file."""
        hash_sha256 = hashlib.sha256()
        with open(file_path, "rb") as f:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(4096), b""):
                hash_sha256.update(chunk)
        return hash_sha256.hexdigest()

    def verify_checksum(self, file_path: Path) -> bool:
        """Verifikasi checksum file."""
        with self._lock:
            str_path = str(file_path)
            if str_path not in self._checksums:
                return False

            current_checksum = self._calculate_checksum(file_path)
            return current_checksum == self._checksums[str_path]

    def verify_all(self) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """Verifikasi semua file kritis."""
        results = {}
        with self._lock:
            for file_path, checksum in self._checksums.items():
                path_obj = Path(file_path)
                if path_obj.exists():
                    current_checksum = self._calculate_checksum(path_obj)
                    results[file_path] = current_checksum == checksum
                else:
                    results[file_path] = False
        return results

    def update_checksum(self, file_path: Path) -> None:
        """Update checksum untuk file."""
        with self._lock:
            str_path = str(file_path)
            if file_path.exists():
                self._checksums[str_path] = self._calculate_checksum(file_path)
                logger.info(f"Updated checksum for {file_path}")
            else:
                logger.warning(f"File not found: {file_path}")

    def add_protected_file(self, file_path: Path) -> None:
        """Tambahkan file ke daftar protected."""
        with self._lock:
            if file_path.exists():
                self._checksums[str(file_path)] = self._calculate_checksum(file_path)
                logger.info(f"Added protected file: {file_path}")

    def remove_protected_file(self, file_path: Path) -> None:
        """Hapus file dari daftar protected."""
        with self._lock:
            str_path = str(file_path)
            if str_path in self._checksums:
                del self._checksums[str_path]
                logger.info(f"Removed protected file: {file_path}")

    def check_state_consistency(self, expected_state: Dict[str, Any], actual_state: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Cek konsistensi state."""
        if set(expected_state.keys()) != set(actual_state.keys()):
            logger.warning("State keys mismatch")
            return False

        for key, expected_value in expected_state.items():
            actual_value = actual_state[key]
            if expected_value != actual_value:
                logger.warning(f"State mismatch for {key}: expected {expected_value}, got {actual_value}")
                return False

        return True

    def prevent_replay(self, nonce: str, timestamp: datetime, ttl_seconds: int = 300) -> bool:
        """Cegah replay attack dengan nonce dan timestamp."""
        if (datetime.now() - timestamp).total_seconds() > ttl_seconds:
            logger.warning("Replay attack detected: expired timestamp")
            return False
        return True

### **Rencana Output Blueprint:**
1. **Setiap part (PAR)** akan berisi **500-1000 baris** (atau lebih jika diperlukan untuk kelengkapan).
2. **Semua konten blueprint** (termasuk struktur folder, code, logic, dan detail) **akan berada di dalam block text** (```).
3. **Subfolder** dalam `browser` **boleh ditambahkan** jika diperlukan untuk **kelengkapan enterprise-grade** (asalkan **tidak mengurangi** struktur yang sudah ada).
4. **Setiap PAR** akan diakhiri dengan `--- END OF PAR [X] ---` untuk memudahkan konfirmasi.
5. **Semua detail** (security, validation, event broker, transaction control, dll.) **akan dimasukkan** ke dalam blueprint.

"""
Rendering Engine Implementation
=================================
Implementasi rendering menggunakan CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) atau WebKit.
"""

import os
import sys
import ctypes
import threading
import weakref
from typing import Optional, Dict, List, Tuple, Any, Callable
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
import logging
import subprocess
import tempfile
import shutil
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    TabConfig,
    TabState,
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    BrowserError,
    ResourceError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.config.settings_loader import SettingsLoader

# --- ENUM ---
class RendererType(Enum):
    """Tipe renderer yang didukung."""
    CEF = auto()
    WEBKIT = auto()
    HEADLESS = auto()

class RenderingMode(Enum):
    """Mode rendering."""
    STANDARD = auto()
    ACCELERATED = auto()  # Hardware-accelerated
    SOFTWARE = auto()    # Software-only

class RenderingError(Enum):
    """Jenis error rendering."""
    INITIALIZATION_FAILED = "Initialization failed"
    CONTEXT_LOST = "Rendering context lost"
    RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED = "Resource exhausted"
    UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE = "Unsupported feature"
    CRASHED = "Renderer crashed"

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class RenderingContext:
    """Konteks untuk rendering sebuah tab."""
    tab_id: str
    url: str
    width: int = 1280
    height: int = 720
    device_scale_factor: float = 1.0
    is_visible: bool = True
    is_focused: bool = False
    background_color: Tuple[int, int, int, int] = (255, 255, 255, 255)  # RGBA
    user_agent: Optional[str] = None
    headers: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    proxy: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    sandbox_enabled: bool = True
    gpu_acceleration: bool = True

@dataclass
class RenderingStats:
    """Statistik performa rendering."""
    fps: float = 0.0
    frame_time_ms: float = 0.0
    memory_usage_mb: float = 0.0
    gpu_memory_usage_mb: float = 0.0
    cpu_usage_percent: float = 0.0
    dropped_frames: int = 0
    last_frame_time: float = 0.0

# --- ABSTRACT BASE CLASS ---
class BaseRenderer(ABC):
    """Abstract base class untuk renderer."""

    def __init__(self, renderer_type: RendererType):
        self.renderer_type = renderer_type
        self._initialized = False
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._contexts: Dict[str, RenderingContext] = {}
        self._stats: Dict[str, RenderingStats] = {}

    @abstractmethod
    def initialize(self, settings: BrowserSettings) -> bool:
        """Inisialisasi renderer."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def create_context(self, tab_id: str, config: TabConfig) -> bool:
        """Buat konteks rendering untuk tab."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def destroy_context(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hancurkan konteks rendering."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def render(self, tab_id: str, url: str) -> bool:
        """Render URL ke tab."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def resize(self, tab_id: str, width: int, height: int) -> bool:
        """Ubah ukuran konteks rendering."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def execute_javascript(self, tab_id: str, script: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Eksekusi JavaScript di tab."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def capture_screenshot(self, tab_id: str, path: str) -> bool:
        """Ambili screenshot tab."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def get_stats(self, tab_id: str) -> RenderingStats:
        """Dapatkan statistik performa."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def handle_input_event(self, tab_id: str, event: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Handle input event (mouse, keyboard)."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        pass

    def is_initialized(self) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah renderer sudah diinisialisasi."""
        return self._initialized

    def get_context(self, tab_id: str) -> Optional[RenderingContext]:
        """Dapatkan konteks rendering untuk tab."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._contexts.get(tab_id)

    def update_context(self, tab_id: str, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update konteks rendering."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id in self._contexts:
                for key, value in kwargs.items():
                    if hasattr(self._contexts[tab_id], key):
                        setattr(self._contexts[tab_id], key, value)

    def log_stats(self, tab_id: str) -> None:
        """Log statistik performa."""
        stats = self.get_stats(tab_id)
        logger.info(
            f"RenderingStats | Tab: {tab_id} | "
            f"FPS: {stats.fps:.2f} | "
            f"FrameTime: {stats.frame_time_ms:.2f}ms | "
            f"Memory: {stats.memory_usage_mb:.2f}MB | "
            f"GPU: {stats.gpu_memory_usage_mb:.2f}MB | "
            f"CPU: {stats.cpu_usage_percent:.2f}%"
        )

# --- CEF RENDERER (Chromium Embedded Framework) ---
class CEFRenderer(BaseRenderer):
    """Renderer menggunakan Chromium Embedded Framework (CEF)."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(RendererType.CEF)
        self._cef_initialized = False
        self._cef_settings = None
        self._cef_app = None
        self._cef_client = None
        self._cef_browser_hosts: Dict[str, Any] = {}  # tab_id -> browser host
        self._temp_dir = None
        self._subprocess_args = None

    def initialize(self, settings: BrowserSettings) -> bool:
        """Inisialisasi CEF."""
        with self._lock:
            if self._cef_initialized:
                return True

            try:
                # Buat temporary directory untuk CEF
                self._temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="musgo_cef_")
                os.environ["CEF_TEMP_DIR"] = self._temp_dir

                # Set subprocess arguments
                self._subprocess_args = {
                    "no-sandbox": not settings.enable_sandboxing,
                    "disable-gpu": not settings.enable_gpu_acceleration,
                    "disable-software-rasterizer": True,
                    "disable-dev-shm-usage": True,
                    "disable-setuid-sandbox": True,
                    "disable-web-security": False,
                    "allow-running-insecure-content": False,
                    "enable-begin-frame-scheduling": True,
                }

                # Inisialisasi CEF (contoh: menggunakan cefpython3)
                # Note: Ini adalah blueprint, implementasi sebenarnya memerlukan cefpython3
                import cefpython3
                self._cef_settings = {
                    "browser_subprocess_path": str(Path(__file__).parent / "cef_subprocess"),
                    "cache_path": str(CACHE_PATH / "cef"),
                    "log_file": str(LOGS_PATH / "cef.log"),
                    "log_severity": cefpython3.LOGSEVERITY_INFO,
                    "release_dcheck_enabled": True,
                    "multi_threaded_message_loop": True,
                    "externally_managed_environment": True,
                }

                # Set user agent
                if settings.user_agent:
                    cefpython3.g_instance.SetUserAgent(settings.user_agent)

                # Inisialisasi CEF
                cefpython3.Initialize(self._cef_settings)
                self._cef_initialized = True
                self._initialized = True
                logger.info("CEF renderer initialized successfully")
                return True

            except ImportError:
                logger.error("CEF (cefpython3) not installed. Install with: pip install cefpython3")
                return False
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to initialize CEF: {e}")
                self.cleanup()
                return False

    def create_context(self, tab_id: str, config: TabConfig) -> bool:
        """Buat konteks rendering untuk tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if not self._cef_initialized:
                raise ResourceError("CEF not initialized")

            if tab_id in self._contexts:
                logger.warning(f"Context for tab {tab_id} already exists")
                return False

            try:
                # Simpan konteks
                context = RenderingContext(
                    tab_id=tab_id,
                    url=config.url,
                    width=1280,
                    height=720,
                    device_scale_factor=1.0,
                    is_visible=True,
                    user_agent=config.user_agent or self._cef_settings.get("user_agent"),
                    headers=config.custom_headers,
                    proxy=config.proxy_settings,
                    sandbox_enabled=config.security_level.value >= 2,  # MEDIUM atau HIGH
                    gpu_acceleration=True,
                )
                self._contexts[tab_id] = context

                # Buat browser host (contoh: menggunakan cefpython3)
                # Note: Ini blueprint, implementasi sebenarnya memerlukan cefpython3
                window_info = cefpython3.WindowInfo()
                window_info.SetAsWindowless(None)  # Off-screen rendering

                client = cefpython3.Client()
                browser_host = cefpython3.CreateBrowserSync(
                    window_info,
                    client,
                    url=config.url,
                    extra_info={
                        "user_agent": context.user_agent,
                        "headers": context.headers,
                    }
                )

                self._cef_browser_hosts[tab_id] = browser_host
                logger.info(f"Created CEF context for tab: {tab_id}")
                return True

            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to create CEF context for tab {tab_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def destroy_context(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hancurkan konteks rendering."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._cef_browser_hosts:
                return False

            try:
                browser_host = self._cef_browser_hosts[tab_id]
                browser_host.CloseBrowser(True)  # Force close
                del self._cef_browser_hosts[tab_id]
                if tab_id in self._contexts:
                    del self._contexts[tab_id]
                logger.info(f"Destroyed CEF context for tab: {tab_id}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to destroy CEF context for tab {tab_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def render(self, tab_id: str, url: str) -> bool:
        """Render URL ke tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._cef_browser_hosts:
                return False

            try:
                browser_host = self._cef_browser_hosts[tab_id]
                browser_host.GetMainFrame().LoadURL(url)
                if tab_id in self._contexts:
                    self._contexts[tab_id].url = url
                logger.info(f"Rendered URL {url} for tab: {tab_id}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to render URL {url} for tab {tab_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def resize(self, tab_id: str, width: int, height: int) -> bool:
        """Ubah ukuran konteks rendering."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._contexts:
                return False

            try:
                self._contexts[tab_id].width = width
                self._contexts[tab_id].height = height
                # Update browser host size (contoh: untuk cefpython3)
                if tab_id in self._cef_browser_hosts:
                    browser_host = self._cef_browser_hosts[tab_id]
                    browser_host.Resize(width, height)
                logger.info(f"Resized tab {tab_id} to {width}x{height}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to resize tab {tab_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def execute_javascript(self, tab_id: str, script: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Eksekusi JavaScript di tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._cef_browser_hosts:
                return None

            try:
                browser_host = self._cef_browser_hosts[tab_id]
                frame = browser_host.GetMainFrame()
                result = frame.ExecuteJavaScript(script)
                logger.info(f"Executed JavaScript in tab {tab_id}")
                return result
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to execute JavaScript in tab {tab_id}: {e}")
                return None

    def capture_screenshot(self, tab_id: str, path: str) -> bool:
        """Ambili screenshot tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._cef_browser_hosts:
                return False

            try:
                browser_host = self._cef_browser_hosts[tab_id]
                # Contoh: Simpan screenshot ke path (implementasi tergantung library)
                # Ini adalah blueprint, implementasi sebenarnya memerlukan image handling
                logger.info(f"Captured screenshot for tab {tab_id} to {path}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to capture screenshot for tab {tab_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def get_stats(self, tab_id: str) -> RenderingStats:
        """Dapatkan statistik performa."""
        # Contoh: Ambil stats dari CEF (blueprint)
        return RenderingStats(
            fps=60.0,
            frame_time_ms=16.67,
            memory_usage_mb=256.0,
            gpu_memory_usage_mb=128.0,
            cpu_usage_percent=45.0,
            dropped_frames=0,
        )

    def handle_input_event(self, tab_id: str, event: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Handle input event (mouse, keyboard)."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._cef_browser_hosts:
                return False

            try:
                browser_host = self._cef_browser_hosts[tab_id]
                # Contoh: Kirim event ke CEF
                # event_type = event.get("type")  # "mouse", "keyboard"
                # if event_type == "mouse":
                #     browser_host.SendMouseEvent(...)
                logger.info(f"Handled input event for tab {tab_id}: {event}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to handle input event for tab {tab_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                # Tutup semua browser host
                for tab_id, browser_host in self._cef_browser_hosts.items():
                    try:
                        browser_host.CloseBrowser(True)
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"Error closing browser host {tab_id}: {e}")

                self._cef_browser_hosts.clear()
                self._contexts.clear()

                # Bersihkan CEF
                if self._cef_initialized:
                    import cefpython3
                    cefpython3.Shutdown()
                    self._cef_initialized = False

                # Hapus temporary directory
                if self._temp_dir and os.path.exists(self._temp_dir):
                    shutil.rmtree(self._temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

                self._initialized = False
                logger.info("CEF renderer cleaned up")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error during CEF cleanup: {e}")
