```text
"""
Networking Module
=================
Modul untuk handling HTTP/HTTPS requests, proxy, caching, dan request interception.
"""

import os
import re
import ssl
import socket
import urllib
import hashlib
import mimetypes
from io import BytesIO
from typing import Optional, Dict, List, Tuple, Any, Callable, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import ipaddress
import dns.resolver
from urllib.parse import urlparse, urljoin, urlencode, parse_qs, quote, unquote
import requests
from requests.adapters import HTTPAdapter
from requests.packages.urllib3.util.retry import Retry
from requests.cookies import RequestsCookieJar

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    Permission,
    BrowserError,
    NetworkError,
    SecurityError,
    ValidationError,
    ResourceError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.config.settings_loader import SettingsLoader

# --- ENUM ---
class HttpMethod(Enum):
    """HTTP methods yang didukung."""
    GET = "GET"
    POST = "POST"
    PUT = "PUT"
    DELETE = "DELETE"
    HEAD = "HEAD"
    OPTIONS = "OPTIONS"
    PATCH = "PATCH"

class RequestStatus(Enum):
    """Status request."""
    PENDING = auto()
    PROCESSING = auto()
    COMPLETED = auto()
    FAILED = auto()
    CANCELLED = auto()
    TIMEOUT = auto()

class CacheControl(Enum):
    """Cache control directives."""
    NO_STORE = "no-store"
    NO_CACHE = "no-cache"
    PRIVATE = "private"
    PUBLIC = "public"
    MAX_AGE = "max-age"
    MUST_REVALIDATE = "must-revalidate"

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class Request:
    """Struktur data untuk HTTP request."""
    request_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f"req_{int(time.time() * 1000)}")
    url: str = ""
    method: HttpMethod = HttpMethod.GET
    headers: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    body: Optional[Union[str, bytes]] = None
    params: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    cookies: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    timeout: int = 30
    allow_redirects: bool = True
    max_redirects: int = 5
    proxy: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None
    verify_ssl: bool = True
    stream: bool = False
    status: RequestStatus = RequestStatus.PENDING
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    retries: int = 0
    max_retries: int = 3

@dataclass
class Response:
    """Struktur data untuk HTTP response."""
    request_id: str = ""
    status_code: int = 200
    headers: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    body: Optional[Union[str, bytes]] = None
    url: str = ""
    elapsed: float = 0.0
    encoding: Optional[str] = None
    is_from_cache: bool = False
    cache_key: Optional[str] = None
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

@dataclass
class CacheEntry:
    """Entry untuk cache."""
    key: str
    response: Response
    expires_at: datetime
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    hit_count: int = 0
    size_bytes: int = 0

@dataclass
class ProxyConfig:
    """Konfigurasi proxy."""
    http: Optional[str] = None
    https: Optional[str] = None
    ftp: Optional[str] = None
    socks: Optional[str] = None
    no_proxy: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    bypass_local: bool = True

# --- NETWORK MANAGER ---
class NetworkManager:
    """Manager untuk handling HTTP/HTTPS requests."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings, security_policy: SecurityPolicy):
        self.settings = settings
        self.security_policy = security_policy
        self._session: Optional[requests.Session] = None
        self._cache: Dict[str, CacheEntry] = {}
        self._cache_lock = threading.RLock()
        self._request_lock = threading.RLock()
        self._active_requests: Dict[str, Request] = {}
        self._proxy_config: Optional[ProxyConfig] = None
        self._cookie_jar = RequestsCookieJar()
        self._user_agent = settings.user_agent or "MusGoBrowser/1.0"
        self._timeout = settings.timeout_seconds if hasattr(settings, "timeout_seconds") else 30
        self._max_retries = 3
        self._retry_strategy = Retry(
            total=self._max_retries,
            backoff_factor=1,
            status_forcelist=[408, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504],
            allowed_methods=["HEAD", "GET", "OPTIONS", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "PATCH"],
        )
        self._adapter = HTTPAdapter(max_retries=self._retry_strategy)
        self._init_session()
        self._init_proxy()
        self._init_ssl_context()

    def _init_session(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi session HTTP."""
        self._session = requests.Session()
        self._session.headers.update({
            "User-Agent": self._user_agent,
            "Accept": "text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8",
            "Accept-Language": self.settings.language or "en-US,en;q=0.5",
            "Accept-Encoding": "gzip, deflate, br",
            "Connection": "keep-alive",
        })
        self._session.cookies = self._cookie_jar
        self._session.mount("http://", self._adapter)
        self._session.mount("https://", self._adapter)

    def _init_proxy(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi konfigurasi proxy."""
        if self.settings.proxy_settings:
            self._proxy_config = ProxyConfig(**self.settings.proxy_settings)
            proxies = {}
            if self._proxy_config.http:
                proxies["http"] = self._proxy_config.http
            if self._proxy_config.https:
                proxies["https"] = self._proxy_config.https
            if proxies:
                self._session.proxies.update(proxies)

    def _init_ssl_context(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi SSL context."""
        if not self.security_policy.enable_hsts:
            self._session.verify = False
        else:
            self._session.verify = True
            # Tambahkan custom CA bundle jika diperlukan
            if hasattr(self.settings, "ca_bundle_path"):
                self._session.verify = self.settings.ca_bundle_path

    def _generate_cache_key(self, request: Request) -> str:
        """Generate cache key untuk request."""
        key_parts = [
            request.method.value,
            request.url,
            str(sorted(request.headers.items())),
            str(request.body) if request.body else "",
            str(sorted(request.params.items())),
        ]
        return hashlib.sha256("|".join(key_parts).encode()).hexdigest()

    def _is_cacheable(self, request: Request, response: Response) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah response bisa di-cache."""
        if request.method != HttpMethod.GET:
            return False
        if response.status_code != 200:
            return False
        if "Cache-Control" in response.headers:
            cache_control = response.headers["Cache-Control"].lower()
            if CacheControl.NO_STORE.value in cache_control:
                return False
        return True

    def _get_cache_ttl(self, response: Response) -> int:
        """Dapatkan TTL (Time-To-Live) untuk cache."""
        cache_control = response.headers.get("Cache-Control", "").lower()
        max_age = None
        for part in cache_control.split(","):
            if part.strip().startswith(CacheControl.MAX_AGE.value):
                try:
                    max_age = int(part.split("=")[1].strip())
                    break
                except (IndexError, ValueError):
                    pass
        if max_age is not None:
            return max_age
        return 3600  # Default: 1 jam

    def _is_expired(self, entry: CacheEntry) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah cache entry sudah expired."""
        return datetime.now() > entry.expires_at

    def _validate_url(self, url: str) -> bool:
        """Validasi URL berdasarkan security policy."""
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        if parsed.scheme not in self.security_policy.allowed_schemes:
            raise SecurityError(f"Unsupported scheme: {parsed.scheme}")
        if self.security_policy.blocked_schemes and parsed.scheme in self.security_policy.blocked_schemes:
            raise SecurityError(f"Blocked scheme: {parsed.scheme}")
        if parsed.netloc:
            if self._is_blocked_domain(url):
                raise SecurityError(f"Blocked domain: {parsed.netloc}")
        return True

    def _is_blocked_domain(self, url: str) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah domain diblokir."""
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        domain = parsed.netloc.lower()
        for blocked in self.security_policy.blocked_domains:
            if blocked.startswith("*."):
                if domain.endswith(blocked[1:]):
                    return True
            elif domain == blocked.lower():
                return True
        return False

    def _sanitize_headers(self, headers: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Sanitize headers untuk mencegah header injection."""
        sanitized = {}
        for key, value in headers.items():
            # Remove newlines dan karakter berbahaya
            sanitized_key = re.sub(r"[^\w\-]", "", key).title()
            sanitized_value = re.sub(r"[\r\n]", "", value)
            sanitized[sanitized_key] = sanitized_value
        return sanitized

    def _intercept_request(self, request: Request) -> Optional[Request]:
        """Intercept request sebelum dikirim (untuk security, ad-blocking, dll.)."""
        # Cek apakah URL diizinkan
        self._validate_url(request.url)

        # Cek apakah request memerlukan izin khusus
        if request.url.startswith("file://"):
            if Permission.READ_FILESYSTEM not in self.settings.default_permissions:
                raise PermissionError("File system access denied")

        # Tambahkan header keamanan
        request.headers.update({
            "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
            "X-Frame-Options": self.security_policy.frame_options,
            "X-XSS-Protection": "1; mode=block" if self.security_policy.enable_xss_protection else "0",
            "Referrer-Policy": self.security_policy.referrer_policy,
        })

        # Sanitize headers
        request.headers = self._sanitize_headers(request.headers)

        return request

    def _intercept_response(self, response: Response) -> Response:
        """Intercept response sebelum dikembalikan (untuk security, caching, dll.)."""
        # Tambahkan header keamanan ke response
        response.headers.update({
            "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
            "X-Frame-Options": self.security_policy.frame_options,
            "X-XSS-Protection": "1; mode=block" if self.security_policy.enable_xss_protection else "0",
        })

        # Cek apakah response bisa di-cache
        if self._is_cacheable(self._active_requests.get(response.request_id), response):
            self._cache_response(response)

        return response

    def _cache_response(self, response: Response) -> None:
        """Simpan response ke cache."""
        with self._cache_lock:
            cache_key = self._generate_cache_key(self._active_requests.get(response.request_id))
            ttl = self._get_cache_ttl(response)
            expires_at = datetime.now() + timedelta(seconds=ttl)
            entry = CacheEntry(
                key=cache_key,
                response=response,
                expires_at=expires_at,
                size_bytes=len(response.body) if response.body else 0,
            )
            self._cache[cache_key] = entry
            logger.debug(f"Cached response for key: {cache_key}")

    def _get_cached_response(self, request: Request) -> Optional[Response]:
        """Dapatkan response dari cache."""
        with self._cache_lock:
            cache_key = self._generate_cache_key(request)
            if cache_key in self._cache:
                entry = self._cache[cache_key]
                if not self._is_expired(entry):
                    entry.hit_count += 1
                    response = entry.response
                    response.is_from_cache = True
                    response.cache_key = cache_key
                    logger.debug(f"Cache hit for key: {cache_key}")
                    return response
                else:
                    # Hapus cache yang expired
                    del self._cache[cache_key]
        return None

    def _cleanup_cache(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan cache yang expired."""
        with self._cache_lock:
            expired_keys = [
                key for key, entry in self._cache.items()
                if self._is_expired(entry)
            ]
            for key in expired_keys:
                del self._cache[key]
            logger.info(f"Cleaned up {len(expired_keys)} expired cache entries")

    def send_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        """Kirim HTTP request."""
        with self._request_lock:
            # Intercept request
            request = self._intercept_request(request)
            self._active_requests[request.request_id] = request

            # Cek cache
            cached_response = self._get_cached_response(request)
            if cached_response:
                return cached_response

            # Kirim request
            try:
                request.started_at = datetime.now()
                method = request.method.value
                url = request.url
                headers = request.headers
                data = request.body
                params = request.params
                cookies = request.cookies
                timeout = request.timeout
                allow_redirects = request.allow_redirects
                proxy = request.proxy

                # Set proxy jika ada
                proxies = {}
                if proxy:
                    if "http" in proxy:
                        proxies["http"] = proxy["http"]
                    if "https" in proxy:
                        proxies["https"] = proxy["https"]

                # Kirim request
                start_time = time.time()
                if method == HttpMethod.GET.value:
                    response = self._session.get(
                        url,
                        headers=headers,
                        params=params,
                        cookies=cookies,
                        timeout=timeout,
                        allow_redirects=allow_redirects,
                        proxies=proxies or None,
                        stream=request.stream,
                    )
                elif method == HttpMethod.POST.value:
                    response = self._session.post(
                        url,
                        headers=headers,
                        data=data,
                        params=params,
                        cookies=cookies,
                        timeout=timeout,
                        allow_redirects=allow_redirects,
                        proxies=proxies or None,
                        stream=request.stream,
                    )
                elif method == HttpMethod.PUT.value:
                    response = self._session.put(
                        url,
                        headers=headers,
                        data=data,
                        params=params,
                        cookies=cookies,
                        timeout=timeout,
                        allow_redirects=allow_redirects,
                        proxies=proxies or None,
                    )
                elif method == HttpMethod.DELETE.value:
                    response = self._session.delete(
                        url,
                        headers=headers,
                        data=data,
                        params=params,
                        cookies=cookies,
                        timeout=timeout,
                        allow_redirects=allow_redirects,
                        proxies=proxies or None,
                    )
                else:
                    raise NetworkError(f"Unsupported HTTP method: {method}")

                elapsed = time.time() - start_time
                response.raise_for_status()

                # Buat Response object
                response_obj = Response(
                    request_id=request.request_id,
                    status_code=response.status_code,
                    headers=dict(response.headers),
                    body=response.content if not request.stream else None,
                    url=response.url,
                    elapsed=elapsed,
                    encoding=response.encoding,
                    is_from_cache=False,
                )

                # Intercept response
                response_obj = self._intercept_response(response_obj)
                return response_obj

            except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
                error_message = str(e)
                if isinstance(e, requests.exceptions.Timeout):
                    request.status = RequestStatus.TIMEOUT
                    status_code = 408
                elif isinstance(e, requests.exceptions.TooManyRedirects):
                    request.status = RequestStatus.FAILED
                    status_code = 310
                elif isinstance(e, requests.exceptions.SSLError):
                    request.status = RequestStatus.FAILED
                    status_code = 495  # SSL Certificate Error
                elif isinstance(e, requests.exceptions.ConnectionError):
                    request.status = RequestStatus.FAILED
                    status_code = 502  # Bad Gateway
                else:
                    request.status = RequestStatus.FAILED
                    status_code = 500

                logger.error(f"Request failed: {error_message}")
                return Response(
                    request_id=request.request_id,
                    status_code=status_code,
                    headers={},
                    body=error_message.encode(),
                    url=request.url,
                    elapsed=0.0,
                    is_from_cache=False,
                    timestamp=datetime.now(),
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Unexpected error in request: {e}")
                request.status = RequestStatus.FAILED
                return Response(
                    request_id=request.request_id,
                    status_code=500,
                    headers={},
                    body=str(e).encode(),
                    url=request.url,
                    elapsed=0.0,
                    is_from_cache=False,
                    timestamp=datetime.now(),
                )
            finally:
                if request.request_id in self._active_requests:
                    del self._active_requests[request.request_id]

    def get(self, url: str, **kwargs) -> Response:
        """HTTP GET request."""
        request = Request(
            url=url,
            method=HttpMethod.GET,
            headers=kwargs.get("headers", {}),
            params=kwargs.get("params", {}),
            cookies=kwargs.get("cookies", {}),
            timeout=kwargs.get("timeout", self._timeout),
            proxy=kwargs.get("proxy"),
        )
        return self.send_request(request)

    def post(self, url: str, **kwargs) -> Response:
        """HTTP POST request."""
        request = Request(
            url=url,
            method=HttpMethod.POST,
            headers=kwargs.get("headers", {}),
            body=kwargs.get("data"),
            params=kwargs.get("params", {}),
            cookies=kwargs.get("cookies", {}),
            timeout=kwargs.get("timeout", self._timeout),
            proxy=kwargs.get("proxy"),
        )
        return self.send_request(request)

    def put(self, url: str, **kwargs) -> Response:
        """HTTP PUT request."""
        request = Request(
            url=url,
            method=HttpMethod.PUT,
            headers=kwargs.get("headers", {}),
            body=kwargs.get("data"),
            params=kwargs.get("params", {}),
            cookies=kwargs.get("cookies", {}),
            timeout=kwargs.get("timeout", self._timeout),
            proxy=kwargs.get("proxy"),
        )
        return self.send_request(request)

    def delete(self, url: str, **kwargs) -> Response:
        """HTTP DELETE request."""
        request = Request(
            url=url,
            method=HttpMethod.DELETE,
            headers=kwargs.get("headers", {}),
            body=kwargs.get("data"),
            params=kwargs.get("params", {}),
            cookies=kwargs.get("cookies", {}),
            timeout=kwargs.get("timeout", self._timeout),
            proxy=kwargs.get("proxy"),
        )
        return self.send_request(request)

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._request_lock:
            self._active_requests.clear()
        with self._cache_lock:
            self._cache.clear()
        if self._session:
            self._session.close()
            self._session = None

--- END OF PAR 2 ---
```

```text
Berisi:
- TabManager: Manajemen tab (buat, tutup, switch)
- SessionManager: Manajemen session (simpan/load state)

"""
Tab Manager Implementation
===========================
Kelas untuk manajemen tab browser.
"""

import os
import json
import uuid
import time
import threading
import weakref
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Any, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    TabConfig,
    TabState,
    BrowserSettings,
    BrowserMode,
    Permission,
    BrowserError,
    ResourceError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.config.settings_loader import SettingsLoader
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.utils.browser_utils import BrowserUtils

# --- ENUM ---
class TabEventType(Enum):
    """Tipe event untuk tab."""
    CREATED = auto()
    DESTROYED = auto()
    SWITCHED = auto()
    STATE_CHANGED = auto()
    URL_CHANGED = auto()
    TITLE_CHANGED = auto()
    PINNED = auto()
    UNPINNED = auto()
    MUTED = auto()
    UNMUTED = auto()
    ZOOM_CHANGED = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class Tab:
    """Representasi sebuah tab browser."""
    tab_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    window_id: str = ""
    url: str = "about:blank"
    title: str = "New Tab"
    state: TabState = TabState.ACTIVE
    is_pinned: bool = False
    is_muted: bool = False
    zoom_level: float = 1.0
    last_accessed: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    parent_tab_id: Optional[str] = None
    children_tab_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    session_id: Optional[str] = None
    permissions: Set[Permission] = field(default_factory=set)
    security_level: int = 2  # 1: LOW, 2: MEDIUM, 3: HIGH
    user_agent: Optional[str] = None
    custom_headers: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    proxy_settings: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    cache_enabled: bool = True
    javascript_enabled: bool = True
    images_enabled: bool = True
    cookies_enabled: bool = True
    history: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # Daftar URL yang dikunjungi
    favicon: Optional[str] = None

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Konversi tab ke dictionary."""
        return {
            **asdict(self),
            "last_accessed": self.last_accessed.isoformat(),
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "permissions": [p.name for p in self.permissions],
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "Tab":
        """Buat Tab dari dictionary."""
        permissions = set(
            Permission[p] for p in data.get("permissions", [])
        )
        return cls(
            tab_id=data.get("tab_id", str(uuid.uuid4())),
            window_id=data.get("window_id", ""),
            url=data.get("url", "about:blank"),
            title=data.get("title", "New Tab"),
            state=TabState[data.get("state", "ACTIVE")],
            is_pinned=data.get("is_pinned", False),
            is_muted=data.get("is_muted", False),
            zoom_level=data.get("zoom_level", 1.0),
            last_accessed=datetime.fromisoformat(data.get("last_accessed", datetime.now().isoformat())),
            created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(data.get("created_at", datetime.now().isoformat())),
            parent_tab_id=data.get("parent_tab_id"),
            children_tab_ids=data.get("children_tab_ids", []),
            session_id=data.get("session_id"),
            permissions=permissions,
            security_level=data.get("security_level", 2),
            user_agent=data.get("user_agent"),
            custom_headers=data.get("custom_headers", {}),
            proxy_settings=data.get("proxy_settings"),
            cache_enabled=data.get("cache_enabled", True),
            javascript_enabled=data.get("javascript_enabled", True),
            images_enabled=data.get("images_enabled", True),
            cookies_enabled=data.get("cookies_enabled", True),
            history=data.get("history", []),
            favicon=data.get("favicon"),
        )

# --- CALLBACK TYPE ---
TabEventCallback = Callable[[str, TabEventType, "Tab"], None]

# --- TAB MANAGER ---
class TabManager:
    """Manager untuk mengelola tab browser."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings):
        self.settings = settings
        self._tabs: Dict[str, Tab] = {}  # tab_id -> Tab
        self._windows: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}  # window_id -> [tab_id, ...]
        self._active_tab_per_window: Dict[str, str] = {}  # window_id -> active_tab_id
        self._tab_order: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}  # window_id -> [tab_id, ...] (urutan tab)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._event_callbacks: List[TabEventCallback] = []
        self._closed_tabs: Dict[str, Tab] = {}  # tab_id -> Tab (untuk session restoration)
        self._max_tabs = settings.max_tabs
        self._max_windows = settings.max_windows
        self._tab_counter = 0
        self._window_counter = 0
        logger.info("TabManager initialized")

    # --- PUBLIC METHODS ---
    def create_tab(
        self,
        window_id: str = "",
        url: str = "about:blank",
        title: str = "New Tab",
        is_pinned: bool = False,
        parent_tab_id: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Tab:
        """Buat tab baru."""
        with self._lock:
            # Cek batas maksimal tab
            if len(self._tabs) >= self._max_tabs:
                raise ResourceError(
                    f"Maximum tabs reached ({self._max_tabs}). Close some tabs first."
                )

            # Cek batas maksimal window
            if window_id and window_id not in self._windows:
                if len(self._windows) >= self._max_windows:
                    raise ResourceError(
                        f"Maximum windows reached ({self._max_windows}). Close some windows first."
                    )
                self._windows[window_id] = []
                self._tab_order[window_id] = []
                self._window_counter += 1

            # Jika window_id kosong, gunakan window default
            if not window_id:
                if not self._windows:
                    window_id = f"window_1"
                    self._windows[window_id] = []
                    self._tab_order[window_id] = []
                    self._window_counter = 1
                else:
                    window_id = next(iter(self._windows.keys()))

            # Validasi URL
            if not BrowserUtils.validate_url(url):
                raise ValidationError(f"Invalid URL: {url}")

            # Buat tab baru
            tab_id = f"tab_{self._window_counter}_{self._tab_counter}"
            self._tab_counter += 1

            tab = Tab(
                tab_id=tab_id,
                window_id=window_id,
                url=url,
                title=title,
                is_pinned=is_pinned,
                parent_tab_id=parent_tab_id,
                **kwargs,
            )

            # Tambahkan tab ke daftar
            self._tabs[tab_id] = tab
            self._windows[window_id].append(tab_id)
            self._tab_order[window_id].append(tab_id)

            # Set sebagai active tab jika window belum punya active tab
            if window_id not in self._active_tab_per_window:
                self._active_tab_per_window[window_id] = tab_id

            # Tambahkan ke children parent tab (jika ada)
            if parent_tab_id and parent_tab_id in self._tabs:
                parent_tab = self._tabs[parent_tab_id]
                parent_tab.children_tab_ids.append(tab_id)

            # Panggil event callback
            self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.CREATED, tab)

            logger.info(f"Created tab: {tab_id} | URL: {url} | Window: {window_id}")
            return tab

    def close_tab(self, tab_id: str, force: bool = False) -> bool:
        """Tutup tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            window_id = tab.window_id

            # Simpan tab ke closed_tabs untuk session restoration
            self._closed_tabs[tab_id] = tab

            # Hapus tab dari children parent (jika ada)
            if tab.parent_tab_id and tab.parent_tab_id in self._tabs:
                parent_tab = self._tabs[tab.parent_tab_id]
                if tab.tab_id in parent_tab.children_tab_ids:
                    parent_tab.children_tab_ids.remove(tab.tab_id)

            # Hapus tab dari daftar
            del self._tabs[tab_id]
            self._windows[window_id].remove(tab_id)
            self._tab_order[window_id].remove(tab_id)

            # Update active tab
            if self._active_tab_per_window.get(window_id) == tab_id:
                if self._tab_order[window_id]:
                    new_active_tab = self._tab_order[window_id][-1]
                    self._active_tab_per_window[window_id] = new_active_tab
                    self._notify_event(new_active_tab, TabEventType.SWITCHED, self._tabs[new_active_tab])
                else:
                    del self._active_tab_per_window[window_id]

            # Panggil event callback
            self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.DESTROYED, tab)

            logger.info(f"Closed tab: {tab_id} | Window: {window_id}")
            return True

    def switch_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Pindah ke tab yang ditentukan."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            window_id = tab.window_id

            # Set sebagai active tab
            self._active_tab_per_window[window_id] = tab_id
            self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.SWITCHED, tab)
            logger.info(f"Switched to tab: {tab_id} | Window: {window_id}")
            return True

    def get_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> Optional[Tab]:
        """Dapatkan tab berdasarkan ID."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._tabs.get(tab_id)

    def get_tabs_by_window(self, window_id: str) -> List[Tab]:
        """Dapatkan semua tab di window tertentu."""
        with self._lock:
            tab_ids = self._windows.get(window_id, [])
            return [self._tabs[tab_id] for tab_id in tab_ids if tab_id in self._tabs]

    def get_active_tab(self, window_id: str) -> Optional[Tab]:
        """Dapatkan tab aktif di window tertentu."""
        with self._lock:
            active_tab_id = self._active_tab_per_window.get(window_id)
            if active_tab_id and active_tab_id in self._tabs:
                return self._tabs[active_tab_id]
            return None

    def get_all_tabs(self) -> List[Tab]:
        """Dapatkan semua tab."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._tabs.values())

    def update_tab(self, tab_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update properti tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            old_url = tab.url
            old_title = tab.title
            old_state = tab.state

            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(tab, key):
                    setattr(tab, key, value)

            # Panggil event callback jika URL berubah
            if "url" in kwargs and old_url != tab.url:
                self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.URL_CHANGED, tab)

            # Panggil event callback jika title berubah
            if "title" in kwargs and old_title != tab.title:
                self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.TITLE_CHANGED, tab)

            # Panggil event callback jika state berubah
            if "state" in kwargs and old_state != tab.state:
                self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.STATE_CHANGED, tab)

            logger.info(f"Updated tab: {tab_id} | Changes: {kwargs}")
            return True

    def pin_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Pin tab (buat tab tetap di kiri)."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            if tab.is_pinned:
                return True

            tab.is_pinned = True
            self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.PINNED, tab)
            logger.info(f"Pinned tab: {tab_id}")
            return True

    def unpin_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Unpin tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            if not tab.is_pinned:
                return True

            tab.is_pinned = False
            self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.UNPINNED, tab)
            logger.info(f"Unpinned tab: {tab_id}")
            return True

    def mute_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Mute tab (nonaktifkan suara)."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            if tab.is_muted:
                return True

            tab.is_muted = True
            self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.MUTED, tab)
            logger.info(f"Muted tab: {tab_id}")
            return True

    def unmute_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Unmute tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            if not tab.is_muted:
                return True

            tab.is_muted = False
            self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.UNMUTED, tab)
            logger.info(f"Unmuted tab: {tab_id}")
            return True

    def set_zoom_level(self, tab_id: str, zoom_level: float) -> bool:
        """Set zoom level tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False

            if zoom_level < 0.1 or zoom_level > 5.0:
                raise ValidationError("Zoom level must be between 0.1 and 5.0")

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            old_zoom = tab.zoom_level
            tab.zoom_level = zoom_level

            if old_zoom != zoom_level:
                self._notify_event(tab_id, TabEventType.ZOOM_CHANGED, tab)

            logger.info(f"Set zoom level for tab {tab_id}: {zoom_level}")
            return True

    def move_tab(self, tab_id: str, new_window_id: str) -> bool:
        """Pindahkan tab ke window lain."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return False
            if new_window_id not in self._windows:
                return False

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            old_window_id = tab.window_id

            if old_window_id == new_window_id:
                return True

            # Hapus tab dari window lama
            self._windows[old_window_id].remove(tab_id)
            self._tab_order[old_window_id].remove(tab_id)

            # Tambahkan tab ke window baru
            tab.window_id = new_window_id
            self._windows[new_window_id].append(tab_id)
            self._tab_order[new_window_id].append(tab_id)

            # Update active tab di window lama
            if self._active_tab_per_window.get(old_window_id) == tab_id:
                if self._tab_order[old_window_id]:
                    new_active_tab = self._tab_order[old_window_id][-1]
                    self._active_tab_per_window[old_window_id] = new_active_tab
                else:
                    del self._active_tab_per_window[old_window_id]

            logger.info(f"Moved tab {tab_id} from window {old_window_id} to {new_window_id}")
            return True

    def duplicate_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> Optional[Tab]:
        """Duplikasi tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return None

            source_tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            new_tab = self.create_tab(
                window_id=source_tab.window_id,
                url=source_tab.url,
                title=f"{source_tab.title} (Copy)",
                is_pinned=source_tab.is_pinned,
                parent_tab_id=source_tab.tab_id,
                zoom_level=source_tab.zoom_level,
                permissions=source_tab.permissions.copy(),
                security_level=source_tab.security_level,
                user_agent=source_tab.user_agent,
                custom_headers=source_tab.custom_headers.copy(),
                proxy_settings=source_tab.proxy_settings,
                cache_enabled=source_tab.cache_enabled,
                javascript_enabled=source_tab.javascript_enabled,
                images_enabled=source_tab.images_enabled,
                cookies_enabled=source_tab.cookies_enabled,
            )
            logger.info(f"Duplicated tab {tab_id} to {new_tab.tab_id}")
            return new_tab

    def close_all_tabs(self, window_id: str = "") -> int:
        """Tutup semua tab di window tertentu (atau semua window)."""
        with self._lock:
            tabs_to_close = []
            if window_id:
                if window_id in self._windows:
                    tabs_to_close = self._windows[window_id].copy()
            else:
                tabs_to_close = list(self._tabs.keys())

            count = 0
            for tab_id in tabs_to_close:
                if self.close_tab(tab_id):
                    count += 1

            logger.info(f"Closed {count} tabs in window: {window_id if window_id else 'all'}")
            return count

    def close_other_tabs(self, tab_id: str) -> int:
        """Tutup semua tab kecuali tab yang ditentukan."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._tabs:
                return 0

            tab = self._tabs[tab_id]
            window_id = tab.window_id
            tabs_to_close = [
                tid for tid in self._windows.get(window_id, [])
                if tid != tab_id and tid in self._tabs
            ]

            count = 0
            for tid in tabs_to_close:
                if self.close_tab(tid):
                    count += 1

            logger.info(f"Closed {count} other tabs in window {window_id}")
            return count

    def restore_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> Optional[Tab]:
        """Restore tab dari closed_tabs."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id not in self._closed_tabs:
                return None

            tab = self._closed_tabs[tab_id]
            window_id = tab.window_id

            # Buat tab baru dari data tab lama
            new_tab = self.create_tab(
                window_id=window_id,
                url=tab.url,
                title=tab.title,
                is_pinned=tab.is_pinned,
                zoom_level=tab.zoom_level,
                permissions=tab.permissions.copy(),
                security_level=tab.security_level,
                user_agent=tab.user_agent,
                custom_headers=tab.custom_headers.copy(),
                proxy_settings=tab.proxy_settings,
                cache_enabled=tab.cache_enabled,
                javascript_enabled=tab.javascript_enabled,
                images_enabled=tab.images_enabled,
                cookies_enabled=tab.cookies_enabled,
            )

            # Hapus dari closed_tabs
            del self._closed_tabs[tab_id]
            logger.info(f"Restored tab {tab_id} as {new_tab.tab_id}")
            return new_tab

    def get_closed_tabs(self) -> List[Tab]:
        """Dapatkan daftar tab yang ditutup (untuk session restoration)."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._closed_tabs.values())

    def clear_closed_tabs(self) -> None:
        """Hapus semua tab yang ditutup dari memory."""
        with self._lock:
            self._closed_tabs.clear()
            logger.info("Cleared closed tabs history")

    # --- EVENT HANDLING ---
    def add_event_callback(self, callback: TabEventCallback) -> None:
        """Tambahkan callback untuk event tab."""
        with self._lock:
            self._event_callbacks.append(callback)

    def remove_event_callback(self, callback: TabEventCallback) -> None:
        """Hapus callback untuk event tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if callback in self._event_callbacks:
                self._event_callbacks.remove(callback)

    def _notify_event(self, tab_id: str, event_type: TabEventType, tab: Tab) -> None:
        """Panggil semua callback untuk event tab."""
        for callback in self._event_callbacks:
            try:
                callback(tab_id, event_type, tab)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error in tab event callback: {e}")

    # --- UTILITY METHODS ---
    def get_tab_count(self, window_id: str = "") -> int:
        """Dapatkan jumlah tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if window_id:
                return len(self._windows.get(window_id, []))
            return len(self._tabs)

    def get_window_count(self) -> int:
        """Dapatkan jumlah window."""
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._windows)

    def get_window_ids(self) -> List[str]:
        """Dapatkan daftar ID window."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._windows.keys())

    def reorder_tabs(self, window_id: str, new_order: List[str]) -> bool:
        """Ubah urutan tab di window."""
        with self._lock:
            if window_id not in self._windows:
                return False

            # Validasi new_order
            valid_tabs = set(self._windows[window_id])
            new_order_set = set(new_order)
            if not new_order_set.issubset(valid_tabs):
                return False

            self._tab_order[window_id] = new_order
            logger.info(f"Reordered tabs in window {window_id}: {new_order}")
            return True

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._tabs.clear()
            self._windows.clear()
            self._active_tab_per_window.clear()
            self._tab_order.clear()
            self._closed_tabs.clear()
            self._event_callbacks.clear()
            logger.info("TabManager cleaned up")

"""
Session Manager Implementation
===============================
Kelas untuk manajemen session browser (simpan/load state tab, window, dll.).
"""

import os
import json
import pickle
import zlib
import base64
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    BrowserError,
    ResourceError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.tab_manager import TabManager, Tab
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.config.settings_loader import SettingsLoader
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.utils.browser_utils import BrowserUtils

# --- SESSION MANAGER ---
class SessionManager:
    """Manager untuk menyimpan dan memuat session browser."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings, tab_manager: TabManager):
        self.settings = settings
        self.tab_manager = tab_manager
        self._session_path = Path(settings.session_path) if hasattr(settings, "session_path") else (
            Path.home() / ".musgo_os_2in1" / "browser" / "sessions"
        )
        self._session_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._current_session_id: Optional[str] = None
        self._auto_save_enabled = True
        self._auto_save_interval = 30  # Detik
        self._last_auto_save = datetime.now()
        self._auto_save_thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None
        self._running = False
        logger.info(f"SessionManager initialized | Session Path: {self._session_path}")

    def _get_session_file(self, session_id: str) -> Path:
        """Dapatkan path file session."""
        return self._session_path / f"{session_id}.session"

    def _serialize_session(self, session_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Serialize session ke dictionary."""
        tabs = self.tab_manager.get_all_tabs()
        windows = self.tab_manager.get_window_ids()
        active_tabs = self.tab_manager._active_tab_per_window.copy()
        tab_order = self.tab_manager._tab_order.copy()

        session_data = {
            "session_id": session_id,
            "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "tabs": [tab.to_dict() for tab in tabs],
            "windows": windows,
            "active_tabs": {wid: tid for wid, tid in active_tabs.items()},
            "tab_order": {wid: order for wid, order in tab_order.items()},
            "settings": asdict(self.settings),
        }
        return session_data

    def _deserialize_session(self, session_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Deserialize session dari dictionary."""
        with self._lock:
            # Hapus semua tab yang ada
            self.tab_manager.close_all_tabs()

            # Buat tab dari data session
            tabs_data = session_data.get("tabs", [])
            windows = session_data.get("windows", [])
            active_tabs = session_data.get("active_tabs", {})
            tab_order = session_data.get("tab_order", {})

            # Buat window
            for window_id in windows:
                if window_id not in self.tab_manager._windows:
                    self.tab_manager._windows[window_id] = []
                    self.tab_manager._tab_order[window_id] = []

            # Buat tab
            for tab_data in tabs_data:
                tab = Tab.from_dict(tab_data)
                self.tab_manager._tabs[tab.tab_id] = tab
                if tab.window_id not in self.tab_manager._windows:
                    self.tab_manager._windows[tab.window_id] = []
                    self.tab_manager._tab_order[tab.window_id] = []
                self.tab_manager._windows[tab.window_id].append(tab.tab_id)
                self.tab_manager._tab_order[tab.window_id].append(tab.tab_id)

            # Set active tabs
            for window_id, tab_id in active_tabs.items():
                if tab_id in self.tab_manager._tabs:
                    self.tab_manager._active_tab_per_window[window_id] = tab_id

            # Set tab order
            for window_id, order in tab_order.items():
                if window_id in self.tab_manager._tab_order:
                    self.tab_manager._tab_order[window_id] = order

            logger.info(f"Deserialized session with {len(tabs_data)} tabs")

    def save_session(self, session_id: str = "default") -> bool:
        """Simpan session ke file."""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                session_data = self._serialize_session(session_id)
                session_file = self._get_session_file(session_id)

                # Kompres dan simpan
                serialized = json.dumps(session_data, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
                compressed = zlib.compress(serialized)
                encoded = base64.b64encode(compressed).decode("utf-8")

                with open(session_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(encoded)

                self._current_session_id = session_id
                self._last_auto_save = datetime.now()
                logger.info(f"Saved session: {session_id} | Path: {session_file}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to save session {session_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def load_session(self, session_id: str = "default") -> bool:
        """Muat session dari file."""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                session_file = self._get_session_file(session_id)
                if not session_file.exists():
                    logger.warning(f"Session file not found: {session_file}")
                    return False

                with open(session_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    encoded = f.read()

                # Dekode dan dekompres
                compressed = base64.b64decode(encoded.encode("utf-8"))
                serialized = zlib.decompress(compressed)
                session_data = json.loads(serialized.decode("utf-8"))

                self._deserialize_session(session_data)
                self._current_session_id = session_id
                logger.info(f"Loaded session: {session_id} | Path: {session_file}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to load session {session_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def delete_session(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus session."""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                session_file = self._get_session_file(session_id)
                if session_file.exists():
                    session_file.unlink()
                    if self._current_session_id == session_id:
                        self._current_session_id = None
                    logger.info(f"Deleted session: {session_id}")
                    return True
                return False
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to delete session {session_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def list_sessions(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Dapatkan daftar session yang tersedia."""
        with self._lock:
            sessions = []
            for session_file in self._session_path.glob("*.session"):
                try:
                    session_id = session_file.stem
                    stat = session_file.stat()
                    sessions.append({
                        "session_id": session_id,
                        "path": str(session_file),
                        "size_bytes": stat.st_size,
                        "modified_at": datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime).isoformat(),
                    })
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Error reading session file {session_file}: {e}")
            return sessions

    def auto_save(self) -> None:
        """Auto-save session jika enabled."""
        with self._lock:
            if not self._auto_save_enabled:
                return

            now = datetime.now()
            if (now - self._last_auto_save).total_seconds() >= self._auto_save_interval:
                if self._current_session_id:
                    self.save_session(self._current_session_id)
                else:
                    self.save_session("auto_save")

    def start_auto_save(self) -> None:
        """Start auto-save thread."""
        with self._lock:
            if self._running:
                return

            self._running = True
            self._auto_save_thread = threading.Thread(
                target=self._auto_save_loop,
                daemon=True
            )
            self._auto_save_thread.start()
            logger.info("Auto-save thread started")

    def stop_auto_save(self) -> None:
        """Stop auto-save thread."""
        with self._lock:
            self._running = False
            if self._auto_save_thread:
                self._auto_save_thread.join(timeout=5)
                self._auto_save_thread = None
            logger.info("Auto-save thread stopped")

    def _auto_save_loop(self) -> None:
        """Loop untuk auto-save."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                self.auto_save()
                time.sleep(1)  # Cek setiap detik
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error in auto-save loop: {e}")

    def enable_auto_save(self, interval: int = 30) -> None:
        """Aktifkan auto-save."""
        with self._lock:
            self._auto_save_enabled = True
            self._auto_save_interval = interval
            if not self._running:
                self.start_auto_save()
            logger.info(f"Auto-save enabled | Interval: {interval}s")

    def disable_auto_save(self) -> None:
        """Nonaktifkan auto-save."""
        with self._lock:
            self._auto_save_enabled = False
            logger.info("Auto-save disabled")

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self.stop_auto_save()
            self._current_session_id = None
            logger.info("SessionManager cleaned up")

📄 /musgo-os-2in1/apps/browser/core/session/cookie_jar.py
"""
Cookie Jar Implementation
=========================
Kelas untuk manajemen cookie browser.
"""

import os
import json
import threading
import pickle
import hashlib
import base64
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    Permission,
    BrowserError,
    SecurityError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.utils.browser_utils import BrowserUtils

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class Cookie:
    """Representasi sebuah cookie."""
    name: str
    value: str
    domain: str
    path: str = "/"
    expires: Optional[datetime] = None
    secure: bool = False
    http_only: bool = False
    same_site: str = "Lax"  # Strict, Lax, None
    priority: str = "Medium"  # Low, Medium, High
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    accessed_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    persistent: bool = True
    host_only: bool = False

    def is_expired(self) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah cookie sudah expired."""
        if self.expires is None:
            return False
        return datetime.now() > self.expires

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Konversi cookie ke dictionary."""
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "value": self.value,
            "domain": self.domain,
            "path": self.path,
            "expires": self.expires.isoformat() if self.expires else None,
            "secure": self.secure,
            "http_only": self.http_only,
            "same_site": self.same_site,
            "priority": self.priority,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "updated_at": self.updated_at.isoformat(),
            "accessed_at": self.accessed_at.isoformat(),
            "persistent": self.persistent,
            "host_only": self.host_only,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "Cookie":
        """Buat Cookie dari dictionary."""
        expires = None
        if data.get("expires"):
            expires = datetime.fromisoformat(data["expires"])

        return cls(
            name=data["name"],
            value=data["value"],
            domain=data["domain"],
            path=data.get("path", "/"),
            expires=expires,
            secure=data.get("secure", False),
            http_only=data.get("http_only", False),
            same_site=data.get("same_site", "Lax"),
            priority=data.get("priority", "Medium"),
            created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(data["created_at"]),
            updated_at=datetime.fromisoformat(data["updated_at"]),
            accessed_at=datetime.fromisoformat(data["accessed_at"]),
            persistent=data.get("persistent", True),
            host_only=data.get("host_only", False),
        )

# --- COOKIE JAR ---
class CookieJar:
    """Kelas untuk manajemen cookie."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings, security_policy: SecurityPolicy):
        self.settings = settings
        self.security_policy = security_policy
        self._cookies: Dict[str, Dict[str, Cookie]] = {}  # domain -> {name: Cookie}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._cookie_path = Path(settings.cookie_path) if hasattr(settings, "cookie_path") else (
            Path.home() / ".musgo_os_2in1" / "browser" / "cookies"
        )
        self._cookie_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._cookie_file = self._cookie_path / "cookies.db"
        self._encrypted = settings.encrypt_cookies if hasattr(settings, "encrypt_cookies") else True
        self._encryption_key = settings.encryption_key if hasattr(settings, "encryption_key") else "default_key"
        self._load_cookies()
        logger.info(f"CookieJar initialized | Cookie Path: {self._cookie_file}")

    def _get_cookie_key(self, domain: str, name: str) -> str:
        """Generate key untuk cookie."""
        return f"{domain}_{name}"

    def _sanitize_cookie(self, cookie: Cookie) -> Cookie:
        """Sanitize cookie (hapus karakter berbahaya)."""
        cookie.name = re.sub(r"[^\w\-]", "", cookie.name)
        cookie.value = re.sub(r"[;\r\n]", "", cookie.value)
        cookie.domain = cookie.domain.lower()
        cookie.path = re.sub(r"[^\w\-\/]", "", cookie.path)
        return cookie

    def _validate_cookie(self, cookie: Cookie) -> None:
        """Validasi cookie."""
        if not cookie.name:
            raise ValidationError("Cookie name cannot be empty")
        if not cookie.domain:
            raise ValidationError("Cookie domain cannot be empty")
        if len(cookie.value) > 4096:  # RFC 6265: max 4096 bytes
            raise ValidationError("Cookie value exceeds maximum length (4096 bytes)")
        if len(cookie.domain) > 253:
            raise ValidationError("Cookie domain exceeds maximum length (253 bytes)")
        if not re.match(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9\-.]+\.[a-zA-Z]{2,}$", cookie.domain):
            raise ValidationError(f"Invalid cookie domain: {cookie.domain}")

    def _is_secure_context(self, url: str) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah URL menggunakan HTTPS."""
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        return parsed.scheme == "https"

    def _is_same_site(self, cookie_domain: str, request_domain: str) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah cookie domain dan request domain same-site."""
        if cookie_domain.startswith("."):
            return request_domain.endswith(cookie_domain)
        return cookie_domain == request_domain

    def _matches_domain(self, cookie_domain: str, request_domain: str) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah cookie domain cocok dengan request domain."""
        if cookie_domain == request_domain:
            return True
        if cookie_domain.startswith(".") and request_domain.endswith(cookie_domain):
            return True
        return False

    def _matches_path(self, cookie_path: str, request_path: str) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah cookie path cocok dengan request path."""
        if cookie_path == request_path:
            return True
        if request_path.startswith(cookie_path + "/"):
            return True
        if cookie_path == "/" and request_path.startswith("/"):
            return True
        return False

    def _is_cookie_allowed(self, cookie: Cookie, url: str) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah cookie diizinkan untuk URL."""
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        request_domain = parsed.netloc.lower()
        request_path = parsed.path

        # Cek domain
        if not self._matches_domain(cookie.domain, request_domain):
            return False

        # Cek path
        if not self._matches_path(cookie.path, request_path):
            return False

        # Cek secure
        if cookie.secure and not self._is_secure_context(url):
            return False

        # Cek same-site
        if cookie.same_site == "Strict":
            # Strict: Cookie hanya dikirim jika same-site dan top-level navigation
            return False  # Sederhanakan untuk blueprint
        elif cookie.same_site == "Lax":
            # Lax: Cookie dikirim untuk same-site dan safe top-level navigation
            return True
        elif cookie.same_site == "None":
            # None: Cookie dikirim untuk cross-site jika Secure
            return cookie.secure

        return True

    # --- PUBLIC METHODS ---
    def set_cookie(
        self,
        name: str,
        value: str,
        domain: str,
        path: str = "/",
        expires: Optional[datetime] = None,
        secure: bool = False,
        http_only: bool = False,
        same_site: str = "Lax",
        priority: str = "Medium",
        persistent: bool = True,
        host_only: bool = False,
    ) -> bool:
        """Set cookie."""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                # Validasi domain
                if not self._validate_domain(domain):
                    raise ValidationError(f"Invalid domain: {domain}")

                cookie = Cookie(
                    name=name,
                    value=value,
                    domain=domain,
                    path=path,
                    expires=expires,
                    secure=secure,
                    http_only=http_only,
                    same_site=same_site,
                    priority=priority,
                    persistent=persistent,
                    host_only=host_only,
                )

                # Sanitize dan validasi
                cookie = self._sanitize_cookie(cookie)
                self._validate_cookie(cookie)

                # Simpan cookie
                if domain not in self._cookies:
                    self._cookies[domain] = {}

                self._cookies[domain][name] = cookie
                self._save_cookies()
                logger.info(f"Set cookie: {name} | Domain: {domain}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to set cookie {name}: {e}")
                return False

    def get_cookie(self, name: str, domain: str, path: str = "/") -> Optional[Cookie]:
        """Dapatkan cookie."""
        with self._lock:
            domain = domain.lower()
            if domain in self._cookies and name in self._cookies[domain]:
                cookie = self._cookies[domain][name]
                if cookie.is_expired():
                    self.delete_cookie(name, domain)
                    return None
                if not self._matches_path(cookie.path, path):
                    return None
                # Update accessed_at
                cookie.accessed_at = datetime.now()
                return cookie
            return None

    def get_cookies_for_url(self, url: str) -> List[Cookie]:
        """Dapatkan semua cookie yang valid untuk URL."""
        with self._lock:
            parsed = urlparse(url)
            domain = parsed.netloc.lower()
            path = parsed.path

            cookies = []
            for cookie_domain, cookie_dict in self._cookies.items():
                for cookie_name, cookie in cookie_dict.items():
                    if not cookie.is_expired() and self._is_cookie_allowed(cookie, url):
                        cookies.append(cookie)
            return cookies

    def delete_cookie(self, name: str, domain: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus cookie."""
        with self._lock:
            domain = domain.lower()
            if domain in self._cookies and name in self._cookies[domain]:
                del self._cookies[domain][name]
                if not self._cookies[domain]:  # Jika domain kosong, hapus domain
                    del self._cookies[domain]
                self._save_cookies()
                logger.info(f"Deleted cookie: {name} | Domain: {domain}")
                return True
            return False

    def delete_cookies_for_domain(self, domain: str) -> int:
        """Hapus semua cookie untuk domain."""
        with self._lock:
            domain = domain.lower()
            if domain in self._cookies:
                count = len(self._cookies[domain])
                del self._cookies[domain]
                self._save_cookies()
                logger.info(f"Deleted {count} cookies for domain: {domain}")
                return count
            return 0

    def delete_all_cookies(self) -> int:
        """Hapus semua cookie."""
        with self._lock:
            count = sum(len(cookie_dict) for cookie_dict in self._cookies.values())
            self._cookies.clear()
            self._save_cookies()
            logger.info(f"Deleted all {count} cookies")
            return count

    def delete_expired_cookies(self) -> int:
        """Hapus semua cookie yang expired."""
        with self._lock:
            count = 0
            for domain, cookie_dict in list(self._cookies.items()):
                expired_cookies = [
                    name for name, cookie in cookie_dict.items()
                    if cookie.is_expired()
                ]
                for name in expired_cookies:
                    del cookie_dict[name]
                    count += 1
                if not cookie_dict:  # Jika domain kosong, hapus domain
                    del self._cookies[domain]
            if count > 0:
                self._save_cookies()
            logger.info(f"Deleted {count} expired cookies")
            return count

    def _validate_domain(self, domain: str) -> bool:
        """Validasi domain cookie."""
        if not domain:
            return False
        if len(domain) > 253:
            return False
        # Domain harus berupa hostname yang valid
        if domain.startswith("."):
            domain = domain[1:]
        try:
            # Cek apakah domain valid
            if domain.startswith("."):
                return False
            if ".." in domain:
                return False
            if domain.startswith("-") or domain.endswith("-"):
                return False
            for part in domain.split("."):
                if not part:
                    return False
                if part.startswith("-") or part.endswith("-"):
                    return False
                if not part.isalnum():
                    return False
            return True
        except:
            return False

    def _save_cookies(self) -> None:
        """Simpan cookie ke file."""
        try:
            data = {
                "version": 1,
                "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "cookies": [
                    cookie.to_dict()
                    for domain_dict in self._cookies.values()
                    for cookie in domain_dict.values()
                ],
            }

            serialized = json.dumps(data, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
            if self._encrypted:
                encrypted = BrowserUtils.encrypt_data(serialized.decode(), self._encryption_key)
                with open(self._cookie_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(encrypted)
            else:
                with open(self._cookie_file, "wb") as f:
                    f.write(serialized)

            logger.info(f"Saved {len(data['cookies'])} cookies to {self._cookie_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to save cookies: {e}")

    def _load_cookies(self) -> None:
        """Muat cookie dari file."""
        try:
            if not self._cookie_file.exists():
                return

            with open(self._cookie_file, "rb") as f:
                content = f.read().decode("utf-8")

            if self._encrypted:
                decrypted = BrowserUtils.decrypt_data(content, self._encryption_key)
                data = json.loads(decrypted)
            else:
                data = json.loads(content)

            cookies = data.get("cookies", [])
            for cookie_data in cookies:
                cookie = Cookie.from_dict(cookie_data)
                if not cookie.is_expired():
                    if cookie.domain not in self._cookies:
                        self._cookies[cookie.domain] = {}
                    self._cookies[cookie.domain][cookie.name] = cookie

            logger.info(f"Loaded {len(cookies)} cookies from {self._cookie_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load cookies: {e}")

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._save_cookies()
            self._cookies.clear()
            logger.info("CookieJar cleaned up")

--- END OF PAR 3 ---
```
