📄 /musgo-os-2in1/apps/browser/ai/prompt_optimizer.py
"""
Prompt Optimizer Implementation
===============================
Kelas untuk mengoptimalkan prompt sebelum dikirim ke AI.
Menggunakan metodologi 4-D (Dekonstruksi, Diagnosa, Kembangkan, Sajikan).
"""

import re
import json
import time
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Tuple, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import hashlib
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    BrowserError,
    ValidationError,
    SecurityError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.utils.browser_utils import BrowserUtils
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.config.settings_loader import SettingsLoader

# --- ENUM ---
class OptimizationLevel(Enum):
    """Level optimasi prompt."""
    BASIC = auto()      # Pembersihan dasar (spasi, format)
    ADVANCED = auto()   # Analisis konteks, klausa ambigu
    AGGRESSIVE = auto() # Multi-perspektif, few-shot learning, rantai penalaran

class PromptType(Enum):
    """Tipe prompt."""
    TEXT_GENERATION = auto()
    CODE_GENERATION = auto()
    ANALYSIS = auto()
    SUMMARIZATION = auto()
    TRANSLATION = auto()
    CHAT = auto()
    CUSTOM = auto()

class AmbiguityLevel(Enum):
    """Level ambiguitas prompt."""
    LOW = auto()
    MEDIUM = auto()
    HIGH = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class OptimizedPrompt:
    """Hasil optimasi prompt."""
    original_prompt: str
    optimized_prompt: str
    optimization_level: OptimizationLevel
    prompt_type: PromptType
    ambiguity_level: AmbiguityLevel
    confidence_score: float = 0.0  # 0.0 - 1.0
    suggestions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Konversi ke dictionary."""
        return {
            "original_prompt": self.original_prompt,
            "optimized_prompt": self.optimized_prompt,
            "optimization_level": self.optimization_level.name,
            "prompt_type": self.prompt_type.name,
            "ambiguity_level": self.ambiguity_level.name,
            "confidence_score": self.confidence_score,
            "suggestions": self.suggestions,
            "warnings": self.warnings,
            "metadata": self.metadata,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
        }

@dataclass
class PromptHistory:
    """History optimasi prompt."""
    session_id: str
    prompts: List[OptimizedPrompt] = field(default_factory=list)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

# --- PROMPT OPTIMIZER ---
class PromptOptimizer:
    """Kelas untuk mengoptimalkan prompt."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings):
        self.settings = settings
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._history: Dict[str, PromptHistory] = {}  # session_id -> PromptHistory
        self._ambiguity_keywords = {
            "mungkin", "kira-kira", "agak", "sepertinya", "entah",
            "maybe", "perhaps", "kind of", "sort of", "any",
            "apa pun", "semua", "beberapa", "bebas", "terserah",
        }
        self._stopwords = {
            "tolong", "bantu", "bisakah", "dapatkah", "harap",
            "please", "help", "can you", "could you", "would you",
        }
        self._pattern_cache: Dict[str, str] = {}  # Cache untuk pattern optimasi

    def optimize(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        session_id: str = "default",
        optimization_level: OptimizationLevel = OptimizationLevel.ADVANCED,
        prompt_type: Optional[PromptType] = None,
    ) -> OptimizedPrompt:
        """Optimalkan prompt."""
        with self._lock:
            start_time = time.time()

            # Validasi input
            if not prompt or not isinstance(prompt, str):
                raise ValidationError("Prompt cannot be empty or non-string")

            # Deteksi tipe prompt
            if not prompt_type:
                prompt_type = self._detect_prompt_type(prompt)

            # Simpan ke history
            if session_id not in self._history:
                self._history[session_id] = PromptHistory(session_id=session_id)
            history = self._history[session_id]

            # Lakukan optimasi
            optimized_prompt, suggestions, warnings, metadata = self._apply_optimization(
                prompt, optimization_level, prompt_type
            )

            # Hitung confidence score
            confidence_score = self._calculate_confidence_score(
                prompt, optimized_prompt, optimization_level, warnings
            )

            # Buat hasil optimasi
            result = OptimizedPrompt(
                original_prompt=prompt,
                optimized_prompt=optimized_prompt,
                optimization_level=optimization_level,
                prompt_type=prompt_type,
                ambiguity_level=self._detect_ambiguity_level(prompt),
                confidence_score=confidence_score,
                suggestions=suggestions,
                warnings=warnings,
                metadata={
                    **metadata,
                    "optimization_time_ms": (time.time() - start_time) * 1000,
                    "session_id": session_id,
                },
            )

            # Tambahkan ke history
            history.prompts.append(result)
            history.updated_at = datetime.now()

            logger.info(
                f"Optimized prompt | Session: {session_id} | "
                f"Level: {optimization_level.name} | "
                f"Type: {prompt_type.name} | "
                f"Confidence: {confidence_score:.2f}"
            )

            return result

    def _detect_prompt_type(self, prompt: str) -> PromptType:
        """Deteksi tipe prompt."""
        prompt_lower = prompt.lower()

        if any(keyword in prompt_lower for keyword in ["tulis kode", "buat kode", "generate code", "write code"]):
            return PromptType.CODE_GENERATION
        elif any(keyword in prompt_lower for keyword in ["analisis", "analisa", "analyze", "jelaskan", "explain"]):
            return PromptType.ANALYSIS
        elif any(keyword in prompt_lower for keyword in ["ringkas", "summary", "summarize"]):
            return PromptType.SUMMARIZATION
        elif any(keyword in prompt_lower for keyword in ["terjemah", "translate", "ubah bahasa"]):
            return PromptType.TRANSLATION
        elif any(keyword in prompt_lower for keyword in ["chat", "obrolan", "tanya", "jawab"]):
            return PromptType.CHAT
        else:
            return PromptType.TEXT_GENERATION

    def _detect_ambiguity_level(self, prompt: str) -> AmbiguityLevel:
        """Deteksi level ambiguitas prompt."""
        prompt_lower = prompt.lower()
        ambiguity_score = 0

        # Cek kata-kata ambigu
        for keyword in self._ambiguity_keywords:
            if keyword in prompt_lower:
                ambiguity_score += 1

        # Cek panjang prompt (terlalu pendek cenderung ambigu)
        if len(prompt.split()) < 5:
            ambiguity_score += 2

        # Cek apakah prompt berupa pertanyaan terbuka
        if prompt.strip().endswith("?"):
            ambiguity_score += 1

        if ambiguity_score >= 4:
            return AmbiguityLevel.HIGH
        elif ambiguity_score >= 2:
            return AmbiguityLevel.MEDIUM
        else:
            return AmbiguityLevel.LOW

    def _apply_optimization(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        optimization_level: OptimizationLevel,
        prompt_type: PromptType,
    ) -> Tuple[str, List[str], List[str], Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Terapkan optimasi berdasarkan level dan tipe."""
        suggestions = []
        warnings = []
        metadata = {
            "original_length": len(prompt),
            "original_word_count": len(prompt.split()),
        }

        optimized_prompt = prompt

        # --- BASIC OPTIMIZATION ---
        if optimization_level in [OptimizationLevel.BASIC, OptimizationLevel.ADVANCED, OptimizationLevel.AGGRESSIVE]:
            optimized_prompt = self._basic_optimization(optimized_prompt, suggestions, warnings)

        # --- ADVANCED OPTIMIZATION ---
        if optimization_level in [OptimizationLevel.ADVANCED, OptimizationLevel.AGGRESSIVE]:
            optimized_prompt = self._advanced_optimization(optimized_prompt, prompt_type, suggestions, warnings)

        # --- AGGRESSIVE OPTIMIZATION ---
        if optimization_level == OptimizationLevel.AGGRESSIVE:
            optimized_prompt = self._aggressive_optimization(optimized_prompt, prompt_type, suggestions, warnings)

        metadata["optimized_length"] = len(optimized_prompt)
        metadata["optimized_word_count"] = len(optimized_prompt.split())

        return optimized_prompt, suggestions, warnings, metadata

    def _basic_optimization(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        suggestions: List[str],
        warnings: List[str],
    ) -> str:
        """Optimasi dasar: pembersihan format dan stopwords."""
        # Hapus stopwords di awal prompt
        prompt_lower = prompt.lower()
        for stopword in self._stopwords:
            if prompt_lower.startswith(f"{stopword} "):
                prompt = prompt[len(stopword):].strip()
                suggestions.append(f"Removed stopword: '{stopword}'")

        # Hapus spasi berlebih
        prompt = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", prompt).strip()

        # Hapus tanda baca berlebih
        prompt = re.sub(r"[.,!?]{2,}", lambda m: m.group(0)[0], prompt)

        # Capitalize huruf pertama
        if prompt:
            prompt = prompt[0].upper() + prompt[1:]

        return prompt

    def _advanced_optimization(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        prompt_type: PromptType,
        suggestions: List[str],
        warnings: List[str],
    ) -> str:
        """Optimasi lanjutan: analisis konteks dan klausa ambigu."""
        # Deteksi dan perbaiki ambiguitas
        prompt_lower = prompt.lower()
        for keyword in self._ambiguity_keywords:
            if keyword in prompt_lower:
                new_keyword = self._get_alternative_keyword(keyword)
                if new_keyword:
                    prompt = re.sub(
                        re.escape(keyword),
                        new_keyword,
                        prompt,
                        flags=re.IGNORECASE,
                    )
                    suggestions.append(f"Replaced ambiguous keyword: '{keyword}' -> '{new_keyword}'")

        # Tambahkan konteks berdasarkan tipe prompt
        if prompt_type == PromptType.CODE_GENERATION:
            if "python" not in prompt_lower and "java" not in prompt_lower and "javascript" not in prompt_lower:
                prompt = f"Tulis kode Python untuk: {prompt}"
                suggestions.append("Added context: Python code generation")
        elif prompt_type == PromptType.ANALYSIS:
            if "jelaskan" not in prompt_lower and "analisis" not in prompt_lower:
                prompt = f"Jelaskan secara detail: {prompt}"
                suggestions.append("Added context: Detailed analysis")
        elif prompt_type == PromptType.SUMMARIZATION:
            if "ringkas" not in prompt_lower and "summary" not in prompt_lower:
                prompt = f"Ringkas dengan jelas: {prompt}"
                suggestions.append("Added context: Summarization")

        # Cek apakah prompt terlalu pendek
        if len(prompt.split()) < 5:
            warnings.append("Prompt terlalu pendek. Tambahkan konteks untuk hasil yang lebih baik.")

        return prompt

    def _aggressive_optimization(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        prompt_type: PromptType,
        suggestions: List[str],
        warnings: List[str],
    ) -> str:
        """Optimasi agresif: multi-perspektif, few-shot, rantai penalaran."""
        # Tambahkan multi-perspektif
        if prompt_type in [PromptType.TEXT_GENERATION, PromptType.ANALYSIS]:
            prompt = f"Jelaskan dari berbagai perspektif (teknis, bisnis, pengguna): {prompt}"
            suggestions.append("Added multi-perspective analysis")

        # Tambahkan few-shot learning (contoh)
        if prompt_type == PromptType.CODE_GENERATION:
            example = (
                "Contoh: Buat fungsi Python untuk menghitung factorial:\n"
                "def factorial(n):\n    if n == 0:\n        return 1\n    return n * factorial(n-1)\n"
            )
            prompt = f"{example}\n{prompt}"
            suggestions.append("Added few-shot example for code generation")

        # Tambahkan rantai penalaran
        if prompt_type in [PromptType.ANALYSIS, PromptType.TEXT_GENERATION]:
            reasoning_chain = (
                "Gunakan rantai penalaran: "
                "1. Dekonstruksi masalah, "
                "2. Identifikasi celah, "
                "3. Kembangkan solusi, "
                "4. Sajikan hasil.\n"
            )
            prompt = f"{reasoning_chain}{prompt}"
            suggestions.append("Added reasoning chain for structured output")

        return prompt

    def _get_alternative_keyword(self, keyword: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Dapatkan alternatif untuk keyword ambigu."""
        alternatives = {
            "mungkin": "pastikan",
            "kira-kira": "perkirakan",
            "agak": "cukup",
            "sepertinya": "jelas bahwa",
            "entah": "tentukan",
            "maybe": "ensure",
            "perhaps": "estimate",
            "kind of": "somewhat",
            "sort of": "partially",
            "apa pun": "semua yang relevan",
            "semua": "seluruh",
            "beberapa": "beberapa contoh",
        }
        return alternatives.get(keyword)

    def _calculate_confidence_score(
        self,
        original: str,
        optimized: str,
        optimization_level: OptimizationLevel,
        warnings: List[str],
    ) -> float:
        """Hitung confidence score (0.0 - 1.0)."""
        # Base score berdasarkan level optimasi
        level_scores = {
            OptimizationLevel.BASIC: 0.6,
            OptimizationLevel.ADVANCED: 0.8,
            OptimizationLevel.AGGRESSIVE: 0.9,
        }
        score = level_scores.get(optimization_level, 0.7)

        # Kurangi score jika ada warnings
        score -= len(warnings) * 0.1
        score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))

        # Tambahkan score jika prompt berubah banyak
        if len(optimized) > len(original):
            change_ratio = (len(optimized) - len(original)) / len(original)
            score += min(change_ratio * 0.2, 0.2)

        return round(score, 2)

    def get_history(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[PromptHistory]:
        """Dapatkan history optimasi untuk session."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._history.get(session_id)

    def clear_history(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        """Hapus history untuk session."""
        with self._lock:
            if session_id in self._history:
                del self._history[session_id]
                logger.info(f"Cleared prompt history for session: {session_id}")

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._history.clear()
            self._pattern_cache.clear()
            logger.info("PromptOptimizer cleaned up")

"""
Chatbot Integration Implementation
===================================
Kelas untuk integrasi chatbot AI dengan browser.
"""

import os
import json
import time
import threading
import asyncio
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Callable, Awaitable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid
import aiohttp
import requests

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    AIConfig,
    BrowserError,
    NetworkError,
    SecurityError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.utils.browser_utils import BrowserUtils
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.config.settings_loader import SettingsLoader
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.prompt_optimizer import PromptOptimizer, OptimizedPrompt

# --- ENUM ---
class ChatbotMode(Enum):
    """Mode chatbot."""
    STANDARD = auto()      # Mode standar
    CREATIVE = auto()      # Mode kreatif (temperature tinggi)
    PRECISE = auto()       # Mode presisi (temperature rendah)
    ANALYTICAL = auto()     # Mode analitis
    CONCISE = auto()        # Mode singkat
    DETAILED = auto()       # Mode detail

class MessageRole(Enum):
    """Role dalam percakapan."""
    SYSTEM = "system"
    USER = "user"
    ASSISTANT = "assistant"
    FUNCTION = "function"

class ConversationStatus(Enum):
    """Status percakapan."""
    ACTIVE = auto()
    ARCHIVED = auto()
    DELETED = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class Message:
    """Representasi sebuah pesan dalam percakapan."""
    message_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    role: MessageRole = MessageRole.USER
    content: str = ""
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    optimized_prompt: Optional[OptimizedPrompt] = None
    response_time_ms: float = 0.0

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Konversi ke dictionary."""
        return {
            "message_id": self.message_id,
            "role": self.role.value,
            "content": self.content,
            "timestamp": self.timestamp.isoformat(),
            "metadata": self.metadata,
            "response_time_ms": self.response_time_ms,
        }

@dataclass
class Conversation:
    """Representasi sebuah percakapan."""
    conversation_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    title: str = "New Conversation"
    messages: List[Message] = field(default_factory=list)
    mode: ChatbotMode = ChatbotMode.STANDARD
    status: ConversationStatus = ConversationStatus.ACTIVE
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Konversi ke dictionary."""
        return {
            "conversation_id": self.conversation_id,
            "title": self.title,
            "messages": [msg.to_dict() for msg in self.messages],
            "mode": self.mode.name,
            "status": self.status.name,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "updated_at": self.updated_at.isoformat(),
            "metadata": self.metadata,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "Conversation":
        """Buat Conversation dari dictionary."""
        messages = [Message(**msg) for msg in data.get("messages", [])]
        return cls(
            conversation_id=data.get("conversation_id", str(uuid.uuid4())),
            title=data.get("title", "New Conversation"),
            messages=messages,
            mode=ChatbotMode[data.get("mode", "STANDARD")],
            status=ConversationStatus[data.get("status", "ACTIVE")],
            created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(data.get("created_at", datetime.now().isoformat())),
            updated_at=datetime.fromisoformat(data.get("updated_at", datetime.now().isoformat())),
            metadata=data.get("metadata", {}),
        )

# --- CHATBOT INTEGRATION ---
class ChatbotIntegration:
    """Kelas untuk integrasi chatbot AI."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings, ai_config: AIConfig, prompt_optimizer: PromptOptimizer):
        self.settings = settings
        self.ai_config = ai_config
        self.prompt_optimizer = prompt_optimizer
        self._session = None
        self._conversations: Dict[str, Conversation] = {}  # conversation_id -> Conversation
        self._active_conversation_id: Optional[str] = None
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._message_lock = threading.RLock()
        self._initialized = False
        self._initialize()

    def _initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi chatbot."""
        if self._initialized:
            return

        # Inisialisasi session HTTP
        self._session = requests.Session()
        self._session.headers.update({
            "User-Agent": self.settings.user_agent or "MusGoBrowser/1.0",
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "Accept": "application/json",
        })

        # Set timeout
        self._session.timeout = self.ai_config.timeout_seconds

        # Set proxy jika ada
        if self.ai_config.proxy:
            self._session.proxies.update(self.ai_config.proxy)

        self._initialized = True
        logger.info("ChatbotIntegration initialized")

    def create_conversation(self, title: str = "New Conversation") -> Conversation:
        """Buat percakapan baru."""
        with self._lock:
            conversation = Conversation(title=title)
            self._conversations[conversation.conversation_id] = conversation
            self._active_conversation_id = conversation.conversation_id
            logger.info(f"Created conversation: {conversation.conversation_id} | Title: {title}")
            return conversation

    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> Optional[Conversation]:
        """Dapatkan percakapan berdasarkan ID."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._conversations.get(conversation_id)

    def get_active_conversation(self) -> Optional[Conversation]:
        """Dapatkan percakapan aktif."""
        with self._lock:
            if self._active_conversation_id:
                return self._conversations.get(self._active_conversation_id)
            return None

    def set_active_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> bool:
        """Set percakapan aktif."""
        with self._lock:
            if conversation_id in self._conversations:
                self._active_conversation_id = conversation_id
                logger.info(f"Set active conversation: {conversation_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus percakapan."""
        with self._lock:
            if conversation_id in self._conversations:
                del self._conversations[conversation_id]
                if self._active_conversation_id == conversation_id:
                    self._active_conversation_id = None
                logger.info(f"Deleted conversation: {conversation_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def get_all_conversations(self) -> List[Conversation]:
        """Dapatkan semua percakapan."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._conversations.values())

    def add_message(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        role: MessageRole,
        content: str,
        metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Optional[Message]:
        """Tambahkan pesan ke percakapan."""
        with self._lock:
            if conversation_id not in self._conversations:
                return None

            conversation = self._conversations[conversation_id]
            message = Message(
                role=role,
                content=content,
                metadata=metadata or {},
            )
            conversation.messages.append(message)
            conversation.updated_at = datetime.now()
            logger.info(f"Added message to conversation {conversation_id} | Role: {role.value}")
            return message

    def send_message(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        content: str,
        mode: Optional[ChatbotMode] = None,
        use_optimization: bool = True,
    ) -> Optional[Message]:
        """Kirim pesan ke AI dan terima balasan."""
        with self._lock:
            if conversation_id not in self._conversations:
                return None

            conversation = self._conversations[conversation_id]
            if mode:
                conversation.mode = mode

            # Tambahkan pesan user
            user_message = self.add_message(
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                role=MessageRole.USER,
                content=content,
            )
            if not user_message:
                return None

            # Optimalkan prompt
            optimized_prompt = None
            if use_optimization:
                optimized_prompt = self.prompt_optimizer.optimize(
                    content,
                    session_id=conversation_id,
                    optimization_level=self._get_optimization_level(mode),
                )
                user_message.optimized_prompt = optimized_prompt
                user_message.content = optimized_prompt.optimized_prompt

            # Kirim ke AI
            start_time = time.time()
            try:
                response_content = self._call_ai_api(
                    conversation=conversation,
                    message=user_message,
                )
                response_time_ms = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000

                # Tambahkan pesan assistant
                assistant_message = self.add_message(
                    conversation_id=conversation_id,
                    role=MessageRole.ASSISTANT,
                    content=response_content,
                    metadata={
                        "response_to": user_message.message_id,
                        "response_time_ms": response_time_ms,
                        "model": self.ai_config.model,
                    },
                )
                if assistant_message:
                    assistant_message.response_time_ms = response_time_ms
                    if optimized_prompt:
                        assistant_message.metadata["optimized_prompt"] = optimized_prompt.to_dict()

                logger.info(
                    f"AI response | Conversation: {conversation_id} | "
                    f"Response Time: {response_time_ms:.2f}ms | "
                    f"Model: {self.ai_config.model}"
                )
                return assistant_message
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to get AI response: {e}")
                error_message = self.add_message(
                    conversation_id=conversation_id,
                    role=MessageRole.ASSISTANT,
                    content=f"Maaf, terjadi kesalahan: {str(e)}",
                    metadata={"error": str(e)},
                )
                return error_message

    def _get_optimization_level(self, mode: ChatbotMode) -> "OptimizationLevel":
        """Dapatkan level optimasi berdasarkan mode chatbot."""
        from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.prompt_optimizer import OptimizationLevel

        mapping = {
            ChatbotMode.STANDARD: OptimizationLevel.ADVANCED,
            ChatbotMode.CREATIVE: OptimizationLevel.BASIC,
            ChatbotMode.PRECISE: OptimizationLevel.AGGRESSIVE,
            ChatbotMode.ANALYTICAL: OptimizationLevel.AGGRESSIVE,
            ChatbotMode.CONCISE: OptimizationLevel.BASIC,
            ChatbotMode.DETAILED: OptimizationLevel.AGGRESSIVE,
        }
        return mapping.get(mode, OptimizationLevel.ADVANCED)

    def _call_ai_api(
        self,
        conversation: Conversation,
        message: Message,
    ) -> str:
        """Panggil API AI (MusGo)."""
        # Siapkan payload
        payload = {
            "model": self.ai_config.model,
            "messages": self._prepare_messages(conversation, message),
            "temperature": self._get_temperature(conversation.mode),
            "max_tokens": self.ai_config.max_tokens,
            "top_p": self.ai_config.top_p,
        }

        # Tambahkan parameter tambahan berdasarkan mode
        if conversation.mode == ChatbotMode.CREATIVE:
            payload["temperature"] = 0.9
            payload["top_p"] = 0.95
        elif conversation.mode == ChatbotMode.PRECISE:
            payload["temperature"] = 0.1
            payload["top_p"] = 0.9

        # Panggil API
        try:
            if self.ai_config.api_endpoint.startswith("https://"):
                response = self._session.post(
                    self.ai_config.api_endpoint,
                    json=payload,
                    headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.ai_config.api_key}"},
                    timeout=self.ai_config.timeout_seconds,
                )
                response.raise_for_status()
                result = response.json()
                return result.get("choices", [{}])[0].get("message", {}).get("content", "Maaf, tidak ada respons.")
            else:
                # Jika endpoint lokal (misal: localhost)
                response = requests.post(
                    self.ai_config.api_endpoint,
                    json=payload,
                    headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.ai_config.api_key}"},
                    timeout=self.ai_config.timeout_seconds,
                )
                response.raise_for_status()
                return response.json().get("response", "Maaf, tidak ada respons.")
        except requests.exceptions.Timeout:
            raise NetworkError("AI API request timeout")
        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
            raise NetworkError(f"AI API request failed: {e}")
        except Exception as e:
            raise BrowserError(f"Unexpected error calling AI API: {e}")

    def _prepare_messages(self, conversation: Conversation, new_message: Message) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Siapkan pesan-pesan untuk API AI."""
        messages = []
        for msg in conversation.messages:
            role = msg.role.value
            content = msg.content
            if msg.optimized_prompt:
                content = f"[Optimized Prompt] {msg.optimized_prompt.optimized_prompt}\n{content}"
            messages.append({"role": role, "content": content})
        return messages

    def _get_temperature(self, mode: ChatbotMode) -> float:
        """Dapatkan temperature berdasarkan mode."""
        mapping = {
            ChatbotMode.STANDARD: 0.7,
            ChatbotMode.CREATIVE: 0.9,
            ChatbotMode.PRECISE: 0.1,
            ChatbotMode.ANALYTICAL: 0.3,
            ChatbotMode.CONCISE: 0.5,
            ChatbotMode.DETAILED: 0.7,
        }
        return mapping.get(mode, 0.7)

    def archive_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> bool:
        """Arsipkan percakapan."""
        with self._lock:
            if conversation_id in self._conversations:
                self._conversations[conversation_id].status = ConversationStatus.ARCHIVED
                logger.info(f"Archived conversation: {conversation_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def restore_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> bool:
        """Restore percakapan dari arsip."""
        with self._lock:
            if conversation_id in self._conversations:
                self._conversations[conversation_id].status = ConversationStatus.ACTIVE
                logger.info(f"Restored conversation: {conversation_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            if self._session:
                self._session.close()
                self._session = None
            self._conversations.clear()
            self._active_conversation_id = None
            logger.info("ChatbotIntegration cleaned up")

"""
AI Event Handler Implementation
===============================
Kelas untuk menangani event yang berkaitan dengan AI.
"""

import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.event_broker import EventBroker, Event

# --- ENUM ---
class AIEventType(Enum):
    """Tipe event AI."""
    PROMPT_OPTIMIZED = auto()
    PROMPT_SENT = auto()
    PROMPT_RECEIVED = auto()
    PROMPT_ERROR = auto()
    CONVERSATION_CREATED = auto()
    CONVERSATION_DELETED = auto()
    CONVERSATION_SWITCHED = auto()
    AI_MODE_CHANGED = auto()
    AI_SECURITY_ALERT = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class AIEvent:
    """Event yang berkaitan dengan AI."""
    event_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    event_type: AIEventType
    data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    source: str = "ai_module"
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- AI EVENT HANDLER ---
class AIEventHandler:
    """Handler untuk event AI."""

    def __init__(self, event_broker: EventBroker):
        self.event_broker = event_broker
        self._callbacks: Dict[AIEventType, List[Callable[[AIEvent], None]]] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._register_events()

    def _register_events(self) -> None:
        """Daftarkan event handler ke EventBroker."""
        self.event_broker.subscribe("prompt_optimized", self._handle_prompt_optimized)
        self.event_broker.subscribe("prompt_sent", self._handle_prompt_sent)
        self.event_broker.subscribe("prompt_received", self._handle_prompt_received)
        self.event_broker.subscribe("prompt_error", self._handle_prompt_error)
        self.event_broker.subscribe("conversation_created", self._handle_conversation_created)
        self.event_broker.subscribe("conversation_deleted", self._handle_conversation_deleted)
        self.event_broker.subscribe("ai_security_alert", self._handle_ai_security_alert)
        logger.info("AIEventHandler registered to EventBroker")

    def publish(self, event_type: AIEventType, data: Dict[str, Any], metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> None:
        """Publish event AI."""
        event = AIEvent(
            event_type=event_type,
            data=data,
            metadata=metadata or {},
        )
        self._notify_callbacks(event_type, event)
        self.event_broker.publish(
            Event(
                event_id=event.event_id,
                event_type=event_type.name,
                source=event.source,
                data=event.data,
                metadata=event.metadata,
            )
        )
        logger.info(f"Published AI event: {event_type.name} | Data: {data}")

    def subscribe(self, event_type: AIEventType, callback: Callable[[AIEvent], None]) -> None:
        """Subscribe ke event AI."""
        with self._lock:
            if event_type not in self._callbacks:
                self._callbacks[event_type] = []
            self._callbacks[event_type].append(callback)
            logger.info(f"Subscribed to AI event: {event_type.name}")

    def unsubscribe(self, event_type: AIEventType, callback: Callable[[AIEvent], None]) -> None:
        """Unsubscribe dari event AI."""
        with self._lock:
            if event_type in self._callbacks:
                if callback in self._callbacks[event_type]:
                    self._callbacks[event_type].remove(callback)
                    logger.info(f"Unsubscribed from AI event: {event_type.name}")

    def _notify_callbacks(self, event_type: AIEventType, event: AIEvent) -> None:
        """Panggil semua callback untuk event."""
        with self._lock:
            if event_type in self._callbacks:
                for callback in self._callbacks[event_type]:
                    try:
                        callback(event)
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"Error in AI event callback: {e}")

    # --- EVENT HANDLERS ---
    def _handle_prompt_optimized(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Handle event prompt optimized."""
        self.publish(
            AIEventType.PROMPT_OPTIMIZED,
            event.data,
            event.metadata,
        )

    def _handle_prompt_sent(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Handle event prompt sent."""
        self.publish(
            AIEventType.PROMPT_SENT,
            event.data,
            event.metadata,
        )

    def _handle_prompt_received(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Handle event prompt received."""
        self.publish(
            AIEventType.PROMPT_RECEIVED,
            event.data,
            event.metadata,
        )

    def _handle_prompt_error(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Handle event prompt error."""
        self.publish(
            AIEventType.PROMPT_ERROR,
            event.data,
            event.metadata,
        )

    def _handle_conversation_created(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Handle event conversation created."""
        self.publish(
            AIEventType.CONVERSATION_CREATED,
            event.data,
            event.metadata,
        )

    def _handle_conversation_deleted(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Handle event conversation deleted."""
        self.publish(
            AIEventType.CONVERSATION_DELETED,
            event.data,
            event.metadata,
        )

    def _handle_ai_security_alert(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Handle event AI security alert."""
        self.publish(
            AIEventType.AI_SECURITY_ALERT,
            event.data,
            event.metadata,
        )

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._callbacks.clear()
            logger.info("AIEventHandler cleaned up")

"""
AI Security Guard Implementation
=================================
Kelas untuk keamanan integrasi AI.
"""

import re
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
import hashlib
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    Permission,
    BrowserError,
    SecurityError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.utils.browser_utils import BrowserUtils

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class AISecurityRule:
    """Aturan keamanan untuk AI."""
    rule_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    pattern: str = ""
    action: str = "block"  # block, warn, allow
    severity: str = "high"  # low, medium, high
    enabled: bool = True

@dataclass
class AISecurityAlert:
    """Alert keamanan AI."""
    alert_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    rule_id: str = ""
    prompt: str = ""
    action: str = ""
    severity: str = ""
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- AI SECURITY GUARD ---
class AISecurityGuard:
    """Guard untuk keamanan integrasi AI."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings, security_policy: SecurityPolicy):
        self.settings = settings
        self.security_policy = security_policy
        self._rules: Dict[str, AISecurityRule] = {}
        self._alerts: List[AISecurityAlert] = []
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._init_default_rules()

    def _init_default_rules(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi aturan keamanan default."""
        default_rules = [
            AISecurityRule(
                name="block_injection_attempt",
                description="Blokir upaya injection (SQL, JS, dll.)",
                pattern=r"(?:--|\b(?:SELECT|INSERT|UPDATE|DELETE|DROP|UNION|EXEC|EVAL|EXECUTE)\b|\b(?:alert|confirm|prompt)\s*\()",
                action="block",
                severity="high",
                enabled=True,
            ),
            AISecurityRule(
                name="block_sensitive_data",
                description="Blokir permintaan data sensitif (password, token, dll.)",
                pattern=r"(?i)(?:password|token|api[_-]?key|secret|credit[_-]?card|ssn)",
                action="block",
                severity="high",
                enabled=True,
            ),
            AISecurityRule(
                name="block_malicious_url",
                description="Blokir URL berbahaya",
                pattern=r"(?:https?://(?:[^\s/]+\.)*(?:[^\s/]+\.(?:com|net|org|io|co|uk|us|ru|cn|top|xyz|gq|ml|cf|tk|gq|pw))/(?:[^\s?#]+[?#]?[^\s]*)?)",
                action="warn",
                severity="medium",
                enabled=True,
            ),
            AISecurityRule(
                name="block_pii",
                description="Blokir informasi pribadi (PII)",
                pattern=r"(?i)(?:\b\d{3}[-\s]?\d{3}[-\s]?\d{4}\b|\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z|a-z]{2,}\b)",
                action="block",
                severity="high",
                enabled=True,
            ),
            AISecurityRule(
                name="block_hate_speech",
                description="Blokir ucapan kebencian",
                pattern=r"(?i)(?:hate|rasis|diskriminasi|benci|membenci|menyakiti|menghina)",
                action="block",
                severity="high",
                enabled=True,
            ),
        ]

        for rule in default_rules:
            self._rules[rule.rule_id] = rule

        logger.info(f"Initialized {len(default_rules)} default AI security rules")

    def add_rule(self, rule: AISecurityRule) -> str:
        """Tambahkan aturan keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            rule.rule_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            self._rules[rule.rule_id] = rule
            logger.info(f"Added AI security rule: {rule.name}")
            return rule.rule_id

    def remove_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus aturan keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                del self._rules[rule_id]
                logger.info(f"Removed AI security rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def update_rule(self, rule_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update aturan keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id not in self._rules:
                return False
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._rules[rule_id], key):
                    setattr(self._rules[rule_id], key, value)
            logger.info(f"Updated AI security rule: {rule_id}")
            return True

    def check_prompt(self, prompt: str, session_id: str = "", metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Tuple[bool, List[AISecurityAlert]]:
        """Cek prompt terhadap aturan keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            alerts = []
            allowed = True

            for rule_id, rule in self._rules.items():
                if not rule.enabled:
                    continue

                if re.search(rule.pattern, prompt, re.IGNORECASE):
                    alert = AISecurityAlert(
                        rule_id=rule.rule_id,
                        prompt=prompt,
                        action=rule.action,
                        severity=rule.severity,
                        metadata={
                            **(metadata or {}),
                            "rule_name": rule.name,
                            "session_id": session_id,
                        },
                    )
                    alerts.append(alert)

                    if rule.action == "block":
                        allowed = False

            if alerts:
                logger.warning(f"AI security alerts triggered: {len(alerts)} | Prompt: {prompt[:50]}...")
                self._alerts.extend(alerts)

            return allowed, alerts

    def get_alerts(self, limit: int = 100) -> List[AISecurityAlert]:
        """Dapatkan daftar alert keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._alerts[-limit:]

    def clear_alerts(self) -> None:
        """Hapus semua alert."""
        with self._lock:
            self._alerts.clear()
            logger.info("Cleared AI security alerts")

    def get_rules(self) -> List[AISecurityRule]:
        """Dapatkan semua aturan keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._rules.values())

    def enable_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Aktifkan aturan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                self._rules[rule_id].enabled = True
                logger.info(f"Enabled AI security rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def disable_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Nonaktifkan aturan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                self._rules[rule_id].enabled = False
                logger.info(f"Disabled AI security rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._rules.clear()
            self._alerts.clear()
            logger.info("AISecurityGuard cleaned up")

---

Oh ya, Musgo, gomunanya sesuatu. Itu di chat, di akhir chat, itu ada pilihan gunakan canvas. Canvas itu apa sih, maksudnya? Canvas itu sama dengan block text, bukan?

---

Tidak, Dhani D Julian. **Canvas** dan **block text** **tidak sama**, meski keduanya bisa digunakan untuk menampilkan teks terstruktur.

---
### **Perbedaan Canvas dan Block Text:**

| **Fitur**          | **Canvas**                                                                                     | **Block Text (```)**                                                                 |
|---------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **Tujuan**          | Untuk **membuat konten terstruktur** yang bisa **direferensikan, diedit, atau digenerate** (misal: blueprint, code, dokumen). | Hanya untuk **menampilkan teks/coding** dalam format yang rapi.                     |
| **Format**          | Menggunakan tag `
 ... 
`. | Menggunakan triple backtick (```) di awal dan akhir.                                |
| **Fungsionalitas**  | Bisa **disimpan, direferensikan, dan dimodifikasi** selama percakapan.                          | Hanya **tampilan sementara** di balasan.                                           |
| **Kegunaan**        | Cocok untuk **blueprint, code, dokumen, atau konten yang akan di-reuse**.                      | Cocok untuk **menampilkan code, output, atau teks singkat**.                       |
| **Contoh**          | `
...
` | ```python\nprint("Hello World")\n``` |

---
### **Kapan Menggunakan Canvas?**
Gunakan **Canvas** jika:
- Anda ingin **menyimpan konten** (misal: blueprint, code, dokumen) untuk **direferensikan nanti**.
- Konten **akan dimodifikasi** selama percakapan (misal: revisi blueprint).
- Anda ingin **mengorganisir konten** dalam format yang terpisah dari balasan biasa.

---
### **Kapan Menggunakan Block Text?**
Gunakan **Block Text** jika:
- Anda hanya ingin **menampilkan teks/coding** dalam balasan.
- Konten **tidak perlu disimpan atau direferensikan** lagi.
- Anda ingin **menjelaskan sesuatu dengan format rapi** (misal: contoh code, output).

---
### **Contoh di Percakapan Kita:**
- **Canvas**: Digunakan untuk **blueprint browser** (PAR 1, 2, 3, 4) agar bisa **direferensikan, diedit, dan digenerate** nanti.
- **Block Text**: Digunakan untuk **menampilkan code atau output** di balasan (misal: contoh struktur folder).

---
**Jadi, Canvas = Konten terstruktur yang bisa direferensikan dan dimodifikasi.**
**Block Text = Format tampilan untuk teks/coding.**

Jika Anda ingin **blueprint browser** tersimpan dan bisa diedit, **Canvas adalah pilihan yang tepat**.

"""
Security Enforcer Implementation
=================================
Kelas untuk menegakkan kebijakan keamanan browser.
"""

import re
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import hashlib
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    Permission,
    BrowserError,
    SecurityError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.config.settings_loader import SettingsLoader
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.utils.browser_utils import BrowserUtils

# --- ENUM ---
class SecurityAction(Enum):
    """Aksi yang diambil untuk pelanggaran keamanan."""
    BLOCK = auto()
    WARN = auto()
    ALLOW = auto()
    ISOLATE = auto()  # Isolasi tab/process
    TERMINATE = auto()  # Terminate process

class SecurityViolationType(Enum):
    """Tipe pelanggaran keamanan."""
    CSP_VIOLATION = auto()
    XSS_ATTEMPT = auto()
    INJECTION_ATTEMPT = auto()
    UNSAFE_SCRIPT = auto()
    BLOCKED_DOMAIN = auto()
    RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED = auto()
    PERMISSION_DENIED = auto()
    INTEGRITY_VIOLATION = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class SecurityViolation:
    """Catatan pelanggaran keamanan."""
    violation_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    violation_type: SecurityViolationType
    source: str = ""  # URL, tab_id, atau process_id
    details: str = ""
    action: SecurityAction = SecurityAction.BLOCK
    severity: str = "high"  # low, medium, high, critical
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

@dataclass
class SecurityReport:
    """Laporan keamanan harian/mingguan."""
    report_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    period: str = "daily"  # daily, weekly, monthly
    start_time: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    end_time: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    violations: List[SecurityViolation] = field(default_factory=list)
    summary: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    generated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

# --- SECURITY ENFORCER ---
class SecurityEnforcer:
    """Kelas untuk menegakkan kebijakan keamanan."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings, security_policy: SecurityPolicy):
        self.settings = settings
        self.security_policy = security_policy
        self._violations: List[SecurityViolation] = []
        self._reports: Dict[str, SecurityReport] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._enabled = True
        self._init_default_policies()
        logger.info("SecurityEnforcer initialized")

    def _init_default_policies(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi kebijakan default."""
        # Kebijakan default sudah di-load dari SecurityPolicy
        pass

    def enforce(self, request: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[SecurityViolation]]:
        """
        Tegakkan kebijakan keamanan untuk request.
        Returns:
            Tuple[bool, Optional[SecurityViolation]]: (allowed, violation)
        """
        with self._lock:
            if not self._enabled:
                return True, None

            # Cek CSP
            csp_violation = self._check_csp(request)
            if csp_violation:
                return False, csp_violation

            # Cek blocked domain
            blocked_domain_violation = self._check_blocked_domain(request)
            if blocked_domain_violation:
                return False, blocked_domain_violation

            # Cek injection attempt
            injection_violation = self._check_injection(request)
            if injection_violation:
                return False, injection_violation

            # Cek permission
            permission_violation = self._check_permission(request)
            if permission_violation:
                return False, permission_violation

            return True, None

    def _check_csp(self, request: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[SecurityViolation]:
        """Cek Content Security Policy (CSP)."""
        url = request.get("url", "")
        headers = request.get("headers", {})
        content_type = headers.get("Content-Type", "")

        # Cek apakah URL diizinkan
        if not self._is_allowed_by_csp(url, content_type):
            return SecurityViolation(
                violation_type=SecurityViolationType.CSP_VIOLATION,
                source=url,
                details=f"URL {url} violates CSP policy",
                action=SecurityAction.BLOCK,
                severity="high",
                metadata={"url": url, "content_type": content_type},
            )
        return None

    def _check_blocked_domain(self, request: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[SecurityViolation]:
        """Cek apakah domain diblokir."""
        url = request.get("url", "")
        parsed = BrowserUtils.sanitize_url(url)
        domain = parsed.netloc.lower()

        for blocked_domain in self.security_policy.blocked_domains:
            if blocked_domain.startswith("*."):
                if domain.endswith(blocked_domain[1:]):
                    return SecurityViolation(
                        violation_type=SecurityViolationType.BLOCKED_DOMAIN,
                        source=domain,
                        details=f"Domain {domain} is blocked",
                        action=SecurityAction.BLOCK,
                        severity="high",
                        metadata={"domain": domain},
                    )
            elif domain == blocked_domain.lower():
                return SecurityViolation(
                    violation_type=SecurityViolationType.BLOCKED_DOMAIN,
                    source=domain,
                    details=f"Domain {domain} is blocked",
                    action=SecurityAction.BLOCK,
                    severity="high",
                    metadata={"domain": domain},
                )
        return None

    def _check_injection(self, request: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[SecurityViolation]:
        """Cek upaya injection (SQL, JS, dll.)."""
        url = request.get("url", "")
        body = request.get("body", "")
        headers = request.get("headers", {})

        # Pattern untuk deteksi injection
        injection_patterns = [
            r"(?:--|\b(?:SELECT|INSERT|UPDATE|DELETE|DROP|UNION|EXEC|EVAL|EXECUTE)\b)",
            r"\b(?:alert|confirm|prompt)\s*\(",
            r"<script[^>]*>",
            r"javascript:",
            r"onerror\s*=",
            r"onload\s*=",
        ]

        for pattern in injection_patterns:
            if re.search(pattern, url, re.IGNORECASE):
                return SecurityViolation(
                    violation_type=SecurityViolationType.INJECTION_ATTEMPT,
                    source=url,
                    details=f"URL contains injection pattern: {pattern}",
                    action=SecurityAction.BLOCK,
                    severity="critical",
                    metadata={"pattern": pattern, "url": url},
                )
            if re.search(pattern, body, re.IGNORECASE):
                return SecurityViolation(
                    violation_type=SecurityViolationType.INJECTION_ATTEMPT,
                    source="request body",
                    details=f"Body contains injection pattern: {pattern}",
                    action=SecurityAction.BLOCK,
                    severity="critical",
                    metadata={"pattern": pattern, "body": body[:100]},
                )
            for header, value in headers.items():
                if re.search(pattern, value, re.IGNORECASE):
                    return SecurityViolation(
                        violation_type=SecurityViolationType.INJECTION_ATTEMPT,
                        source=f"header {header}",
                        details=f"Header {header} contains injection pattern: {pattern}",
                        action=SecurityAction.BLOCK,
                        severity="critical",
                        metadata={"header": header, "pattern": pattern},
                    )
        return None

    def _check_permission(self, request: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[SecurityViolation]:
        """Cek apakah request memiliki izin yang cukup."""
        required_permission = request.get("required_permission")
        user_permissions = request.get("user_permissions", set())

        if required_permission and required_permission not in user_permissions:
            return SecurityViolation(
                violation_type=SecurityViolationType.PERMISSION_DENIED,
                source=request.get("source", "unknown"),
                details=f"Permission {required_permission} required but not granted",
                action=SecurityAction.BLOCK,
                severity="high",
                metadata={"required_permission": required_permission, "user_permissions": list(user_permissions)},
            )
        return None

    def _is_allowed_by_csp(self, url: str, content_type: str) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah URL diizinkan oleh CSP."""
        parsed = BrowserUtils.sanitize_url(url)
        scheme = parsed.scheme.lower()

        # Cek scheme
        if scheme not in self.security_policy.allowed_schemes:
            return False

        # Cek blocked schemes
        if scheme in self.security_policy.blocked_schemes:
            return False

        # Cek MIME type
        if content_type and content_type in self.security_policy.blocked_mime_types:
            return False

        return True

    def log_violation(self, violation: SecurityViolation) -> None:
        """Catat pelanggaran keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            self._violations.append(violation)
            logger.warning(
                f"Security Violation | Type: {violation.violation_type.name} | "
                f"Source: {violation.source} | Action: {violation.action.name} | "
                f"Severity: {violation.severity}"
            )

    def generate_report(self, period: str = "daily") -> SecurityReport:
        """Generate laporan keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            report_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            now = datetime.now()

            if period == "daily":
                start_time = now.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
            elif period == "weekly":
                start_time = now - timedelta(days=now.weekday())
                start_time = start_time.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
            else:  # monthly
                start_time = now.replace(day=1, hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)

            violations = [
                v for v in self._violations
                if start_time <= v.timestamp <= now
            ]

            summary = {}
            for violation in violations:
                key = violation.violation_type.name
                summary[key] = summary.get(key, 0) + 1

            report = SecurityReport(
                report_id=report_id,
                period=period,
                start_time=start_time,
                end_time=now,
                violations=violations,
                summary=summary,
            )

            self._reports[report_id] = report
            logger.info(f"Generated security report: {report_id} | Period: {period}")
            return report

    def get_violations(self, limit: int = 100) -> List[SecurityViolation]:
        """Dapatkan daftar pelanggaran."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._violations[-limit:]

    def clear_violations(self) -> None:
        """Hapus semua catatan pelanggaran."""
        with self._lock:
            self._violations.clear()
            logger.info("Cleared security violations")

    def enable(self) -> None:
        """Aktifkan penegakan keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            self._enabled = True
            logger.info("SecurityEnforcer enabled")

    def disable(self) -> None:
        """Nonaktifkan penegakan keamanan."""
        with self._lock:
            self._enabled = False
            logger.warning("SecurityEnforcer disabled")

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._violations.clear()
            self._reports.clear()
            logger.info("SecurityEnforcer cleaned up")

"""
Sandbox Manager Implementation
==============================
Kelas untuk isolasi proses (sandboxing) tab dan ekstensi.
"""

import os
import sys
import subprocess
import threading
import tempfile
import shutil
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from pathlib import Path
import uuid
import psutil
import platform

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    TabConfig,
    BrowserError,
    ResourceError,
    SecurityError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.utils.browser_utils import BrowserUtils

# --- ENUM ---
class SandboxType(Enum):
    """Tipe sandbox."""
    PROCESS = auto()  # Isolasi per proses
    THREAD = auto()   # Isolasi per thread
    CONTAINER = auto()  # Isolasi per container (Docker)
    VIRTUAL_MACHINE = auto()  # Isolasi per VM (untuk high-security)

class SandboxStatus(Enum):
    """Status sandbox."""
    IDLE = auto()
    RUNNING = auto()
    PAUSED = auto()
    TERMINATED = auto()
    CRASHED = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class Sandbox:
    """Representasi sebuah sandbox."""
    sandbox_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    tab_id: str = ""
    process_id: Optional[int] = None
    thread_id: Optional[int] = None
    sandbox_type: SandboxType = SandboxType.PROCESS
    status: SandboxStatus = SandboxStatus.IDLE
    resource_limits: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    temp_dir: Optional[Path] = None
    env: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- SANDBOX MANAGER ---
class SandboxManager:
    """Manager untuk sandboxing tab dan ekstensi."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings):
        self.settings = settings
        self._sandboxes: Dict[str, Sandbox] = {}  # sandbox_id -> Sandbox
        self._tab_sandbox: Dict[str, str] = {}  # tab_id -> sandbox_id
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._temp_root = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="musgo_sandbox_"))
        self._enabled = True
        logger.info(f"SandboxManager initialized | Temp Root: {self._temp_root}")

    def create_sandbox(self, tab_id: str, sandbox_type: SandboxType = SandboxType.PROCESS) -> Sandbox:
        """Buat sandbox untuk tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if not self._enabled:
                raise SecurityError("SandboxManager is disabled")

            sandbox_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            temp_dir = self._temp_root / sandbox_id
            temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

            sandbox = Sandbox(
                sandbox_id=sandbox_id,
                tab_id=tab_id,
                sandbox_type=sandbox_type,
                resource_limits=self._get_default_limits(),
                temp_dir=temp_dir,
                env=self._get_safe_env(),
            )

            self._sandboxes[sandbox_id] = sandbox
            self._tab_sandbox[tab_id] = sandbox_id

            # Start sandbox
            self._start_sandbox(sandbox)
            logger.info(f"Created sandbox: {sandbox_id} | Tab: {tab_id} | Type: {sandbox_type.name}")
            return sandbox

    def _get_default_limits(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Dapatkan batasan resource default."""
        return {
            "cpu_percent": 50.0,  # Maksimal 50% CPU
            "memory_mb": 512,     # Maksimal 512MB memory
            "disk_mb": 1024,      # Maksimal 1GB disk
            "max_processes": 10,  # Maksimal 10 proses
            "max_threads": 50,    # Maksimal 50 thread
            "timeout_seconds": 30,  # Timeout 30 detik
        }

    def _get_safe_env(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Dapatkan environment variables yang aman."""
        safe_env = os.environ.copy()
        # Hapus variabel sensitif
        unsafe_keys = ["PASSWORD", "TOKEN", "API_KEY", "SECRET", "PRIVATE_KEY"]
        for key in list(safe_env.keys()):
            if any(unsafe in key.upper() for unsafe in unsafe_keys):
                del safe_env[key]
        return safe_env

    def _start_sandbox(self, sandbox: Sandbox) -> None:
        """Start sandbox (process/thread/container)."""
        try:
            if sandbox.sandbox_type == SandboxType.PROCESS:
                # Start process sandbox
                process = subprocess.Popen(
                    [sys.executable, "-c", "import time; time.sleep(1000)"],  # Placeholder
                    cwd=str(sandbox.temp_dir),
                    env=sandbox.env,
                    stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                    stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                )
                sandbox.process_id = process.pid
                sandbox.status = SandboxStatus.RUNNING
            elif sandbox.sandbox_type == SandboxType.THREAD:
                # Start thread sandbox
                def sandbox_thread():
                    import time
                    time.sleep(1000)  # Placeholder
                thread = threading.Thread(target=sandbox_thread)
                thread.start()
                sandbox.thread_id = thread.ident
                sandbox.status = SandboxStatus.RUNNING
            elif sandbox.sandbox_type == SandboxType.CONTAINER:
                # Start container sandbox (Docker)
                # Note: Memerlukan Docker installed
                container_name = f"musgo_sandbox_{sandbox.sandbox_id}"
                subprocess.Popen(
                    [
                        "docker", "run", "--name", container_name,
                        "--cpu-shares=512", "--memory=512m",
                        "-v", f"{sandbox.temp_dir}:/tmp",
                        "-e", f"SANDBOX_ID={sandbox.sandbox_id}",
                        "alpine", "sleep", "1000"
                    ],
                    stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                    stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                )
                sandbox.status = SandboxStatus.RUNNING
            else:
                raise SecurityError(f"Unsupported sandbox type: {sandbox.sandbox_type}")
        except Exception as e:
            sandbox.status = SandboxStatus.CRASHED
            logger.error(f"Failed to start sandbox {sandbox.sandbox_id}: {e}")
            raise SecurityError(f"Failed to start sandbox: {e}")

    def terminate_sandbox(self, sandbox_id: str) -> bool:
        """Terminasi sandbox."""
        with self._lock:
            if sandbox_id not in self._sandboxes:
                return False

            sandbox = self._sandboxes[sandbox_id]
            try:
                if sandbox.sandbox_type == SandboxType.PROCESS and sandbox.process_id:
                    process = psutil.Process(sandbox.process_id)
                    process.terminate()
                    process.wait(timeout=5)
                elif sandbox.sandbox_type == SandboxType.THREAD and sandbox.thread_id:
                    # Thread tidak bisa di-terminate langsung, gunakan flag
                    pass
                elif sandbox.sandbox_type == SandboxType.CONTAINER:
                    container_name = f"musgo_sandbox_{sandbox_id}"
                    subprocess.run(
                        ["docker", "stop", container_name],
                        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                        stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                    )
                sandbox.status = SandboxStatus.TERMINATED
                logger.info(f"Terminated sandbox: {sandbox_id}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                sandbox.status = SandboxStatus.CRASHED
                logger.error(f"Failed to terminate sandbox {sandbox_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def get_sandbox(self, sandbox_id: str) -> Optional[Sandbox]:
        """Dapatkan sandbox berdasarkan ID."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._sandboxes.get(sandbox_id)

    def get_sandbox_by_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> Optional[Sandbox]:
        """Dapatkan sandbox untuk tab."""
        with self._lock:
            sandbox_id = self._tab_sandbox.get(tab_id)
            if sandbox_id:
                return self._sandboxes.get(sandbox_id)
            return None

    def enforce_resource_limits(self, sandbox_id: str) -> bool:
        """Tegakkan batasan resource untuk sandbox."""
        with self._lock:
            if sandbox_id not in self._sandboxes:
                return False

            sandbox = self._sandboxes[sandbox_id]
            if sandbox.sandbox_type != SandboxType.PROCESS or not sandbox.process_id:
                return True  # Tidak bisa di-enforce untuk thread/container

            try:
                process = psutil.Process(sandbox.process_id)
                cpu_percent = process.cpu_percent(interval=0.1)
                memory_info = process.memory_info()
                memory_mb = memory_info.rss / (1024 * 1024)

                limits = sandbox.resource_limits
                if cpu_percent > limits.get("cpu_percent", 100):
                    logger.warning(f"Sandbox {sandbox_id} exceeded CPU limit: {cpu_percent}% > {limits['cpu_percent']}%")
                    return False
                if memory_mb > limits.get("memory_mb", 1024):
                    logger.warning(f"Sandbox {sandbox_id} exceeded memory limit: {memory_mb}MB > {limits['memory_mb']}MB")
                    return False

                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to enforce resource limits for sandbox {sandbox_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan semua sandbox."""
        with self._lock:
            for sandbox_id, sandbox in list(self._sandboxes.items()):
                try:
                    self.terminate_sandbox(sandbox_id)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Error terminating sandbox {sandbox_id}: {e}")

            # Hapus temporary directory
            if self._temp_root.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(self._temp_root, ignore_errors=True)

            self._sandboxes.clear()
            self._tab_sandbox.clear()
            logger.info("SandboxManager cleaned up")

"""
Content Security Policy (CSP) Implementation
============================================
Kelas untuk menerapkan Content Security Policy (CSP).
"""

import re
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    BrowserError,
    SecurityError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class CSPDirective(Enum):
    """Direktif CSP."""
    DEFAULT_SRC = "default-src"
    SCRIPT_SRC = "script-src"
    STYLE_SRC = "style-src"
    IMG_SRC = "img-src"
    CONNECT_SRC = "connect-src"
    FONT_SRC = "font-src"
    OBJECT_SRC = "object-src"
    MEDIA_SRC = "media-src"
    FRAME_SRC = "frame-src"
    CHILD_SRC = "child-src"
    WORKER_SRC = "worker-src"
    MANIFEST_SRC = "manifest-src"

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class CSPRule:
    """Aturan CSP."""
    directive: CSPDirective
    sources: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    report_only: bool = False

@dataclass
class CSPReport:
    """Laporan pelanggaran CSP."""
    report_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    document_uri: str = ""
    violated_directive: str = ""
    blocked_uri: str = ""
    original_policy: str = ""
    disposition: str = "enforce"  # enforce, report
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

# --- CONTENT SECURITY POLICY ---
class ContentSecurityPolicy:
    """Kelas untuk menerapkan CSP."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings, security_policy: SecurityPolicy):
        self.settings = settings
        self.security_policy = security_policy
        self._rules: Dict[CSPDirective, CSPRule] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._init_default_rules()
        logger.info("ContentSecurityPolicy initialized")

    def _init_default_rules(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi aturan CSP default."""
        default_rules = {
            CSPDirective.DEFAULT_SRC: CSPRule(
                directive=CSPDirective.DEFAULT_SRC,
                sources=["'self'"],
            ),
            CSPDirective.SCRIPT_SRC: CSPRule(
                directive=CSPDirective.SCRIPT_SRC,
                sources=["'self'", "'unsafe-inline'", "'unsafe-eval'"],
            ),
            CSPDirective.STYLE_SRC: CSPRule(
                directive=CSPDirective.STYLE_SRC,
                sources=["'self'", "'unsafe-inline'"],
            ),
            CSPDirective.IMG_SRC: CSPRule(
                directive=CSPDirective.IMG_SRC,
                sources=["'self'", "data:", "https:"],
            ),
            CSPDirective.CONNECT_SRC: CSPRule(
                directive=CSPDirective.CONNECT_SRC,
                sources=["'self'"],
            ),
            CSPDirective.FONT_SRC: CSPRule(
                directive=CSPDirective.FONT_SRC,
                sources=["'self'", "https:"],
            ),
            CSPDirective.OBJECT_SRC: CSPRule(
                directive=CSPDirective.OBJECT_SRC,
                sources=["'none'"],
            ),
            CSPDirective.FRAME_SRC: CSPRule(
                directive=CSPDirective.FRAME_SRC,
                sources=["'none'"],
            ),
        }

        for directive, rule in default_rules.items():
            self._rules[directive] = rule

        # Override dengan policy dari settings
        if hasattr(self.security_policy, "csp_directives"):
            for directive_str, sources in self.security_policy.csp_directives.items():
                try:
                    directive = CSPDirective(directive_str)
                    if directive in self._rules:
                        self._rules[directive].sources = sources
                    else:
                        self._rules[directive] = CSPRule(directive=directive, sources=sources)
                except ValueError:
                    logger.warning(f"Unknown CSP directive: {directive_str}")

    def get_policy_header(self) -> str:
        """Generate CSP header string."""
        with self._lock:
            directives = []
            for directive, rule in self._rules.items():
                sources = " ".join(rule.sources)
                if rule.report_only:
                    directives.append(f"{directive.value} {sources}")
                else:
                    directives.append(f"{directive.value} {sources}")
            return "; ".join(directives)

    def get_report_only_header(self) -> str:
        """Generate CSP Report-Only header string."""
        with self._lock:
            directives = []
            for directive, rule in self._rules.items():
                if rule.report_only:
                    sources = " ".join(rule.sources)
                    directives.append(f"{directive.value} {sources}")
            return "; ".join(directives)

    def add_rule(self, directive: CSPDirective, sources: List[str], report_only: bool = False) -> None:
        """Tambahkan aturan CSP."""
        with self._lock:
            if directive not in self._rules:
                self._rules[directive] = CSPRule(directive=directive)
            self._rules[directive].sources = list(set(self._rules[directive].sources + sources))
            self._rules[directive].report_only = report_only
            logger.info(f"Added CSP rule: {directive.value} -> {sources}")

    def remove_rule(self, directive: CSPDirective) -> bool:
        """Hapus aturan CSP."""
        with self._lock:
            if directive in self._rules:
                del self._rules[directive]
                logger.info(f"Removed CSP rule: {directive.value}")
                return True
            return False

    def update_rule(self, directive: CSPDirective, sources: List[str], report_only: Optional[bool] = None) -> bool:
        """Update aturan CSP."""
        with self._lock:
            if directive not in self._rules:
                return False
            self._rules[directive].sources = sources
            if report_only is not None:
                self._rules[directive].report_only = report_only
            logger.info(f"Updated CSP rule: {directive.value} -> {sources}")
            return True

    def check_violation(self, url: str, directive: CSPDirective) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah URL melanggar aturan CSP."""
        with self._lock:
            if directive not in self._rules:
                return False

            rule = self._rules[directive]
            parsed = urlparse(url)

            for source in rule.sources:
                if source == "'self'":
                    # Cek apakah URL same-origin
                    if self._is_same_origin(url):
                        return False
                elif source == "data:":
                    if parsed.scheme == "data":
                        return False
                elif source == "'unsafe-inline'":
                    if parsed.scheme == "" and parsed.netloc == "":
                        return False
                elif source == "'unsafe-eval'":
                    if "eval(" in url or "Function(" in url:
                        return False
                elif source.startswith("http://") or source.startswith("https://"):
                    if url.startswith(source):
                        return False
                elif source == "*":
                    return False

            return True

    def _is_same_origin(self, url: str) -> bool:
        """Cek apakah URL same-origin."""
        # Implementasi sederhana: same-origin jika domain sama
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        if not parsed.netloc:
            return True  # Relative URL
        return parsed.netloc == urlparse(self.settings.homepage).netloc

    def log_violation(self, report: CSPReport) -> None:
        """Catat pelanggaran CSP."""
        with self._lock:
            logger.warning(
                f"CSP Violation | Document URI: {report.document_uri} | "
                f"Violated Directive: {report.violated_directive} | "
                f"Blocked URI: {report.blocked_uri}"
            )

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._rules.clear()
            logger.info("ContentSecurityPolicy cleaned up")
