```text
"""
Extension Sandbox Implementation
==================================
Kelas untuk sandbox ekstensi (isolasi eksekusi).
"""

import os
import sys
import subprocess
import threading
import tempfile
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    BrowserError,
    SecurityError,
    ResourceError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.security.sandbox import SandboxManager, SandboxType

# --- ENUM ---
class SandboxMode(Enum):
    """Mode sandbox."""
    STRICT = auto()  # Isolasi penuh (tidak ada akses ke sistem)
    MODERATE = auto()  # Isolasi sebagian (akses terbatas)
    PERMISSIVE = auto()  # Isolasi minimal (untuk ekstensi terpercaya)

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class ExtensionSandbox:
    """Sandbox untuk ekstensi."""
    sandbox_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    extension_id: str = ""
    mode: SandboxMode = SandboxMode.STRICT
    process: Optional[subprocess.Popen] = None
    temp_dir: Optional[Path] = None
    env: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    resource_limits: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    status: str = "idle"  # idle, running, paused, terminated

# --- EXTENSION SANDBOX ---
class ExtensionSandboxManager:
    """Manager untuk sandbox ekstensi."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings, sandbox_manager: SandboxManager):
        self.settings = settings
        self.sandbox_manager = sandbox_manager
        self._sandboxes: Dict[str, ExtensionSandbox] = {}  # sandbox_id -> ExtensionSandbox
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        logger.info("ExtensionSandboxManager initialized")

    def create_sandbox(
        self,
        extension_id: str,
        mode: SandboxMode = SandboxMode.STRICT,
    ) -> ExtensionSandbox:
        """Buat sandbox untuk ekstensi."""
        with self._lock:
            sandbox_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f"musgo_ext_sandbox_{sandbox_id}_"))

            sandbox = ExtensionSandbox(
                sandbox_id=sandbox_id,
                extension_id=extension_id,
                mode=mode,
                temp_dir=temp_dir,
                env=self._get_safe_env(mode),
                resource_limits=self._get_resource_limits(mode),
            )

            self._sandboxes[sandbox_id] = sandbox
            logger.info(f"Created sandbox for extension: {extension_id} | Mode: {mode.name}")
            return sandbox

    def _get_safe_env(self, mode: SandboxMode) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Dapatkan environment variables yang aman."""
        safe_env = os.environ.copy()

        # Hapus variabel sensitif
        unsafe_keys = ["PASSWORD", "TOKEN", "API_KEY", "SECRET"]
        for key in list(safe_env.keys()):
            if any(unsafe in key.upper() for unsafe in unsafe_keys):
                del safe_env[key]

        # Tambahkan variabel sandbox
        safe_env["SANDBOX_MODE"] = mode.name
        safe_env["SANDBOX_ID"] = str(uuid.uuid4())

        if mode == SandboxMode.STRICT:
            # Hapus semua variabel kecuali yang diperlukan
            allowed_keys = ["PATH", "LANG", "HOME"]
            for key in list(safe_env.keys()):
                if key not in allowed_keys:
                    del safe_env[key]

        return safe_env

    def _get_resource_limits(self, mode: SandboxMode) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Dapatkan batasan resource berdasarkan mode."""
        if mode == SandboxMode.STRICT:
            return {
                "cpu_percent": 20.0,
                "memory_mb": 128,
                "disk_mb": 256,
                "max_processes": 5,
            }
        elif mode == SandboxMode.MODERATE:
            return {
                "cpu_percent": 50.0,
                "memory_mb": 512,
                "disk_mb": 1024,
                "max_processes": 10,
            }
        else:  # PERMISSIVE
            return {
                "cpu_percent": 80.0,
                "memory_mb": 1024,
                "disk_mb": 2048,
                "max_processes": 20,
            }

    def execute_in_sandbox(
        self,
        sandbox_id: str,
        command: List[str],
        timeout: int = 30,
    ) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """
        Eksekusi command di dalam sandbox.
        Returns:
            Tuple[bool, str]: (success, output)
        """
        with self._lock:
            if sandbox_id not in self._sandboxes:
                return False, f"Sandbox not found: {sandbox_id}"

            sandbox = self._sandboxes[sandbox_id]
            if sandbox.status != "idle":
                return False, f"Sandbox not idle: {sandbox_id}"

            try:
                # Set status to running
                sandbox.status = "running"

                # Eksekusi command di subprocess
                process = subprocess.Popen(
                    command,
                    cwd=str(sandbox.temp_dir),
                    env=sandbox.env,
                    stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                    stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                    text=True,
                )

                # Tunggu hingga timeout
                try:
                    stdout, stderr = process.communicate(timeout=timeout)
                    output = stdout or stderr
                    success = process.returncode == 0
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    process.kill()
                    output = f"Command timed out after {timeout} seconds"
                    success = False

                sandbox.status = "idle"
                return success, output
            except Exception as e:
                sandbox.status = "idle"
                logger.error(f"Error executing in sandbox {sandbox_id}: {e}")
                return False, str(e)

    def terminate_sandbox(self, sandbox_id: str) -> bool:
        """Terminasi sandbox."""
        with self._lock:
            if sandbox_id not in self._sandboxes:
                return False

            sandbox = self._sandboxes[sandbox_id]
            try:
                if sandbox.process:
                    sandbox.process.terminate()
                    sandbox.process.wait(timeout=5)

                if sandbox.temp_dir and sandbox.temp_dir.exists():
                    shutil.rmtree(sandbox.temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

                sandbox.status = "terminated"
                logger.info(f"Terminated sandbox: {sandbox_id}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to terminate sandbox {sandbox_id}: {e}")
                return False

    def get_sandbox(self, sandbox_id: str) -> Optional[ExtensionSandbox]:
        """Dapatkan sandbox."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._sandboxes.get(sandbox_id)

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan semua sandbox."""
        with self._lock:
            for sandbox_id in list(self._sandboxes.keys()):
                self.terminate_sandbox(sandbox_id)
            self._sandboxes.clear()
            logger.info("ExtensionSandboxManager cleaned up")

"""
Extension API Implementation
============================
API untuk ekstensi berinteraksi dengan browser.
"""

import json
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Callable, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    Tab,
    Permission,
    BrowserError,
    SecurityError,
    logger,
)
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.tab_manager import TabManager
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.security.permission_validator import PermissionValidator

# --- ENUM ---
class APIMethod(Enum):
    """Metode API."""
    GET = auto()
    POST = auto()
    PUT = auto()
    DELETE = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class APIRequest:
    """Request API dari ekstensi."""
    request_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    extension_id: str = ""
    method: APIMethod = APIMethod.GET
    endpoint: str = ""
    data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

@dataclass
class APIResponse:
    """Response API untuk ekstensi."""
    request_id: str = ""
    success: bool = True
    data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    error: Optional[str] = None
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

# --- EXTENSION API ---
class ExtensionAPI:
    """API untuk ekstensi."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        settings: BrowserSettings,
        tab_manager: TabManager,
        permission_validator: PermissionValidator,
    ):
        self.settings = settings
        self.tab_manager = tab_manager
        self.permission_validator = permission_validator
        self._api_handlers: Dict[str, Callable] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._register_default_handlers()
        logger.info("ExtensionAPI initialized")

    def _register_default_handlers(self) -> None:
        """Daftarkan handler API default."""
        # Handler untuk tabs
        self.register_handler("tabs.list", self._handle_tabs_list)
        self.register_handler("tabs.get", self._handle_tabs_get)
        self.register_handler("tabs.create", self._handle_tabs_create)
        self.register_handler("tabs.close", self._handle_tabs_close)
        self.register_handler("tabs.switch", self._handle_tabs_switch)

        # Handler untuk permissions
        self.register_handler("permissions.request", self._handle_permissions_request)

        # Handler untuk storage
        self.register_handler("storage.get", self._handle_storage_get)
        self.register_handler("storage.set", self._handle_storage_set)

        logger.info("Registered default API handlers")

    def register_handler(self, endpoint: str, handler: Callable) -> None:
        """Daftarkan handler untuk endpoint."""
        with self._lock:
            self._api_handlers[endpoint] = handler
            logger.info(f"Registered API handler: {endpoint}")

    def unregister_handler(self, endpoint: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus handler untuk endpoint."""
        with self._lock:
            if endpoint in self._api_handlers:
                del self._api_handlers[endpoint]
                logger.info(f"Unregistered API handler: {endpoint}")
                return True
            return False

    def call_api(self, request: APIRequest) -> APIResponse:
        """Panggil API dari ekstensi."""
        with self._lock:
            if request.endpoint not in self._api_handlers:
                return APIResponse(
                    request_id=request.request_id,
                    success=False,
                    error=f"Endpoint not found: {request.endpoint}",
                )

            try:
                handler = self._api_handlers[request.endpoint]
                data = handler(request)
                return APIResponse(
                    request_id=request.request_id,
                    success=True,
                    data=data,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error in API handler {request.endpoint}: {e}")
                return APIResponse(
                    request_id=request.request_id,
                    success=False,
                    error=str(e),
                )

    # --- DEFAULT HANDLERS ---
    def _handle_tabs_list(self, request: APIRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Handler untuk tabs.list."""
        tabs = self.tab_manager.get_all_tabs()
        return {
            "tabs": [
                {
                    "tab_id": tab.tab_id,
                    "title": tab.title,
                    "url": tab.url,
                    "is_active": tab.tab_id == self.tab_manager.get_active_tab(tab.window_id)?.tab_id,
                    "is_pinned": tab.is_pinned,
                    "is_muted": tab.is_muted,
                }
                for tab in tabs
            ]
        }

    def _handle_tabs_get(self, request: APIRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Handler untuk tabs.get."""
        tab_id = request.data.get("tab_id")
        if not tab_id:
            raise ValidationError("tab_id is required")

        tab = self.tab_manager.get_tab(tab_id)
        if not tab:
            raise SecurityError(f"Tab not found: {tab_id}")

        return {
            "tab": {
                "tab_id": tab.tab_id,
                "title": tab.title,
                "url": tab.url,
                "window_id": tab.window_id,
                "is_active": tab.tab_id == self.tab_manager.get_active_tab(tab.window_id)?.tab_id,
                "is_pinned": tab.is_pinned,
                "is_muted": tab.is_muted,
                "zoom_level": tab.zoom_level,
            }
        }

    def _handle_tabs_create(self, request: APIRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Handler untuk tabs.create."""
        url = request.data.get("url", "about:blank")
        window_id = request.data.get("window_id", "")

        # Cek izin
        if not self.permission_validator.check_permission(
            request.extension_id, Permission.TAB_MANAGEMENT
        ):
            raise SecurityError("Permission denied: TAB_MANAGEMENT")

        tab = self.tab_manager.create_tab(window_id=window_id, url=url)
        return {
            "tab_id": tab.tab_id,
            "success": True,
        }

    def _handle_tabs_close(self, request: APIRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Handler untuk tabs.close."""
        tab_id = request.data.get("tab_id")
        if not tab_id:
            raise ValidationError("tab_id is required")

        # Cek izin
        if not self.permission_validator.check_permission(
            request.extension_id, Permission.TAB_MANAGEMENT
        ):
            raise SecurityError("Permission denied: TAB_MANAGEMENT")

        success = self.tab_manager.close_tab(tab_id)
        return {
            "success": success,
        }

    def _handle_tabs_switch(self, request: APIRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Handler untuk tabs.switch."""
        tab_id = request.data.get("tab_id")
        if not tab_id:
            raise ValidationError("tab_id is required")

        # Cek izin
        if not self.permission_validator.check_permission(
            request.extension_id, Permission.TAB_MANAGEMENT
        ):
            raise SecurityError("Permission denied: TAB_MANAGEMENT")

        success = self.tab_manager.switch_tab(tab_id)
        return {
            "success": success,
        }

    def _handle_permissions_request(self, request: APIRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Handler untuk permissions.request."""
        permission_name = request.data.get("permission")
        if not permission_name:
            raise ValidationError("permission is required")

        try:
            permission = Permission[permission_name]
        except KeyError:
            raise ValidationError(f"Unknown permission: {permission_name}")

        # Request permission (untuk persetujuan admin)
        permission_request = self.permission_validator.request_permission(
            user_id=request.extension_id,
            permission=permission,
        )

        return {
            "request_id": permission_request.request_id,
            "status": permission_request.status,
            "message": "Permission request submitted for approval",
        }

    def _handle_storage_get(self, request: APIRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Handler untuk storage.get."""
        key = request.data.get("key")
        if not key:
            raise ValidationError("key is required")

        # Cek izin
        if not self.permission_validator.check_permission(
            request.extension_id, Permission.READ_FILESYSTEM
        ):
            raise SecurityError("Permission denied: READ_FILESYSTEM")

        # Implementasi sederhana: gunakan dictionary untuk storage
        # Dalam produksi, gunakan database atau file system
        storage = getattr(self, "_extension_storage", {})
        value = storage.get(request.extension_id, {}).get(key)
        return {
            "value": value,
        }

    def _handle_storage_set(self, request: APIRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Handler untuk storage.set."""
        key = request.data.get("key")
        value = request.data.get("value")
        if not key:
            raise ValidationError("key is required")

        # Cek izin
        if not self.permission_validator.check_permission(
            request.extension_id, Permission.WRITE_FILESYSTEM
        ):
            raise SecurityError("Permission denied: WRITE_FILESYSTEM")

        # Implementasi sederhana
        if not hasattr(self, "_extension_storage"):
            self._extension_storage = {}

        if request.extension_id not in self._extension_storage:
            self._extension_storage[request.extension_id] = {}

        self._extension_storage[request.extension_id][key] = value
        return {
            "success": True,
        }

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._api_handlers.clear()
            if hasattr(self, "_extension_storage"):
                self._extension_storage.clear()
            logger.info("ExtensionAPI cleaned up")

---
--- END OF PAR 7 ---
```

```text
"""
Browser Client Implementation (Python SDK)
=========================================
Kelas untuk berinteraksi dengan Browser Module dari Python.
"""

import json
import threading
import requests
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    TabConfig,
    TabState,
    BrowserError,
    NetworkError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class BrowserAPIMethod(Enum):
    """Metode API Browser."""
    GET = "GET"
    POST = "POST"
    PUT = "PUT"
    DELETE = "DELETE"

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class BrowserResponse:
    """Response dari Browser API."""
    success: bool = True
    data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    error: Optional[str] = None
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

# --- BROWSER CLIENT ---
class BrowserClient:
    """Kelas untuk berinteraksi dengan Browser Module."""

    def __init__(self, base_url: str = "http://localhost:8000/api/browser", api_key: Optional[str] = None):
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.api_key = api_key
        self._session = requests.Session()
        self._session.headers.update({
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "User-Agent": "MusGoBrowserPythonSDK/1.0",
        })
        if api_key:
            self._session.headers.update({"Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"})
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        logger.info(f"BrowserClient initialized | Base URL: {self.base_url}")

    def _request(
        self,
        method: BrowserAPIMethod,
        endpoint: str,
        data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Kirim request ke Browser API."""
        url = f"{self.base_url}/{endpoint.lstrip('/')}"
        try:
            with self._lock:
                if method == BrowserAPIMethod.GET:
                    response = self._session.get(url, params=params)
                elif method == BrowserAPIMethod.POST:
                    response = self._session.post(url, json=data)
                elif method == BrowserAPIMethod.PUT:
                    response = self._session.put(url, json=data)
                elif method == BrowserAPIMethod.DELETE:
                    response = self._session.delete(url, json=data)
                else:
                    raise BrowserError(f"Unsupported HTTP method: {method}")

                response.raise_for_status()
                return BrowserResponse(
                    success=True,
                    data=response.json(),
                    timestamp=datetime.now(),
                )
        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
            logger.error(f"Browser API request failed: {e}")
            return BrowserResponse(
                success=False,
                error=str(e),
                timestamp=datetime.now(),
            )

    # --- TAB OPERATIONS ---
    def create_tab(
        self,
        url: str = "about:blank",
        window_id: str = "",
        title: str = "New Tab",
        is_pinned: bool = False,
    ) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Buat tab baru."""
        data = {
            "url": url,
            "window_id": window_id,
            "title": title,
            "is_pinned": is_pinned,
        }
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.POST, "tabs", data)

    def close_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Tutup tab."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.DELETE, f"tabs/{tab_id}")

    def switch_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Pindah ke tab."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.POST, f"tabs/{tab_id}/switch")

    def get_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Dapatkan informasi tab."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.GET, f"tabs/{tab_id}")

    def get_all_tabs(self) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Dapatkan semua tab."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.GET, "tabs")

    def update_tab(self, tab_id: str, **kwargs) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Update tab."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.PUT, f"tabs/{tab_id}", kwargs)

    # --- WINDOW OPERATIONS ---
    def create_window(self) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Buat window baru."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.POST, "windows")

    def close_window(self, window_id: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Tutup window."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.DELETE, f"windows/{window_id}")

    def get_all_windows(self) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Dapatkan semua window."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.GET, "windows")

    # --- NAVIGATION ---
    def navigate(self, tab_id: str, url: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Navigasi ke URL."""
        data = {"url": url}
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.POST, f"tabs/{tab_id}/navigate", data)

    def reload_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Reload tab."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.POST, f"tabs/{tab_id}/reload")

    def go_back(self, tab_id: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Kembali ke halaman sebelumnya."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.POST, f"tabs/{tab_id}/back")

    def go_forward(self, tab_id: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Maju ke halaman berikutnya."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.POST, f"tabs/{tab_id}/forward")

    # --- SETTINGS ---
    def get_settings(self) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Dapatkan settings browser."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.GET, "settings")

    def update_settings(self, **kwargs) -> BrowserResponse:
        """Update settings browser."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.PUT, "settings", kwargs)

    # --- SESSION ---
    def save_session(self, session_id: str = "default") -> BrowserResponse:
        """Simpan session."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.POST, f"sessions/{session_id}/save")

    def load_session(self, session_id: str = "default") -> BrowserResponse:
        """Muat session."""
        return self._request(BrowserAPIMethod.GET, f"sessions/{session_id}")

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._session.close()
            logger.info("BrowserClient cleaned up")

"""
AI Client Implementation (Python SDK)
=====================================
Kelas untuk berinteraksi dengan AI Module dari Python.
"""

import json
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    AIConfig,
    BrowserError,
    NetworkError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class AIModel(Enum):
    """Model AI yang didukung."""
    MUSGO_OPTIMIZED = "musgo-optimized"
    MUSGO_STANDARD = "musgo-standard"
    GPT4 = "gpt-4"
    GPT35 = "gpt-3.5-turbo"
    CLAUDE = "claude-3"
    LLAMA = "llama-3"

class AIMode(Enum):
    """Mode AI."""
    STANDARD = "standard"
    CREATIVE = "creative"
    PRECISE = "precise"
    ANALYTICAL = "analytical"
    CONCISE = "concise"
    DETAILED = "detailed"

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class AIMessage:
    """Pesan AI."""
    role: str  # system, user, assistant
    content: str
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

@dataclass
class AIConversation:
    """Percakapan AI."""
    conversation_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    title: str = "New Conversation"
    messages: List[AIMessage] = field(default_factory=list)
    model: AIModel = AIModel.MUSGO_OPTIMIZED
    mode: AIMode = AIMode.STANDARD
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

@dataclass
class AIResponse:
    """Response dari AI API."""
    success: bool = True
    conversation_id: str = ""
    message_id: str = ""
    content: str = ""
    model: str = ""
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    error: Optional[str] = None

# --- AI CLIENT ---
class AIClient:
    """Kelas untuk berinteraksi dengan AI Module."""

    def __init__(self, base_url: str = "http://localhost:8000/api/ai", api_key: Optional[str] = None):
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.api_key = api_key
        self._session = requests.Session()
        self._session.headers.update({
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "User-Agent": "MusGoBrowserAISDK/1.0",
        })
        if api_key:
            self._session.headers.update({"Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"})
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._conversations: Dict[str, AIConversation] = {}
        logger.info(f"AIClient initialized | Base URL: {self.base_url}")

    def _request(
        self,
        method: str,
        endpoint: str,
        data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Kirim request ke AI API."""
        url = f"{self.base_url}/{endpoint.lstrip('/')}"
        try:
            with self._lock:
                if method == "GET":
                    response = self._session.get(url)
                elif method == "POST":
                    response = self._session.post(url, json=data)
                elif method == "PUT":
                    response = self._session.put(url, json=data)
                elif method == "DELETE":
                    response = self._session.delete(url)
                else:
                    raise BrowserError(f"Unsupported HTTP method: {method}")

                response.raise_for_status()
                return response.json()
        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
            logger.error(f"AI API request failed: {e}")
            raise NetworkError(f"AI API request failed: {e}")

    def create_conversation(self, title: str = "New Conversation", model: AIModel = AIModel.MUSGO_OPTIMIZED) -> AIConversation:
        """Buat percakapan baru."""
        try:
            data = self._request("POST", "conversations", {
                "title": title,
                "model": model.value,
            })
            conversation = AIConversation(
                conversation_id=data["conversation_id"],
                title=data["title"],
                model=model,
            )
            self._conversations[conversation.conversation_id] = conversation
            logger.info(f"Created AI conversation: {conversation.conversation_id}")
            return conversation
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to create conversation: {e}")
            raise BrowserError(f"Failed to create conversation: {e}")

    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> Optional[AIConversation]:
        """Dapatkan percakapan."""
        return self._conversations.get(conversation_id)

    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus percakapan."""
        try:
            self._request("DELETE", f"conversations/{conversation_id}")
            if conversation_id in self._conversations:
                del self._conversations[conversation_id]
            logger.info(f"Deleted AI conversation: {conversation_id}")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to delete conversation {conversation_id}: {e}")
            return False

    def send_message(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        content: str,
        mode: AIMode = AIMode.STANDARD,
    ) -> AIResponse:
        """Kirim pesan ke AI."""
        try:
            data = self._request("POST", f"conversations/{conversation_id}/messages", {
                "content": content,
                "mode": mode.value,
            })

            response = AIResponse(
                success=True,
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                message_id=data["message_id"],
                content=data["content"],
                model=data["model"],
                timestamp=datetime.now(),
            )

            # Update percakapan lokal
            if conversation_id in self._conversations:
                conversation = self._conversations[conversation_id]
                conversation.messages.append(AIMessage(
                    role="user",
                    content=content,
                ))
                conversation.messages.append(AIMessage(
                    role="assistant",
                    content=data["content"],
                ))
                conversation.updated_at = datetime.now()

            logger.info(f"Sent AI message | Conversation: {conversation_id} | Content: {content[:50]}...")
            return response
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to send message: {e}")
            return AIResponse(
                success=False,
                error=str(e),
                timestamp=datetime.now(),
            )

    def optimize_prompt(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        optimization_level: str = "advanced",
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Optimalkan prompt."""
        try:
            data = self._request("POST", "prompt/optimize", {
                "prompt": prompt,
                "optimization_level": optimization_level,
            })
            logger.info(f"Optimized prompt: {prompt[:50]}...")
            return data
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to optimize prompt: {e}")
            raise BrowserError(f"Failed to optimize prompt: {e}")

    def get_models(self) -> List[str]:
        """Dapatkan daftar model AI yang tersedia."""
        try:
            data = self._request("GET", "models")
            return data.get("models", [])
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to get models: {e}")
            return []

    def get_conversations(self) -> List[AIConversation]:
        """Dapatkan semua percakapan."""
        return list(self._conversations.values())

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._conversations.clear()
            self._session.close()
            logger.info("AIClient cleaned up")

"""
Tab Manager Client (Python SDK)
================================
Kelas untuk mengelola tab dari Python SDK.
"""

import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    TabConfig,
    TabState,
    BrowserError,
    logger,
)
from .browser_client import BrowserClient

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class Tab:
    """Representasi tab untuk SDK."""
    tab_id: str
    window_id: str
    url: str
    title: str
    state: TabState
    is_pinned: bool = False
    is_muted: bool = False
    zoom_level: float = 1.0
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "Tab":
        """Buat Tab dari dictionary."""
        return cls(
            tab_id=data.get("tab_id", ""),
            window_id=data.get("window_id", ""),
            url=data.get("url", ""),
            title=data.get("title", ""),
            state=TabState[data.get("state", "ACTIVE")],
            is_pinned=data.get("is_pinned", False),
            is_muted=data.get("is_muted", False),
            zoom_level=data.get("zoom_level", 1.0),
        )

# --- TAB MANAGER CLIENT ---
class TabManagerClient:
    """Kelas untuk mengelola tab dari Python SDK."""

    def __init__(self, browser_client: BrowserClient):
        self.browser_client = browser_client
        self._tabs: Dict[str, Tab] = {}  # tab_id -> Tab
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        logger.info("TabManagerClient initialized")

    def create_tab(
        self,
        url: str = "about:blank",
        window_id: str = "",
        title: str = "New Tab",
        is_pinned: bool = False,
    ) -> Optional[Tab]:
        """Buat tab baru."""
        with self._lock:
            response = self.browser_client.create_tab(url, window_id, title, is_pinned)
            if not response.success:
                logger.error(f"Failed to create tab: {response.error}")
                return None

            tab_data = response.data.get("tab", {})
            tab = Tab.from_dict(tab_data)
            self._tabs[tab.tab_id] = tab
            logger.info(f"Created tab: {tab.tab_id} | URL: {url}")
            return tab

    def close_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Tutup tab."""
        with self._lock:
            response = self.browser_client.close_tab(tab_id)
            if response.success:
                if tab_id in self._tabs:
                    del self._tabs[tab_id]
                logger.info(f"Closed tab: {tab_id}")
                return True
            logger.error(f"Failed to close tab {tab_id}: {response.error}")
            return False

    def switch_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Pindah ke tab."""
        response = self.browser_client.switch_tab(tab_id)
        if response.success:
            logger.info(f"Switched to tab: {tab_id}")
            return True
        logger.error(f"Failed to switch to tab {tab_id}: {response.error}")
        return False

    def get_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> Optional[Tab]:
        """Dapatkan tab."""
        with self._lock:
            if tab_id in self._tabs:
                return self._tabs[tab_id]

            response = self.browser_client.get_tab(tab_id)
            if response.success:
                tab_data = response.data.get("tab", {})
                tab = Tab.from_dict(tab_data)
                self._tabs[tab.tab_id] = tab
                return tab
            return None

    def get_all_tabs(self) -> List[Tab]:
        """Dapatkan semua tab."""
        with self._lock:
            response = self.browser_client.get_all_tabs()
            if response.success:
                tabs = [Tab.from_dict(tab_data) for tab_data in response.data.get("tabs", [])]
                self._tabs = {tab.tab_id: tab for tab in tabs}
                return tabs
            return list(self._tabs.values())

    def update_tab(self, tab_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update tab."""
        response = self.browser_client.update_tab(tab_id, **kwargs)
        if response.success:
            if tab_id in self._tabs:
                for key, value in kwargs.items():
                    if hasattr(self._tabs[tab_id], key):
                        setattr(self._tabs[tab_id], key, value)
            logger.info(f"Updated tab {tab_id}: {kwargs}")
            return True
        logger.error(f"Failed to update tab {tab_id}: {response.error}")
        return False

    def navigate(self, tab_id: str, url: str) -> bool:
        """Navigasi ke URL."""
        response = self.browser_client.navigate(tab_id, url)
        if response.success:
            logger.info(f"Navigated tab {tab_id} to {url}")
            return True
        logger.error(f"Failed to navigate tab {tab_id} to {url}: {response.error}")
        return False

    def reload_tab(self, tab_id: str) -> bool:
        """Reload tab."""
        response = self.browser_client.reload_tab(tab_id)
        if response.success:
            logger.info(f"Reloaded tab: {tab_id}")
            return True
        logger.error(f"Failed to reload tab {tab_id}: {response.error}")
        return False

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._tabs.clear()
            logger.info("TabManagerClient cleaned up")

"""
Session Manager Client (Python SDK)
===================================
Kelas untuk mengelola session dari Python SDK.
"""

import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserError,
    logger,
)
from .browser_client import BrowserClient

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class Session:
    """Representasi session untuk SDK."""
    session_id: str
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    tab_count: int = 0
    window_count: int = 0

# --- SESSION MANAGER CLIENT ---
class SessionManagerClient:
    """Kelas untuk mengelola session dari Python SDK."""

    def __init__(self, browser_client: BrowserClient):
        self.browser_client = browser_client
        self._sessions: Dict[str, Session] = {}  # session_id -> Session
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        logger.info("SessionManagerClient initialized")

    def save_session(self, session_id: str = "default") -> bool:
        """Simpan session."""
        response = self.browser_client.save_session(session_id)
        if response.success:
            logger.info(f"Saved session: {session_id}")
            return True
        logger.error(f"Failed to save session {session_id}: {response.error}")
        return False

    def load_session(self, session_id: str = "default") -> bool:
        """Muat session."""
        response = self.browser_client.load_session(session_id)
        if response.success:
            logger.info(f"Loaded session: {session_id}")
            return True
        logger.error(f"Failed to load session {session_id}: {response.error}")
        return False

    def delete_session(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus session."""
        # Note: Belum diimplementasikan di BrowserClient
        logger.warning("delete_session not implemented in BrowserClient")
        return False

    def get_sessions(self) -> List[Session]:
        """Dapatkan daftar session."""
        # Note: Belum diimplementasikan di BrowserClient
        logger.warning("get_sessions not implemented in BrowserClient")
        return list(self._sessions.values())

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._sessions.clear()
            logger.info("SessionManagerClient cleaned up")

"""
Browser SDK Types (TypeScript)
===============================
Tipe-tipe untuk Browser SDK.
"""

import { Tab, Window, BrowserSettings } from './types';

export interface BrowserClient {
  // Tab operations
  createTab(url?: string, windowId?: string, title?: string, isPinned?: boolean): Promise<Tab>;
  closeTab(tabId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  switchTab(tabId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  getTab(tabId: string): Promise<Tab | null>;
  getAllTabs(): Promise<Tab[]>;
  updateTab(tabId: string, updates: Partial<Tab>): Promise<boolean>;

  // Window operations
  createWindow(): Promise<Window>;
  closeWindow(windowId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  getAllWindows(): Promise<Window[]>;

  // Navigation
  navigate(tabId: string, url: string): Promise<boolean>;
  reloadTab(tabId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  goBack(tabId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  goForward(tabId: string): Promise<boolean>;

  // Settings
  getSettings(): Promise<BrowserSettings>;
  updateSettings(settings: Partial<BrowserSettings>): Promise<boolean>;

  // Session
  saveSession(sessionId?: string): Promise<boolean>;
  loadSession(sessionId?: string): Promise<boolean>;
}

export class BrowserClientImpl implements BrowserClient {
  private baseUrl: string;
  private apiKey?: string;

  constructor(baseUrl: string = 'http://localhost:8000/api/browser', apiKey?: string) {
    this.baseUrl = baseUrl.endsWith('/') ? baseUrl.slice(0, -1) : baseUrl;
    this.apiKey = apiKey;
  }

  private async request<T>(
    method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE',
    endpoint: string,
    data?: any
  ): Promise<T> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/${endpoint}`;
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
    if (this.apiKey) {
      headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`;
    }

    const options: RequestInit = {
      method,
      headers,
    };

    if (data) {
      options.body = JSON.stringify(data);
    }

    const response = await fetch(url, options);
    if (!response.ok) {
      const error = await response.text();
      throw new Error(`Browser API request failed: ${error}`);
    }
    return response.json() as Promise<T>;
  }

  // Implementasi semua metode BrowserClient
  async createTab(url: string = 'about:blank', windowId: string = '', title: string = 'New Tab', isPinned: boolean = false): Promise<Tab> {
    const data = await this.request<Tab>('POST', 'tabs', { url, windowId, title, isPinned });
    return data;
  }

  async closeTab(tabId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('DELETE', `tabs/${tabId}`);
    return data.success;
  }

  async switchTab(tabId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('POST', `tabs/${tabId}/switch`);
    return data.success;
  }

  async getTab(tabId: string): Promise<Tab | null> {
    try {
      const data = await this.request<Tab>('GET', `tabs/${tabId}`);
      return data;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async getAllTabs(): Promise<Tab[]> {
    const data = await this.request<{ tabs: Tab[] }>('GET', 'tabs');
    return data.tabs;
  }

  async updateTab(tabId: string, updates: Partial<Tab>): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('PUT', `tabs/${tabId}`, updates);
    return data.success;
  }

  async createWindow(): Promise<Window> {
    const data = await this.request<Window>('POST', 'windows');
    return data;
  }

  async closeWindow(windowId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('DELETE', `windows/${windowId}`);
    return data.success;
  }

  async getAllWindows(): Promise<Window[]> {
    const data = await this.request<{ windows: Window[] }>('GET', 'windows');
    return data.windows;
  }

  async navigate(tabId: string, url: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('POST', `tabs/${tabId}/navigate`, { url });
    return data.success;
  }

  async reloadTab(tabId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('POST', `tabs/${tabId}/reload`);
    return data.success;
  }

  async goBack(tabId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('POST', `tabs/${tabId}/back`);
    return data.success;
  }

  async goForward(tabId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('POST', `tabs/${tabId}/forward`);
    return data.success;
  }

  async getSettings(): Promise<BrowserSettings> {
    const data = await this.request<BrowserSettings>('GET', 'settings');
    return data;
  }

  async updateSettings(settings: Partial<BrowserSettings>): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('PUT', 'settings', settings);
    return data.success;
  }

  async saveSession(sessionId: string = 'default'): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('POST', `sessions/${sessionId}/save`);
    return data.success;
  }

  async loadSession(sessionId: string = 'default'): Promise<boolean> {
    const data = await this.request<{ success: boolean }>('GET', `sessions/${sessionId}`);
    return data.success;
  }
}

"""
AI SDK Types (TypeScript)
=========================
Tipe-tipe untuk AI SDK.
"""

import { AIModel, AIMode } from './types';

export interface AIMessage {
  role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant';
  content: string;
  timestamp: string;
}

export interface AIConversation {
  conversationId: string;
  title: string;
  messages: AIMessage[];
  model: AIModel;
  mode: AIMode;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface AIResponse {
  success: boolean;
  conversationId: string;
  messageId: string;
  content: string;
  model: string;
  timestamp: string;
  error?: string;
}

export interface AIClient {
  // Conversation operations
  createConversation(title?: string, model?: AIModel): Promise<AIConversation>;
  getConversation(conversationId: string): Promise<AIConversation | null>;
  deleteConversation(conversationId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  getConversations(): Promise<AIConversation[]>;

  // Message operations
  sendMessage(conversationId: string, content: string, mode?: AIMode): Promise<AIResponse>;

  // Prompt operations
  optimizePrompt(prompt: string, optimizationLevel?: string): Promise<any>;

  // Utility
  getModels(): Promise<string[]>;
}

export class AIClientImpl implements AIClient {
  private baseUrl: string;
  private apiKey?: string;
  private conversations: Map<string, AIConversation> = new Map();

  constructor(baseUrl: string = 'http://localhost:8000/api/ai', apiKey?: string) {
    this.baseUrl = baseUrl.endsWith('/') ? baseUrl.slice(0, -1) : baseUrl;
    this.apiKey = apiKey;
  }

  private async request<T>(
    method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE',
    endpoint: string,
    data?: any
  ): Promise<T> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/${endpoint}`;
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
    if (this.apiKey) {
      headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`;
    }

    const options: RequestInit = {
      method,
      headers,
    };

    if (data) {
      options.body = JSON.stringify(data);
    }

    const response = await fetch(url, options);
    if (!response.ok) {
      const error = await response.text();
      throw new Error(`AI API request failed: ${error}`);
    }
    return response.json() as Promise<T>;
  }

  async createConversation(title: string = 'New Conversation', model: AIModel = AIModel.MUSGO_OPTIMIZED): Promise<AIConversation> {
    const data = await this.request<{ conversationId: string; title: string; model: string }>(
      'POST',
      'conversations',
      { title, model }
    );

    const conversation: AIConversation = {
      conversationId: data.conversationId,
      title: data.title,
      messages: [],
      model: model,
      mode: AIMode.STANDARD,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    this.conversations.set(conversation.conversationId, conversation);
    return conversation;
  }

  async getConversation(conversationId: string): Promise<AIConversation | null> {
    return this.conversations.get(conversationId) || null;
  }

  async deleteConversation(conversationId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await this.request('DELETE', `conversations/${conversationId}`);
      this.conversations.delete(conversationId);
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  async getConversations(): Promise<AIConversation[]> {
    return Array.from(this.conversations.values());
  }

  async sendMessage(
    conversationId: string,
    content: string,
    mode: AIMode = AIMode.STANDARD
  ): Promise<AIResponse> {
    const data = await this.request<{
      messageId: string;
      content: string;
      model: string;
    }>('POST', `conversations/${conversationId}/messages`, { content, mode });

    const response: AIResponse = {
      success: true,
      conversationId,
      messageId: data.messageId,
      content: data.content,
      model: data.model,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    // Update local conversation
    const conversation = this.conversations.get(conversationId);
    if (conversation) {
      conversation.messages.push({
        role: 'user',
        content,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      });
      conversation.messages.push({
        role: 'assistant',
        content: data.content,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      });
      conversation.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    }

    return response;
  }

  async optimizePrompt(prompt: string, optimizationLevel: string = 'advanced'): Promise<any> {
    return this.request('POST', 'prompt/optimize', { prompt, optimization_level: optimizationLevel });
  }

  async getModels(): Promise<string[]> {
    const data = await this.request<{ models: string[] }>('GET', 'models');
    return data.models;
  }
}

"""
API Utilities (TypeScript)
=========================
Utility untuk panggilan API.
"""

export const fetchWithTimeout = async (
  url: string,
  options: RequestInit = {},
  timeout: number = 5000
): Promise<Response> => {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeout);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      ...options,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    return response;
  } catch (error) {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    throw error;
  }
};

export const handleApiError = (error: unknown): string => {
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    return error.message;
  }
  return 'An unknown error occurred';
};

"""
```
Settings Loader Implementation
==============================
Kelas untuk memuat, menyimpan, dan mengelola settings browser.
"""

import os
import json
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from enum import Enum, auto
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    AIConfig,
    BrowserError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class SettingsSource(Enum):
    """Sumber settings."""
    DEFAULT = auto()
    FILE = auto()
    ENVIRONMENT = auto()
    DATABASE = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class SettingsMetadata:
    """Metadata untuk settings."""
    source: SettingsSource = SettingsSource.DEFAULT
    loaded_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    modified_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    version: str = "1.0"

# --- SETTINGS LOADER ---
class SettingsLoader:
    """Kelas untuk memuat dan mengelola settings."""

    def __init__(self, config_path: Optional[Union[str, Path]] = None):
        self.config_path = Path(config_path) if config_path else (
            Path.home() / ".musgo_os_2in1" / "browser" / "configs"
        )
        self.config_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        self._settings: BrowserSettings = BrowserSettings()
        self._security_policy: SecurityPolicy = SecurityPolicy()
        self._ai_config: AIConfig = AIConfig()
        self._metadata: SettingsMetadata = SettingsMetadata()
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._observers: List[Any] = []
        self._load_all()
        logger.info(f"SettingsLoader initialized | Config Path: {self.config_path}")

    def _load_all(self) -> None:
        """Muat semua konfigurasi."""
        self._load_settings()
        self._load_security_policy()
        self._load_ai_config()

    def _load_settings(self) -> None:
        """Muat settings dari file."""
        settings_file = self.config_path / "browser_settings.json"
        try:
            if settings_file.exists():
                with open(settings_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self._settings = BrowserSettings(**data)
                    self._metadata.source = SettingsSource.FILE
                    self._metadata.loaded_at = datetime.now()
                    logger.info(f"Loaded settings from {settings_file}")
            else:
                self._save_settings()
                logger.info("Created default settings file")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load settings: {e}")
            self._settings = BrowserSettings()

    def _load_security_policy(self) -> None:
        """Muat security policy dari file."""
        policy_file = self.config_path / "security_policies.json"
        try:
            if policy_file.exists():
                with open(policy_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self._security_policy = SecurityPolicy(**data)
                    logger.info(f"Loaded security policy from {policy_file}")
            else:
                self._save_security_policy()
                logger.info("Created default security policy file")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load security policy: {e}")
            self._security_policy = SecurityPolicy()

    def _load_ai_config(self) -> None:
        """Muat AI config dari file."""
        ai_config_file = self.config_path / "ai_config.json"
        try:
            if ai_config_file.exists():
                with open(ai_config_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self._ai_config = AIConfig(**data)
                    logger.info(f"Loaded AI config from {ai_config_file}")
            else:
                self._save_ai_config()
                logger.info("Created default AI config file")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load AI config: {e}")
            self._ai_config = AIConfig()

    def _save_settings(self) -> None:
        """Simpan settings ke file."""
        settings_file = self.config_path / "browser_settings.json"
        try:
            with open(settings_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(asdict(self._settings), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            self._metadata.modified_at = datetime.now()
            logger.info(f"Saved settings to {settings_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to save settings: {e}")

    def _save_security_policy(self) -> None:
        """Simpan security policy ke file."""
        policy_file = self.config_path / "security_policies.json"
        try:
            with open(policy_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(asdict(self._security_policy), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            logger.info(f"Saved security policy to {policy_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to save security policy: {e}")

    def _save_ai_config(self) -> None:
        """Simpan AI config ke file."""
        ai_config_file = self.config_path / "ai_config.json"
        try:
            with open(ai_config_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(asdict(self._ai_config), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            logger.info(f"Saved AI config to {ai_config_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to save AI config: {e}")

    @property
    def settings(self) -> BrowserSettings:
        """Dapatkan settings."""
        return self._settings

    @property
    def security_policy(self) -> SecurityPolicy:
        """Dapatkan security policy."""
        return self._security_policy

    @property
    def ai_config(self) -> AIConfig:
        """Dapatkan AI config."""
        return self._ai_config

    def update_settings(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update settings."""
        with self._lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._settings, key):
                    setattr(self._settings, key, value)
            self._save_settings()
            self._notify_observers("settings_updated", kwargs)

    def update_security_policy(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update security policy."""
        with self._lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._security_policy, key):
                    setattr(self._security_policy, key, value)
            self._save_security_policy()
            self._notify_observers("security_policy_updated", kwargs)

    def update_ai_config(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update AI config."""
        with self._lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._ai_config, key):
                    setattr(self._ai_config, key, value)
            self._save_ai_config()
            self._notify_observers("ai_config_updated", kwargs)

    def reset_to_defaults(self) -> None:
        """Reset semua konfigurasi ke default."""
        with self._lock:
            self._settings = BrowserSettings()
            self._security_policy = SecurityPolicy()
            self._ai_config = AIConfig()
            self._save_settings()
            self._save_security_policy()
            self._save_ai_config()
            self._notify_observers("config_reset", {})
            logger.info("Reset all configurations to defaults")

    def add_observer(self, observer: Any) -> None:
        """Tambahkan observer untuk perubahan konfigurasi."""
        with self._lock:
            if observer not in self._observers:
                self._observers.append(observer)
                logger.info(f"Added settings observer: {observer}")

    def remove_observer(self, observer: Any) -> None:
        """Hapus observer."""
        with self._lock:
            if observer in self._observers:
                self._observers.remove(observer)
                logger.info(f"Removed settings observer: {observer}")

    def _notify_observers(self, event: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Beritahu semua observer."""
        for observer in self._observers:
            if hasattr(observer, "on_config_change"):
                try:
                    observer.on_config_change(event, data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Error in settings observer: {e}")

    def get_metadata(self) -> SettingsMetadata:
        """Dapatkan metadata settings."""
        return self._metadata

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._observers.clear()
            logger.info("SettingsLoader cleaned up")

"""
Policy Enforcer Implementation
===============================
Kelas untuk menegakkan kebijakan browser.
"""

import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    Permission,
    BrowserError,
    SecurityError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)
from .settings_loader import SettingsLoader

# --- ENUM ---
class PolicyType(Enum):
    """Tipe kebijakan."""
    SECURITY = auto()
    PRIVACY = auto()
    RESOURCE = auto()
    ACCESS_CONTROL = auto()

class PolicyAction(Enum):
    """Aksi kebijakan."""
    ALLOW = auto()
    BLOCK = auto()
    WARN = auto()
    LOG = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class PolicyRule:
    """Aturan kebijakan."""
    rule_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    policy_type: PolicyType = PolicyType.SECURITY
    condition: Callable[[Any], bool] = lambda x: True
    action: PolicyAction = PolicyAction.ALLOW
    priority: int = 0
    enabled: bool = True

@dataclass
class PolicyViolation:
    """Pelanggaran kebijakan."""
    violation_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    rule_id: str = ""
    policy_type: PolicyType = PolicyType.SECURITY
    details: str = ""
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- POLICY ENFORCER ---
class PolicyEnforcer:
    """Kelas untuk menegakkan kebijakan."""

    def __init__(self, settings_loader: SettingsLoader):
        self.settings_loader = settings_loader
        self._rules: Dict[str, PolicyRule] = {}
        self._violations: List[PolicyViolation] = []
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._init_default_rules()
        logger.info("PolicyEnforcer initialized")

    def _init_default_rules(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi aturan kebijakan default."""
        # Security policies
        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="block_unsafe_schemes",
            description="Blokir scheme yang tidak aman (misal: javascript:)",
            policy_type=PolicyType.SECURITY,
            condition=lambda request: any(
                request.get("url", "").startswith(scheme)
                for scheme in ["javascript:", "data:text/html", "vbscript:"]
            ),
            action=PolicyAction.BLOCK,
            priority=10,
        ))

        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="warn_on_http",
            description="Peringatan untuk URL HTTP (non-HTTPS)",
            policy_type=PolicyType.SECURITY,
            condition=lambda request: request.get("url", "").startswith("http://"),
            action=PolicyAction.WARN,
            priority=5,
        ))

        # Resource policies
        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="limit_max_tabs",
            description="Batasan maksimal tab",
            policy_type=PolicyType.RESOURCE,
            condition=lambda context: context.get("tab_count", 0) > self.settings_loader.settings.max_tabs,
            action=PolicyAction.BLOCK,
            priority=10,
        ))

        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="limit_max_windows",
            description="Batasan maksimal window",
            policy_type=PolicyType.RESOURCE,
            condition=lambda context: context.get("window_count", 0) > self.settings_loader.settings.max_windows,
            action=PolicyAction.BLOCK,
            priority=10,
        ))

        # Access control policies
        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="require_file_access_permission",
            description="Memerlukan izin untuk akses file system",
            policy_type=PolicyType.ACCESS_CONTROL,
            condition=lambda context: (
                context.get("action") == "read_file" or
                context.get("action") == "write_file"
            ) and Permission.READ_FILESYSTEM not in context.get("permissions", set()),
            action=PolicyAction.BLOCK,
            priority=10,
        ))

        logger.info(f"Initialized {len(self._rules)} default policy rules")

    def add_rule(self, rule: PolicyRule) -> str:
        """Tambahkan aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            rule.rule_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            self._rules[rule.rule_id] = rule
            logger.info(f"Added policy rule: {rule.name}")
            return rule.rule_id

    def remove_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                del self._rules[rule_id]
                logger.info(f"Removed policy rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def update_rule(self, rule_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id not in self._rules:
                return False
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._rules[rule_id], key):
                    setattr(self._rules[rule_id], key, value)
            logger.info(f"Updated policy rule: {rule_id}")
            return True

    def enforce(self, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, List[PolicyViolation]]:
        """
        Tegakkan kebijakan untuk konteks tertentu.
        Returns:
            Tuple[bool, List[PolicyViolation]]: (allowed, violations)
        """
        with self._lock:
            violations = []
            allowed = True

            # Urutkan rule berdasarkan priority (descending)
            sorted_rules = sorted(
                self._rules.values(),
                key=lambda r: r.priority,
                reverse=True,
            )

            for rule in sorted_rules:
                if not rule.enabled:
                    continue

                if rule.condition(context):
                    violation = PolicyViolation(
                        rule_id=rule.rule_id,
                        policy_type=rule.policy_type,
                        details=f"Policy '{rule.name}' triggered: {rule.description}",
                        metadata={"context": context},
                    )
                    violations.append(violation)

                    if rule.action == PolicyAction.BLOCK:
                        allowed = False
                        break
                    elif rule.action == PolicyAction.WARN:
                        logger.warning(f"Policy warning: {violation.details}")

            if violations:
                self._violations.extend(violations)
                logger.info(f"Policy enforcement | Allowed: {allowed} | Violations: {len(violations)}")

            return allowed, violations

    def get_violations(self, limit: int = 100) -> List[PolicyViolation]:
        """Dapatkan daftar pelanggaran kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._violations[-limit:]

    def clear_violations(self) -> None:
        """Hapus semua pelanggaran kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            self._violations.clear()
            logger.info("Cleared policy violations")

    def get_rules(self, policy_type: Optional[PolicyType] = None) -> List[PolicyRule]:
        """Dapatkan daftar aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if policy_type:
                return [rule for rule in self._rules.values() if rule.policy_type == policy_type]
            return list(self._rules.values())

    def enable_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Aktifkan aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                self._rules[rule_id].enabled = True
                logger.info(f"Enabled policy rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def disable_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Nonaktifkan aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                self._rules[rule_id].enabled = False
                logger.info(f"Disabled policy rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._rules.clear()
            self._violations.clear()
            logger.info("PolicyEnforcer cleaned up")

"""
Feature Flags Implementation
============================
Kelas untuk manajemen feature flags.
"""

import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    BrowserError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class FlagStatus(Enum):
    """Status feature flag."""
    ENABLED = auto()
    DISABLED = auto()
    ROLLOUT = auto()  # Sedang di-rollout (A/B testing)

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class FeatureFlag:
    """Feature flag."""
    flag_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    status: FlagStatus = FlagStatus.DISABLED
    rollout_percent: float = 0.0  # 0.0 - 100.0
    conditions: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

@dataclass
class FlagEvaluation:
    """Hasil evaluasi feature flag."""
    flag_id: str = ""
    name: str = ""
    enabled: bool = False
    reason: str = ""
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- FEATURE FLAGS ---
class FeatureFlags:
    """Kelas untuk manajemen feature flags."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings):
        self.settings = settings
        self._flags: Dict[str, FeatureFlag] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._init_default_flags()
        logger.info("FeatureFlags initialized")

    def _init_default_flags(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi feature flags default."""
        default_flags = [
            FeatureFlag(
                name="ai_integration",
                description="Aktifkan integrasi AI (MusGo)",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="ad_blocker",
                description="Aktifkan ad blocker",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="sandboxing",
                description="Aktifkan sandboxing untuk tab",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="private_mode",
                description="Aktifkan private mode (incognito)",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="extensions",
                description="Aktifkan dukungan ekstensi",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="experimental_ui",
                description="Aktifkan UI eksperimental",
                status=FlagStatus.DISABLED,
                rollout_percent=0.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="voice_control",
                description="Aktifkan kontrol suara",
                status=FlagStatus.DISABLED,
                rollout_percent=0.0,
            ),
        ]

        for flag in default_flags:
            self._flags[flag.name] = flag

        logger.info(f"Initialized {len(default_flags)} default feature flags")

    def add_flag(self, flag: FeatureFlag) -> str:
        """Tambahkan feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            flag.flag_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            self._flags[flag.name] = flag
            logger.info(f"Added feature flag: {flag.name}")
            return flag.flag_id

    def remove_flag(self, flag_name: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name in self._flags:
                del self._flags[flag_name]
                logger.info(f"Removed feature flag: {flag_name}")
                return True
            return False

    def update_flag(self, flag_name: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name not in self._flags:
                return False
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._flags[flag_name], key):
                    setattr(self._flags[flag_name], key, value)
            self._flags[flag_name].updated_at = datetime.now()
            logger.info(f"Updated feature flag: {flag_name}")
            return True

    def is_enabled(self, flag_name: str, context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> FlagEvaluation:
        """Cek apakah feature flag diaktifkan."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name not in self._flags:
                return FlagEvaluation(
                    flag_id="",
                    name=flag_name,
                    enabled=False,
                    reason="Flag not found",
                )

            flag = self._flags[flag_name]

            # Cek status
            if flag.status == FlagStatus.DISABLED:
                return FlagEvaluation(
                    flag_id=flag.flag_id,
                    name=flag_name,
                    enabled=False,
                    reason="Flag disabled",
                )

            # Cek rollout percent (untuk ROLLOUT)
            if flag.status == FlagStatus.ROLLOUT:
                if not context:
                    context = {}
                user_id = context.get("user_id", "default")
                # Sederhana: gunakan hash user_id untuk menentukan apakah diaktifkan
                user_hash = hash(user_id) % 100
                if user_hash >= flag.rollout_percent:
                    return FlagEvaluation(
                        flag_id=flag.flag_id,
                        name=flag_name,
                        enabled=False,
                        reason=f"Rollout not reached (user hash: {user_hash}, rollout: {flag.rollout_percent}%)",
                    )

            # Cek conditions
            if flag.conditions:
                for key, value in flag.conditions.items():
                    if key not in context or context[key] != value:
                        return FlagEvaluation(
                            flag_id=flag.flag_id,
                            name=flag_name,
                            enabled=False,
                            reason=f"Condition not met: {key} != {value}",
                        )

            return FlagEvaluation(
                flag_id=flag.flag_id,
                name=flag_name,
                enabled=True,
                reason="Flag enabled",
            )

    def get_flag(self, flag_name: str) -> Optional[FeatureFlag]:
        """Dapatkan feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._flags.get(flag_name)

    def get_all_flags(self) -> List[FeatureFlag]:
        """Dapatkan semua feature flags."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._flags.values())

    def enable_flag(self, flag_name: str) -> bool:
        """Aktifkan feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name in self._flags:
                self._flags[flag_name].status = FlagStatus.ENABLED
                self._flags[flag_name].updated_at = datetime.now()
                logger.info(f"Enabled feature flag: {flag_name}")
                return True
            return False

    def disable_flag(self, flag_name: str) -> bool:
        """Nonaktifkan feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name in self._flags:
                self._flags[flag_name].status = FlagStatus.DISABLED
                self._flags[flag_name].updated_at = datetime.now()
                logger.info(f"Disabled feature flag: {flag_name}")
                return True
            return False

    def set_rollout(self, flag_name: str, percent: float) -> bool:
        """Set rollout percent untuk feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name not in self._flags:
                return False
            if percent < 0 or percent > 100:
                raise ValidationError("Rollout percent must be between 0 and 100")
            self._flags[flag_name].rollout_percent = percent
            self._flags[flag_name].status = FlagStatus.ROLLOUT
            self._flags[flag_name].updated_at = datetime.now()
            logger.info(f"Set rollout for {flag_name}: {percent}%")
            return True

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._flags.clear()
            logger.info("FeatureFlags cleaned up")
