"""
Config Validator Implementation
===============================
Kelas untuk validasi konfigurasi.
"""

import re
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    AIConfig,
    BrowserError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class ValidationLevel(Enum):
    """Level validasi."""
    STRICT = auto()  # Validasi ketat
    MODERATE = auto()  # Validasi sedang
    LOOSE = auto()  # Validasi longgar

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class ValidationResult:
    """Hasil validasi."""
    is_valid: bool = True
    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- CONFIG VALIDATOR ---
class ConfigValidator:
    """Kelas untuk validasi konfigurasi."""

    def __init__(self, validation_level: ValidationLevel = ValidationLevel.STRICT):
        self.validation_level = validation_level
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        logger.info(f"ConfigValidator initialized | Level: {validation_level.name}")

    def validate_settings(self, settings: BrowserSettings) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validasi BrowserSettings."""
        errors = []
        warnings = []

        # Validasi homepage
        if not settings.homepage:
            errors.append("Homepage cannot be empty")
        elif not settings.homepage.startswith(("http://", "https://", "about:")):
            errors.append("Homepage must start with http://, https://, or about:")

        # Validasi default_search_engine
        if not settings.default_search_engine:
            errors.append("Default search engine cannot be empty")
        elif not settings.default_search_engine.startswith("http"):
            errors.append("Default search engine must start with http:// or https://")

        # Validasi max_tabs
        if settings.max_tabs < 1 or settings.max_tabs > 200:
            errors.append("max_tabs must be between 1 and 200")

        # Validasi max_windows
        if settings.max_windows < 1 or settings.max_windows > 20:
            errors.append("max_windows must be between 1 and 20")

        # Validasi cache_size_mb
        if settings.cache_size_mb < 10 or settings.cache_size_mb > 5000:
            errors.append("cache_size_mb must be between 10 and 5000")

        # Validasi session_timeout_minutes
        if settings.session_timeout_minutes < 1 or settings.session_timeout_minutes > 1440:
            errors.append("session_timeout_minutes must be between 1 and 1440")

        # Validasi theme
        if settings.theme not in ["light", "dark", "system"]:
            errors.append("theme must be 'light', 'dark', or 'system'")

        # Validasi font_size
        if settings.font_size < 8 or settings.font_size > 24:
            errors.append("font_size must be between 8 and 24")

        # Validasi proxy_settings (jika ada)
        if settings.proxy_settings:
            proxy = settings.proxy_settings
            if "http" in proxy and not isinstance(proxy["http"], str):
                errors.append("proxy_settings.http must be a string")
            if "https" in proxy and not isinstance(proxy["https"], str):
                errors.append("proxy_settings.https must be a string")

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(errors) == 0,
            errors=errors,
            warnings=warnings,
        )

    def validate_security_policy(self, policy: SecurityPolicy) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validasi SecurityPolicy."""
        errors = []
        warnings = []

        # Validasi allowed_schemes
        if not policy.allowed_schemes:
            errors.append("allowed_schemes cannot be empty")

        # Validasi blocked_schemes
        for scheme in policy.blocked_schemes:
            if scheme not in ["http", "https", "data", "file", "about", "blob"]:
                warnings.append(f"Unrecognized scheme in blocked_schemes: {scheme}")

        # Validasi max_script_length
        if policy.max_script_length < 100 or policy.max_script_length > 10000000:
            errors.append("max_script_length must be between 100 and 10MB")

        # Validasi max_style_length
        if policy.max_style_length < 100 or policy.max_style_length > 5000000:
            errors.append("max_style_length must be between 100 and 5MB")

        # Validasi max_image_size
        if policy.max_image_size < 100 or policy.max_image_size > 50000000:
            errors.append("max_image_size must be between 100 and 50MB")

        # Validasi frame_options
        if policy.frame_options not in ["DENY", "SAMEORIGIN", "ALLOW-FROM"]:
            errors.append("frame_options must be 'DENY', 'SAMEORIGIN', or 'ALLOW-FROM'")

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(errors) == 0,
            errors=errors,
            warnings=warnings,
        )

    def validate_ai_config(self, config: AIConfig) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validasi AIConfig."""
        errors = []
        warnings = []

        # Validasi api_endpoint
        if not config.api_endpoint:
            errors.append("api_endpoint cannot be empty")
        elif not config.api_endpoint.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
            errors.append("api_endpoint must start with http:// or https://")

        # Validasi timeout_seconds
        if config.timeout_seconds < 1 or config.timeout_seconds > 300:
            errors.append("timeout_seconds must be between 1 and 300")

        # Validasi max_tokens
        if config.max_tokens < 1 or config.max_tokens > 100000:
            errors.append("max_tokens must be between 1 and 100000")

        # Validasi temperature
        if config.temperature < 0 or config.temperature > 2:
            errors.append("temperature must be between 0 and 2")

        # Validasi top_p
        if config.top_p < 0 or config.top_p > 1:
            errors.append("top_p must be between 0 and 1")

        # Validasi prompt_optimization_level
        if config.prompt_optimization_level < 1 or config.prompt_optimization_level > 3:
            errors.append("prompt_optimization_level must be between 1 and 3")

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(errors) == 0,
            errors=errors,
            warnings=warnings,
        )

    def validate_all(
        self,
        settings: BrowserSettings,
        security_policy: SecurityPolicy,
        ai_config: AIConfig,
    ) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validasi semua konfigurasi."""
        all_errors = []
        all_warnings = []

        # Validasi settings
        settings_result = self.validate_settings(settings)
        all_errors.extend(settings_result.errors)
        all_warnings.extend(settings_result.warnings)

        # Validasi security policy
        policy_result = self.validate_security_policy(security_policy)
        all_errors.extend(policy_result.errors)
        all_warnings.extend(policy_result.warnings)

        # Validasi AI config
        ai_result = self.validate_ai_config(ai_config)
        all_errors.extend(ai_result.errors)
        all_warnings.extend(ai_result.warnings)

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(all_errors) == 0,
            errors=all_errors,
            warnings=all_warnings,
        )

    def set_validation_level(self, level: ValidationLevel) -> None:
        """Set level validasi."""
        with self._lock:
            self.validation_level = level
            logger.info(f"Set validation level: {level.name}")

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        logger.info("ConfigValidator cleaned up")

"""
MusGo-OS 2in1 - Browser Config Module
======================================
Modul untuk konfigurasi browser.
Berisi:
- SettingsLoader: Pemuatan dan manajemen settings
- PolicyEnforcer: Penegakan kebijakan
- FeatureFlags: Manajemen feature flags
- ConfigValidator: Validasi konfigurasi
"""

from .settings_loader import SettingsLoader

"""
Settings Loader Implementation
==============================
Kelas untuk memuat, menyimpan, dan mengelola settings browser.
"""

import os
import json
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from enum import Enum, auto
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    AIConfig,
    BrowserError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class SettingsSource(Enum):
    """Sumber settings."""
    DEFAULT = auto()
    FILE = auto()
    ENVIRONMENT = auto()
    DATABASE = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class SettingsMetadata:
    """Metadata untuk settings."""
    source: SettingsSource = SettingsSource.DEFAULT
    loaded_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    modified_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    version: str = "1.0"

# --- SETTINGS LOADER ---
class SettingsLoader:
    """Kelas untuk memuat dan mengelola settings."""

    def __init__(self, config_path: Optional[Union[str, Path]] = None):
        self.config_path = Path(config_path) if config_path else (
            Path.home() / ".musgo_os_2in1" / "browser" / "configs"
        )
        self.config_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        self._settings: BrowserSettings = BrowserSettings()
        self._security_policy: SecurityPolicy = SecurityPolicy()
        self._ai_config: AIConfig = AIConfig()
        self._metadata: SettingsMetadata = SettingsMetadata()
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._observers: List[Any] = []
        self._load_all()
        logger.info(f"SettingsLoader initialized | Config Path: {self.config_path}")

    def _load_all(self) -> None:
        """Muat semua konfigurasi."""
        self._load_settings()
        self._load_security_policy()
        self._load_ai_config()

    def _load_settings(self) -> None:
        """Muat settings dari file."""
        settings_file = self.config_path / "browser_settings.json"
        try:
            if settings_file.exists():
                with open(settings_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self._settings = BrowserSettings(**data)
                    self._metadata.source = SettingsSource.FILE
                    self._metadata.loaded_at = datetime.now()
                    logger.info(f"Loaded settings from {settings_file}")
            else:
                self._save_settings()
                logger.info("Created default settings file")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load settings: {e}")
            self._settings = BrowserSettings()

    def _load_security_policy(self) -> None:
        """Muat security policy dari file."""
        policy_file = self.config_path / "security_policies.json"
        try:
            if policy_file.exists():
                with open(policy_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self._security_policy = SecurityPolicy(**data)
                    logger.info(f"Loaded security policy from {policy_file}")
            else:
                self._save_security_policy()
                logger.info("Created default security policy file")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load security policy: {e}")
            self._security_policy = SecurityPolicy()

    def _load_ai_config(self) -> None:
        """Muat AI config dari file."""
        ai_config_file = self.config_path / "ai_config.json"
        try:
            if ai_config_file.exists():
                with open(ai_config_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self._ai_config = AIConfig(**data)
                    logger.info(f"Loaded AI config from {ai_config_file}")
            else:
                self._save_ai_config()
                logger.info("Created default AI config file")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load AI config: {e}")
            self._ai_config = AIConfig()

    def _save_settings(self) -> None:
        """Simpan settings ke file."""
        settings_file = self.config_path / "browser_settings.json"
        try:
            with open(settings_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(asdict(self._settings), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            self._metadata.modified_at = datetime.now()
            logger.info(f"Saved settings to {settings_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to save settings: {e}")

    def _save_security_policy(self) -> None:
        """Simpan security policy ke file."""
        policy_file = self.config_path / "security_policies.json"
        try:
            with open(policy_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(asdict(self._security_policy), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            logger.info(f"Saved security policy to {policy_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to save security policy: {e}")

    def _save_ai_config(self) -> None:
        """Simpan AI config ke file."""
        ai_config_file = self.config_path / "ai_config.json"
        try:
            with open(ai_config_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(asdict(self._ai_config), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            logger.info(f"Saved AI config to {ai_config_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to save AI config: {e}")

    @property
    def settings(self) -> BrowserSettings:
        """Dapatkan settings."""
        return self._settings

    @property
    def security_policy(self) -> SecurityPolicy:
        """Dapatkan security policy."""
        return self._security_policy

    @property
    def ai_config(self) -> AIConfig:
        """Dapatkan AI config."""
        return self._ai_config

    def update_settings(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update settings."""
        with self._lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._settings, key):
                    setattr(self._settings, key, value)
            self._save_settings()
            self._notify_observers("settings_updated", kwargs)

    def update_security_policy(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update security policy."""
        with self._lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._security_policy, key):
                    setattr(self._security_policy, key, value)
            self._save_security_policy()
            self._notify_observers("security_policy_updated", kwargs)

    def update_ai_config(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        """Update AI config."""
        with self._lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._ai_config, key):
                    setattr(self._ai_config, key, value)
            self._save_ai_config()
            self._notify_observers("ai_config_updated", kwargs)

    def reset_to_defaults(self) -> None:
        """Reset semua konfigurasi ke default."""
        with self._lock:
            self._settings = BrowserSettings()
            self._security_policy = SecurityPolicy()
            self._ai_config = AIConfig()
            self._save_settings()
            self._save_security_policy()
            self._save_ai_config()
            self._notify_observers("config_reset", {})
            logger.info("Reset all configurations to defaults")

    def add_observer(self, observer: Any) -> None:
        """Tambahkan observer untuk perubahan konfigurasi."""
        with self._lock:
            if observer not in self._observers:
                self._observers.append(observer)
                logger.info(f"Added settings observer: {observer}")

    def remove_observer(self, observer: Any) -> None:
        """Hapus observer."""
        with self._lock:
            if observer in self._observers:
                self._observers.remove(observer)
                logger.info(f"Removed settings observer: {observer}")

    def _notify_observers(self, event: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Beritahu semua observer."""
        for observer in self._observers:
            if hasattr(observer, "on_config_change"):
                try:
                    observer.on_config_change(event, data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Error in settings observer: {e}")

    def get_metadata(self) -> SettingsMetadata:
        """Dapatkan metadata settings."""
        return self._metadata

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._observers.clear()
            logger.info("SettingsLoader cleaned up")

"""
Policy Enforcer Implementation
===============================
Kelas untuk menegakkan kebijakan browser.
"""

import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    Permission,
    BrowserError,
    SecurityError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)
from .settings_loader import SettingsLoader

# --- ENUM ---
class PolicyType(Enum):
    """Tipe kebijakan."""
    SECURITY = auto()
    PRIVACY = auto()
    RESOURCE = auto()
    ACCESS_CONTROL = auto()

class PolicyAction(Enum):
    """Aksi kebijakan."""
    ALLOW = auto()
    BLOCK = auto()
    WARN = auto()
    LOG = auto()

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class PolicyRule:
    """Aturan kebijakan."""
    rule_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    policy_type: PolicyType = PolicyType.SECURITY
    condition: Callable[[Any], bool] = lambda x: True
    action: PolicyAction = PolicyAction.ALLOW
    priority: int = 0
    enabled: bool = True

@dataclass
class PolicyViolation:
    """Pelanggaran kebijakan."""
    violation_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    rule_id: str = ""
    policy_type: PolicyType = PolicyType.SECURITY
    details: str = ""
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- POLICY ENFORCER ---
class PolicyEnforcer:
    """Kelas untuk menegakkan kebijakan."""

    def __init__(self, settings_loader: SettingsLoader):
        self.settings_loader = settings_loader
        self._rules: Dict[str, PolicyRule] = {}
        self._violations: List[PolicyViolation] = []
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._init_default_rules()
        logger.info("PolicyEnforcer initialized")

    def _init_default_rules(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi aturan kebijakan default."""
        # Security policies
        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="block_unsafe_schemes",
            description="Blokir scheme yang tidak aman (misal: javascript:)",
            policy_type=PolicyType.SECURITY,
            condition=lambda request: any(
                request.get("url", "").startswith(scheme)
                for scheme in ["javascript:", "data:text/html", "vbscript:"]
            ),
            action=PolicyAction.BLOCK,
            priority=10,
        ))

        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="warn_on_http",
            description="Peringatan untuk URL HTTP (non-HTTPS)",
            policy_type=PolicyType.SECURITY,
            condition=lambda request: request.get("url", "").startswith("http://"),
            action=PolicyAction.WARN,
            priority=5,
        ))

        # Resource policies
        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="limit_max_tabs",
            description="Batasan maksimal tab",
            policy_type=PolicyType.RESOURCE,
            condition=lambda context: context.get("tab_count", 0) > self.settings_loader.settings.max_tabs,
            action=PolicyAction.BLOCK,
            priority=10,
        ))

        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="limit_max_windows",
            description="Batasan maksimal window",
            policy_type=PolicyType.RESOURCE,
            condition=lambda context: context.get("window_count", 0) > self.settings_loader.settings.max_windows,
            action=PolicyAction.BLOCK,
            priority=10,
        ))

        # Access control policies
        self.add_rule(PolicyRule(
            name="require_file_access_permission",
            description="Memerlukan izin untuk akses file system",
            policy_type=PolicyType.ACCESS_CONTROL,
            condition=lambda context: (
                context.get("action") == "read_file" or
                context.get("action") == "write_file"
            ) and Permission.READ_FILESYSTEM not in context.get("permissions", set()),
            action=PolicyAction.BLOCK,
            priority=10,
        ))

        logger.info(f"Initialized {len(self._rules)} default policy rules")

    def add_rule(self, rule: PolicyRule) -> str:
        """Tambahkan aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            rule.rule_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            self._rules[rule.rule_id] = rule
            logger.info(f"Added policy rule: {rule.name}")
            return rule.rule_id

    def remove_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                del self._rules[rule_id]
                logger.info(f"Removed policy rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def update_rule(self, rule_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id not in self._rules:
                return False
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._rules[rule_id], key):
                    setattr(self._rules[rule_id], key, value)
            logger.info(f"Updated policy rule: {rule_id}")
            return True

    def enforce(self, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, List[PolicyViolation]]:
        """
        Tegakkan kebijakan untuk konteks tertentu.
        Returns:
            Tuple[bool, List[PolicyViolation]]: (allowed, violations)
        """
        with self._lock:
            violations = []
            allowed = True

            # Urutkan rule berdasarkan priority (descending)
            sorted_rules = sorted(
                self._rules.values(),
                key=lambda r: r.priority,
                reverse=True,
            )

            for rule in sorted_rules:
                if not rule.enabled:
                    continue

                if rule.condition(context):
                    violation = PolicyViolation(
                        rule_id=rule.rule_id,
                        policy_type=rule.policy_type,
                        details=f"Policy '{rule.name}' triggered: {rule.description}",
                        metadata={"context": context},
                    )
                    violations.append(violation)

                    if rule.action == PolicyAction.BLOCK:
                        allowed = False
                        break
                    elif rule.action == PolicyAction.WARN:
                        logger.warning(f"Policy warning: {violation.details}")

            if violations:
                self._violations.extend(violations)
                logger.info(f"Policy enforcement | Allowed: {allowed} | Violations: {len(violations)}")

            return allowed, violations

    def get_violations(self, limit: int = 100) -> List[PolicyViolation]:
        """Dapatkan daftar pelanggaran kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._violations[-limit:]

    def clear_violations(self) -> None:
        """Hapus semua pelanggaran kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            self._violations.clear()
            logger.info("Cleared policy violations")

    def get_rules(self, policy_type: Optional[PolicyType] = None) -> List[PolicyRule]:
        """Dapatkan daftar aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if policy_type:
                return [rule for rule in self._rules.values() if rule.policy_type == policy_type]
            return list(self._rules.values())

    def enable_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Aktifkan aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                self._rules[rule_id].enabled = True
                logger.info(f"Enabled policy rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def disable_rule(self, rule_id: str) -> bool:
        """Nonaktifkan aturan kebijakan."""
        with self._lock:
            if rule_id in self._rules:
                self._rules[rule_id].enabled = False
                logger.info(f"Disabled policy rule: {rule_id}")
                return True
            return False

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._rules.clear()
            self._violations.clear()
            logger.info("PolicyEnforcer cleaned up")

"""
Feature Flags Implementation
============================
Kelas untuk manajemen feature flags.
"""

import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    BrowserError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class FlagStatus(Enum):
    """Status feature flag."""
    ENABLED = auto()
    DISABLED = auto()
    ROLLOUT = auto()  # Sedang di-rollout (A/B testing)

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class FeatureFlag:
    """Feature flag."""
    flag_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    status: FlagStatus = FlagStatus.DISABLED
    rollout_percent: float = 0.0  # 0.0 - 100.0
    conditions: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

@dataclass
class FlagEvaluation:
    """Hasil evaluasi feature flag."""
    flag_id: str = ""
    name: str = ""
    enabled: bool = False
    reason: str = ""
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- FEATURE FLAGS ---
class FeatureFlags:
    """Kelas untuk manajemen feature flags."""

    def __init__(self, settings: BrowserSettings):
        self.settings = settings
        self._flags: Dict[str, FeatureFlag] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._init_default_flags()
        logger.info("FeatureFlags initialized")

    def _init_default_flags(self) -> None:
        """Inisialisasi feature flags default."""
        default_flags = [
            FeatureFlag(
                name="ai_integration",
                description="Aktifkan integrasi AI (MusGo)",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="ad_blocker",
                description="Aktifkan ad blocker",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="sandboxing",
                description="Aktifkan sandboxing untuk tab",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="private_mode",
                description="Aktifkan private mode (incognito)",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="extensions",
                description="Aktifkan dukungan ekstensi",
                status=FlagStatus.ENABLED,
                rollout_percent=100.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="experimental_ui",
                description="Aktifkan UI eksperimental",
                status=FlagStatus.DISABLED,
                rollout_percent=0.0,
            ),
            FeatureFlag(
                name="voice_control",
                description="Aktifkan kontrol suara",
                status=FlagStatus.DISABLED,
                rollout_percent=0.0,
            ),
        ]

        for flag in default_flags:
            self._flags[flag.name] = flag

        logger.info(f"Initialized {len(default_flags)} default feature flags")

    def add_flag(self, flag: FeatureFlag) -> str:
        """Tambahkan feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            flag.flag_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            self._flags[flag.name] = flag
            logger.info(f"Added feature flag: {flag.name}")
            return flag.flag_id

    def remove_flag(self, flag_name: str) -> bool:
        """Hapus feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name in self._flags:
                del self._flags[flag_name]
                logger.info(f"Removed feature flag: {flag_name}")
                return True
            return False

    def update_flag(self, flag_name: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name not in self._flags:
                return False
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(self._flags[flag_name], key):
                    setattr(self._flags[flag_name], key, value)
            self._flags[flag_name].updated_at = datetime.now()
            logger.info(f"Updated feature flag: {flag_name}")
            return True

    def is_enabled(self, flag_name: str, context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> FlagEvaluation:
        """Cek apakah feature flag diaktifkan."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name not in self._flags:
                return FlagEvaluation(
                    flag_id="",
                    name=flag_name,
                    enabled=False,
                    reason="Flag not found",
                )

            flag = self._flags[flag_name]

            # Cek status
            if flag.status == FlagStatus.DISABLED:
                return FlagEvaluation(
                    flag_id=flag.flag_id,
                    name=flag_name,
                    enabled=False,
                    reason="Flag disabled",
                )

            # Cek rollout percent (untuk ROLLOUT)
            if flag.status == FlagStatus.ROLLOUT:
                if not context:
                    context = {}
                user_id = context.get("user_id", "default")
                # Sederhana: gunakan hash user_id untuk menentukan apakah diaktifkan
                user_hash = hash(user_id) % 100
                if user_hash >= flag.rollout_percent:
                    return FlagEvaluation(
                        flag_id=flag.flag_id,
                        name=flag_name,
                        enabled=False,
                        reason=f"Rollout not reached (user hash: {user_hash}, rollout: {flag.rollout_percent}%)",
                    )

            # Cek conditions
            if flag.conditions:
                for key, value in flag.conditions.items():
                    if key not in context or context[key] != value:
                        return FlagEvaluation(
                            flag_id=flag.flag_id,
                            name=flag_name,
                            enabled=False,
                            reason=f"Condition not met: {key} != {value}",
                        )

            return FlagEvaluation(
                flag_id=flag.flag_id,
                name=flag_name,
                enabled=True,
                reason="Flag enabled",
            )

    def get_flag(self, flag_name: str) -> Optional[FeatureFlag]:
        """Dapatkan feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._flags.get(flag_name)

    def get_all_flags(self) -> List[FeatureFlag]:
        """Dapatkan semua feature flags."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._flags.values())

    def enable_flag(self, flag_name: str) -> bool:
        """Aktifkan feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name in self._flags:
                self._flags[flag_name].status = FlagStatus.ENABLED
                self._flags[flag_name].updated_at = datetime.now()
                logger.info(f"Enabled feature flag: {flag_name}")
                return True
            return False

    def disable_flag(self, flag_name: str) -> bool:
        """Nonaktifkan feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name in self._flags:
                self._flags[flag_name].status = FlagStatus.DISABLED
                self._flags[flag_name].updated_at = datetime.now()
                logger.info(f"Disabled feature flag: {flag_name}")
                return True
            return False

    def set_rollout(self, flag_name: str, percent: float) -> bool:
        """Set rollout percent untuk feature flag."""
        with self._lock:
            if flag_name not in self._flags:
                return False
            if percent < 0 or percent > 100:
                raise ValidationError("Rollout percent must be between 0 and 100")
            self._flags[flag_name].rollout_percent = percent
            self._flags[flag_name].status = FlagStatus.ROLLOUT
            self._flags[flag_name].updated_at = datetime.now()
            logger.info(f"Set rollout for {flag_name}: {percent}%")
            return True

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        with self._lock:
            self._flags.clear()
            logger.info("FeatureFlags cleaned up")

"""
Config Validator Implementation
===============================
Kelas untuk validasi konfigurasi.
"""

import re
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import (
    BrowserSettings,
    SecurityPolicy,
    AIConfig,
    BrowserError,
    ValidationError,
    logger,
)

# --- ENUM ---
class ValidationLevel(Enum):
    """Level validasi."""
    STRICT = auto()  # Validasi ketat
    MODERATE = auto()  # Validasi sedang
    LOOSE = auto()  # Validasi longgar

# --- DATA STRUCTURE ---
@dataclass
class ValidationResult:
    """Hasil validasi."""
    is_valid: bool = True
    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

# --- CONFIG VALIDATOR ---
class ConfigValidator:
    """Kelas untuk validasi konfigurasi."""

    def __init__(self, validation_level: ValidationLevel = ValidationLevel.STRICT):
        self.validation_level = validation_level
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        logger.info(f"ConfigValidator initialized | Level: {validation_level.name}")

    def validate_settings(self, settings: BrowserSettings) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validasi BrowserSettings."""
        errors = []
        warnings = []

        # Validasi homepage
        if not settings.homepage:
            errors.append("Homepage cannot be empty")
        elif not settings.homepage.startswith(("http://", "https://", "about:")):
            errors.append("Homepage must start with http://, https://, or about:")

        # Validasi default_search_engine
        if not settings.default_search_engine:
            errors.append("Default search engine cannot be empty")
        elif not settings.default_search_engine.startswith("http"):
            errors.append("Default search engine must start with http:// or https://")

        # Validasi max_tabs
        if settings.max_tabs < 1 or settings.max_tabs > 200:
            errors.append("max_tabs must be between 1 and 200")

        # Validasi max_windows
        if settings.max_windows < 1 or settings.max_windows > 20:
            errors.append("max_windows must be between 1 and 20")

        # Validasi cache_size_mb
        if settings.cache_size_mb < 10 or settings.cache_size_mb > 5000:
            errors.append("cache_size_mb must be between 10 and 5000")

        # Validasi session_timeout_minutes
        if settings.session_timeout_minutes < 1 or settings.session_timeout_minutes > 1440:
            errors.append("session_timeout_minutes must be between 1 and 1440")

        # Validasi theme
        if settings.theme not in ["light", "dark", "system"]:
            errors.append("theme must be 'light', 'dark', or 'system'")

        # Validasi font_size
        if settings.font_size < 8 or settings.font_size > 24:
            errors.append("font_size must be between 8 and 24")

        # Validasi proxy_settings (jika ada)
        if settings.proxy_settings:
            proxy = settings.proxy_settings
            if "http" in proxy and not isinstance(proxy["http"], str):
                errors.append("proxy_settings.http must be a string")
            if "https" in proxy and not isinstance(proxy["https"], str):
                errors.append("proxy_settings.https must be a string")

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(errors) == 0,
            errors=errors,
            warnings=warnings,
        )

    def validate_security_policy(self, policy: SecurityPolicy) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validasi SecurityPolicy."""
        errors = []
        warnings = []

        # Validasi allowed_schemes
        if not policy.allowed_schemes:
            errors.append("allowed_schemes cannot be empty")

        # Validasi blocked_schemes
        for scheme in policy.blocked_schemes:
            if scheme not in ["http", "https", "data", "file", "about", "blob"]:
                warnings.append(f"Unrecognized scheme in blocked_schemes: {scheme}")

        # Validasi max_script_length
        if policy.max_script_length < 100 or policy.max_script_length > 10000000:
            errors.append("max_script_length must be between 100 and 10MB")

        # Validasi max_style_length
        if policy.max_style_length < 100 or policy.max_style_length > 5000000:
            errors.append("max_style_length must be between 100 and 5MB")

        # Validasi max_image_size
        if policy.max_image_size < 100 or policy.max_image_size > 50000000:
            errors.append("max_image_size must be between 100 and 50MB")

        # Validasi frame_options
        if policy.frame_options not in ["DENY", "SAMEORIGIN", "ALLOW-FROM"]:
            errors.append("frame_options must be 'DENY', 'SAMEORIGIN', or 'ALLOW-FROM'")

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(errors) == 0,
            errors=errors,
            warnings=warnings,
        )

    def validate_ai_config(self, config: AIConfig) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validasi AIConfig."""
        errors = []
        warnings = []

        # Validasi api_endpoint
        if not config.api_endpoint:
            errors.append("api_endpoint cannot be empty")
        elif not config.api_endpoint.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
            errors.append("api_endpoint must start with http:// or https://")

        # Validasi timeout_seconds
        if config.timeout_seconds < 1 or config.timeout_seconds > 300:
            errors.append("timeout_seconds must be between 1 and 300")

        # Validasi max_tokens
        if config.max_tokens < 1 or config.max_tokens > 100000:
            errors.append("max_tokens must be between 1 and 100000")

        # Validasi temperature
        if config.temperature < 0 or config.temperature > 2:
            errors.append("temperature must be between 0 and 2")

        # Validasi top_p
        if config.top_p < 0 or config.top_p > 1:
            errors.append("top_p must be between 0 and 1")

        # Validasi prompt_optimization_level
        if config.prompt_optimization_level < 1 or config.prompt_optimization_level > 3:
            errors.append("prompt_optimization_level must be between 1 and 3")

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(errors) == 0,
            errors=errors,
            warnings=warnings,
        )

    def validate_all(
        self,
        settings: BrowserSettings,
        security_policy: SecurityPolicy,
        ai_config: AIConfig,
    ) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validasi semua konfigurasi."""
        all_errors = []
        all_warnings = []

        # Validasi settings
        settings_result = self.validate_settings(settings)
        all_errors.extend(settings_result.errors)
        all_warnings.extend(settings_result.warnings)

        # Validasi security policy
        policy_result = self.validate_security_policy(security_policy)
        all_errors.extend(policy_result.errors)
        all_warnings.extend(policy_result.warnings)

        # Validasi AI config
        ai_result = self.validate_ai_config(ai_config)
        all_errors.extend(ai_result.errors)
        all_warnings.extend(ai_result.warnings)

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(all_errors) == 0,
            errors=all_errors,
            warnings=all_warnings,
        )

    def set_validation_level(self, level: ValidationLevel) -> None:
        """Set level validasi."""
        with self._lock:
            self.validation_level = level
            logger.info(f"Set validation level: {level.name}")

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Bersihkan resource."""
        logger.info("ConfigValidator cleaned up")

---
--- END OF PAR 9 ---
```

"""
Unit Tests for TabManager
========================
Test untuk kelas TabManager.
"""

import unittest
import tempfile
import shutil
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.tab_manager import TabManager, Tab, TabState

class TestTabManager(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.tab_manager = TabManager(self.settings)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tab_manager.cleanup()

    def test_create_tab(self):
        """Test pembuatan tab baru."""
        tab = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com", title="Test Tab")
        self.assertIsInstance(tab, Tab)
        self.assertEqual(tab.url, "https://example.com")
        self.assertEqual(tab.title, "Test Tab")
        self.assertEqual(tab.state, TabState.ACTIVE)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.tab_manager.get_all_tabs()), 1)

    def test_close_tab(self):
        """Test penutupan tab."""
        tab = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com")
        self.assertTrue(self.tab_manager.close_tab(tab.tab_id))
        self.assertEqual(len(self.tab_manager.get_all_tabs()), 0)

    def test_switch_tab(self):
        """Test pergantian tab."""
        tab1 = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example1.com")
        tab2 = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example2.com")
        self.assertTrue(self.tab_manager.switch_tab(tab2.tab_id))
        self.assertEqual(self.tab_manager.get_active_tab(tab2.window_id)?.tab_id, tab2.tab_id)

    def test_duplicate_tab(self):
        """Test duplikasi tab."""
        tab1 = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com")
        tab2 = self.tab_manager.duplicate_tab(tab1.tab_id)
        self.assertIsNotNone(tab2)
        self.assertEqual(tab2.url, tab1.url)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.tab_manager.get_all_tabs()), 2)

    def test_pin_tab(self):
        """Test pin tab."""
        tab = self.tab_manager.create_tab()
        self.assertTrue(self.tab_manager.pin_tab(tab.tab_id))
        self.assertTrue(tab.is_pinned)

    def test_mute_tab(self):
        """Test mute tab."""
        tab = self.tab_manager.create_tab()
        self.assertTrue(self.tab_manager.mute_tab(tab.tab_id))
        self.assertTrue(tab.is_muted)

    def test_set_zoom_level(self):
        """Test set zoom level."""
        tab = self.tab_manager.create_tab()
        self.assertTrue(self.tab_manager.set_zoom_level(tab.tab_id, 1.5))
        self.assertEqual(tab.zoom_level, 1.5)

    def test_max_tabs_limit(self):
        """Test batasan maksimal tab."""
        self.tab_manager.settings.max_tabs = 2
        tab1 = self.tab_manager.create_tab()
        tab2 = self.tab_manager.create_tab()
        with self.assertRaises(Exception):
            self.tab_manager.create_tab()  # Seharusnya gagal

    def test_reorder_tabs(self):
        """Test reorder tab."""
        tab1 = self.tab_manager.create_tab()
        tab2 = self.tab_manager.create_tab()
        tab3 = self.tab_manager.create_tab()
        new_order = [tab3.tab_id, tab1.tab_id, tab2.tab_id]
        self.assertTrue(self.tab_manager.reorder_tabs(tab1.window_id, new_order))

"""
Unit Tests for SessionManager
=============================
Test untuk kelas SessionManager.
"""

import unittest
import tempfile
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.tab_manager import TabManager
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.session_manager import SessionManager

class TestSessionManager(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.tab_manager = TabManager(self.settings)
        self.temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.settings.session_path = str(self.temp_dir)
        self.session_manager = SessionManager(self.settings, self.tab_manager)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.session_manager.cleanup()
        shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_save_session(self):
        """Test penyimpanan session."""
        self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com")
        self.assertTrue(self.session_manager.save_session("test_session"))
        session_file = self.temp_dir / "test_session.session"
        self.assertTrue(session_file.exists())

    def test_load_session(self):
        """Test pemuatan session."""
        self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com")
        self.session_manager.save_session("test_session")
        self.tab_manager.close_all_tabs()
        self.assertTrue(self.session_manager.load_session("test_session"))
        self.assertEqual(len(self.tab_manager.get_all_tabs()), 1)

    def test_delete_session(self):
        """Test penghapusan session."""
        self.session_manager.save_session("test_session")
        self.assertTrue(self.session_manager.delete_session("test_session"))
        session_file = self.temp_dir / "test_session.session"
        self.assertFalse(session_file.exists())

    def test_auto_save(self):
        """Test auto-save."""
        self.session_manager.enable_auto_save(interval=1)
        self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com")
        import time
        time.sleep(2)  # Tunggu auto-save
        session_file = self.temp_dir / "auto_save.session"
        self.assertTrue(session_file.exists())

"""
Unit Tests for SecurityEnforcer
================================
Test untuk kelas SecurityEnforcer.
"""

import unittest
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings, SecurityPolicy
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.security.security_enforcer import SecurityEnforcer, SecurityViolationType

class TestSecurityEnforcer(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.security_policy = SecurityPolicy()
        self.enforcer = SecurityEnforcer(self.settings, self.security_policy)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.enforcer.cleanup()

    def test_enforce_csp(self):
        """Test penegakan CSP."""
        request = {"url": "javascript:alert('XSS')", "headers": {}}
        allowed, violation = self.enforcer.enforce(request)
        self.assertFalse(allowed)
        self.assertEqual(violation.violation_type, SecurityViolationType.CSP_VIOLATION)

    def test_enforce_blocked_domain(self):
        """Test penegakan blocked domain."""
        self.security_policy.blocked_domains = ["example.com"]
        request = {"url": "https://example.com", "headers": {}}
        allowed, violation = self.enforcer.enforce(request)
        self.assertFalse(allowed)
        self.assertEqual(violation.violation_type, SecurityViolationType.BLOCKED_DOMAIN)

    def test_enforce_injection(self):
        """Test penegakan injection."""
        request = {"url": "https://example.com", "body": "<script>alert('XSS')</script>"}
        allowed, violation = self.enforcer.enforce(request)
        self.assertFalse(allowed)
        self.assertEqual(violation.violation_type, SecurityViolationType.INJECTION_ATTEMPT)

    def test_enforce_permission(self):
        """Test penegakan permission."""
        request = {
            "required_permission": "READ_FILESYSTEM",
            "user_permissions": set(),
        }
        allowed, violation = self.enforcer.enforce(request)
        self.assertFalse(allowed)
        self.assertEqual(violation.violation_type, SecurityViolationType.PERMISSION_DENIED)

    def test_allowed_request(self):
        """Test request yang diizinkan."""
        request = {"url": "https://google.com", "headers": {}}
        allowed, violation = self.enforcer.enforce(request)
        self.assertTrue(allowed)
        self.assertIsNone(violation)

"""
Unit Tests for PromptOptimizer
=============================
Test untuk kelas PromptOptimizer.
"""

import unittest
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.prompt_optimizer import PromptOptimizer, OptimizationLevel, PromptType

class TestPromptOptimizer(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.optimizer = PromptOptimizer(self.settings)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.optimizer.cleanup()

    def test_basic_optimization(self):
        """Test optimasi dasar."""
        result = self.optimizer.optimize(
            "tolong jelaskan tentang python",
            optimization_level=OptimizationLevel.BASIC,
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(result.optimized_prompt, "tolong jelaskan tentang python")
        self.assertIn("jelaskan tentang python", result.optimized_prompt)

    def test_advanced_optimization(self):
        """Test optimasi lanjutan."""
        result = self.optimizer.optimize(
            "mungkin bisa jelaskan tentang ai",
            optimization_level=OptimizationLevel.ADVANCED,
        )
        self.assertNotIn("mungkin", result.optimized_prompt)
        self.assertNotIn("bisa", result.optimized_prompt)

    def test_aggressive_optimization(self):
        """Test optimasi agresif."""
        result = self.optimizer.optimize(
            "buat kode python untuk sort",
            optimization_level=OptimizationLevel.AGGRESSIVE,
            prompt_type=PromptType.CODE_GENERATION,
        )
        self.assertIn("Contoh:", result.optimized_prompt)
        self.assertIn("def", result.optimized_prompt)

    def test_detect_prompt_type(self):
        """Test deteksi tipe prompt."""
        self.assertEqual(
            self.optimizer._detect_prompt_type("buat kode python"),
            PromptType.CODE_GENERATION,
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.optimizer._detect_prompt_type("jelaskan tentang ai"),
            PromptType.ANALYSIS,
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.optimizer._detect_prompt_type("ringkas teks ini"),
            PromptType.SUMMARIZATION,
        )

    def test_detect_ambiguity_level(self):
        """Test deteksi level ambiguitas."""
        from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.prompt_optimizer import AmbiguityLevel
        self.assertEqual(
            self.optimizer._detect_ambiguity_level("mungkin bisa"),
            AmbiguityLevel.HIGH,
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.optimizer._detect_ambiguity_level("jelaskan tentang python"),
            AmbiguityLevel.LOW,
        )

"""
Unit Tests for ExtensionLoader
==============================
Test untuk kelas ExtensionLoader.
"""

import unittest
import tempfile
import shutil
import json
from pathlib import Path

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings, ExtensionManifest, Permission
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.security.sandbox import SandboxManager
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.extensions.extension_loader import ExtensionLoader

class TestExtensionLoader(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.settings.extensions_path = str(self.temp_dir)
        self.sandbox_manager = SandboxManager(self.settings)
        self.loader = ExtensionLoader(self.settings, self.sandbox_manager)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.loader.cleanup()
        shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_install_extension(self):
        """Test instalasi ekstensi."""
        # Buat ekstensi dummy
        ext_dir = self.temp_dir / "test_extension"
        ext_dir.mkdir()
        manifest = {
            "name": "Test Extension",
            "version": "1.0.0",
            "description": "Test extension",
            "manifest_version": 3,
            "permissions": ["NETWORK_ACCESS"],
        }
        with open(ext_dir / "manifest.json", "w") as f:
            json.dump(manifest, f)

        success, ext_id = self.loader.install_extension(str(ext_dir))
        self.assertTrue(success)
        self.assertIsNotNone(ext_id)

    def test_load_extension(self):
        """Test pemuatan ekstensi."""
        # Install dulu
        ext_dir = self.temp_dir / "test_extension"
        ext_dir.mkdir()
        manifest = {
            "name": "Test Extension",
            "version": "1.0.0",
            "description": "Test extension",
            "manifest_version": 3,
            "permissions": [],
        }
        with open(ext_dir / "manifest.json", "w") as f:
            json.dump(manifest, f)

        success, ext_id = self.loader.install_extension(str(ext_dir))
        self.assertTrue(success)

        # Load ekstensi
        self.assertTrue(self.loader.load_extension(ext_id))
        ext = self.loader.get_extension(ext_id)
        self.assertEqual(ext?.status.name, "LOADED")

    def test_unload_extension(self):
        """Test unload ekstensi."""
        # Install dan load
        ext_dir = self.temp_dir / "test_extension"
        ext_dir.mkdir()
        manifest = {
            "name": "Test Extension",
            "version": "1.0.0",
            "description": "Test extension",
            "manifest_version": 3,
            "permissions": [],
        }
        with open(ext_dir / "manifest.json", "w") as f:
            json.dump(manifest, f)

        success, ext_id = self.loader.install_extension(str(ext_dir))
        self.assertTrue(success)
        self.loader.load_extension(ext_id)

        # Unload
        self.assertTrue(self.loader.unload_extension(ext_id))
        ext = self.loader.get_extension(ext_id)
        self.assertEqual(ext?.status.name, "UNLOADED")

"""
Integration Tests for Browser Core
=================================
Test integrasi untuk core browser.
"""

import unittest
import tempfile
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings, SecurityPolicy, AIConfig
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.tab_manager import TabManager
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.security.security_enforcer import SecurityEnforcer
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.engine.rendering import CEFRenderer
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.engine.networking import NetworkManager

class TestBrowserCoreIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.security_policy = SecurityPolicy()
        self.tab_manager = TabManager(self.settings)
        self.security_enforcer = SecurityEnforcer(self.settings, self.security_policy)
        self.network_manager = NetworkManager(self.settings, self.security_policy)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tab_manager.cleanup()
        self.security_enforcer.cleanup()
        self.network_manager.cleanup()

    def test_tab_creation_with_security(self):
        """Test pembuatan tab dengan penegakan keamanan."""
        # Buat tab
        tab = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com")
        self.assertIsNotNone(tab)

        # Cek keamanan URL
        request = {"url": tab.url, "headers": {}}
        allowed, violation = self.security_enforcer.enforce(request)
        self.assertTrue(allowed)

    def test_network_request_with_csp(self):
        """Test request jaringan dengan CSP."""
        # Tambahkan blocked domain
        self.security_policy.blocked_domains = ["malicious.com"]
        self.network_manager._security_policy = self.security_policy

        # Cek request ke domain terblokir
        request = {"url": "https://malicious.com", "headers": {}}
        allowed, violation = self.security_enforcer.enforce(request)
        self.assertFalse(allowed)

    def test_tab_and_session_integration(self):
        """Test integrasi tab dan session."""
        # Buat tab
        tab1 = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example1.com")
        tab2 = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example2.com")

        # Simpan session
        from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.session_manager import SessionManager
        session_manager = SessionManager(self.settings, self.tab_manager)
        session_manager.save_session("test_integration")

        # Tutup semua tab
        self.tab_manager.close_all_tabs()

        # Muat session
        session_manager.load_session("test_integration")
        tabs = self.tab_manager.get_all_tabs()
        self.assertEqual(len(tabs), 2)

"""
Integration Tests for AI Module
===============================
Test integrasi untuk modul AI.
"""

import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings, AIConfig
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.prompt_optimizer import PromptOptimizer
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.chatbot_integration import ChatbotIntegration

class TestAIIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.ai_config = AIConfig()
        self.prompt_optimizer = PromptOptimizer(self.settings)
        self.chatbot = ChatbotIntegration(self.settings, self.ai_config, self.prompt_optimizer)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.prompt_optimizer.cleanup()
        self.chatbot.cleanup()

    @patch('musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.chatbot_integration.requests.post')
    def test_send_message(self, mock_post):
        """Test pengiriman pesan ke AI."""
        # Mock response
        mock_post.return_value.status_code = 200
        mock_post.return_value.json.return_value = {
            "choices": [{"message": {"content": "Ini adalah respons AI"}}]
        }

        # Buat percakapan
        conversation = self.chatbot.create_conversation()
        self.assertIsNotNone(conversation)

        # Kirim pesan
        with patch.object(self.chatbot, '_call_ai_api', return_value="Ini adalah respons AI"):
            message = self.chatbot.send_message(
                conversation.conversation_id,
                "Halo, AI!",
            )
        self.assertIsNotNone(message)
        self.assertEqual(message.content, "Ini adalah respons AI")

    def test_prompt_optimization_integration(self):
        """Test integrasi optimasi prompt."""
        conversation = self.chatbot.create_conversation()
        with patch.object(self.chatbot, '_call_ai_api', return_value="Respons"):
            message = self.chatbot.send_message(
                conversation.conversation_id,
                "mungkin jelaskan tentang python",
                use_optimization=True,
            )
        self.assertIsNotNone(message)
        self.assertIsNotNone(message.metadata.get("optimized_prompt"))

"""
E2E Tests for Browser Workflow
==============================
Test untuk alur kerja browser.
"""

import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.tab_manager import TabManager
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.session_manager import SessionManager
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.security.security_enforcer import SecurityEnforcer

class TestBrowserWorkflow(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.tab_manager = TabManager(self.settings)
        self.session_manager = SessionManager(self.settings, self.tab_manager)
        self.security_enforcer = SecurityEnforcer(self.settings, self.settings.security_policy)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tab_manager.cleanup()
        self.session_manager.cleanup()
        self.security_enforcer.cleanup()

    def test_full_browser_workflow(self):
        """Test alur kerja browser lengkap."""
        # 1. Buat tab
        tab1 = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com")
        self.assertIsNotNone(tab1)

        # 2. Navigasi ke URL
        self.assertTrue(self.tab_manager.update_tab(tab1.tab_id, url="https://google.com"))

        # 3. Buat tab baru
        tab2 = self.tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://github.com")
        self.assertIsNotNone(tab2)

        # 4. Pindah tab
        self.assertTrue(self.tab_manager.switch_tab(tab2.tab_id))

        # 5. Simpan session
        self.assertTrue(self.session_manager.save_session("workflow_test"))

        # 6. Tutup semua tab
        self.tab_manager.close_all_tabs()

        # 7. Muat session
        self.assertTrue(self.session_manager.load_session("workflow_test"))
        tabs = self.tab_manager.get_all_tabs()
        self.assertEqual(len(tabs), 2)

        # 8. Cek keamanan
        request = {"url": "https://google.com", "headers": {}}
        allowed, violation = self.security_enforcer.enforce(request)
        self.assertTrue(allowed)

"""
E2E Tests for AI Chat
====================
Test untuk alur AI chat.
"""

import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings, AIConfig
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.prompt_optimizer import PromptOptimizer
from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.chatbot_integration import ChatbotIntegration

class TestAIChatWorkflow(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.settings = BrowserSettings()
        self.ai_config = AIConfig()
        self.prompt_optimizer = PromptOptimizer(self.settings)
        self.chatbot = ChatbotIntegration(self.settings, self.ai_config, self.prompt_optimizer)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.prompt_optimizer.cleanup()
        self.chatbot.cleanup()

    @patch('musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.chatbot_integration.requests.post')
    def test_ai_chat_workflow(self, mock_post):
        """Test alur AI chat."""
        # Mock API response
        mock_post.return_value.status_code = 200
        mock_post.return_value.json.return_value = {
            "choices": [{"message": {"content": "Halo! Bagaimana saya bisa membantu?"}}]
        }

        # 1. Buat percakapan
        conversation = self.chatbot.create_conversation(title="Test Chat")
        self.assertIsNotNone(conversation)

        # 2. Kirim pesan
        with patch.object(self.chatbot, '_call_ai_api', return_value="Halo! Bagaimana saya bisa membantu?"):
            message = self.chatbot.send_message(
                conversation.conversation_id,
                "Halo, AI!",
            )
        self.assertIsNotNone(message)
        self.assertEqual(message.content, "Halo! Bagaimana saya bisa membantu?")

        # 3. Kirim pesan kedua
        with patch.object(self.chatbot, '_call_ai_api', return_value="Baik, terima kasih!"):
            message2 = self.chatbot.send_message(
                conversation.conversation_id,
                "Terima kasih!",
            )
        self.assertIsNotNone(message2)

        # 4. Dapatkan semua percakapan
        conversations = self.chatbot.get_all_conversations()
        self.assertEqual(len(conversations), 1)

        # 5. Arsipkan percakapan
        self.assertTrue(self.chatbot.archive_conversation(conversation.conversation_id))

```markdown
# MusGo-OS 2in1 - Browser Module Developer Guide
==================================================

## 1. Overview
Modul **Browser** dalam **MusGo-OS 2in1** adalah komponen inti yang menyediakan fungsionalitas browser dengan integrasi **AI (MusGo)**, **keamanan enterprise-grade**, dan **ekosistem ekstensi**.

### Fitur Utama:
- **Multi-Tab Management**: Manajemen tab dengan dukungan pin, mute, zoom, dan reorder.
- **AI Integration**: Optimasi prompt, chatbot, dan integrasi dengan **MusGo AI**.
- **Security Hardening**: CSP, sandboxing, anti-tracking, rate limiting, dan permission validation.
- **Session Management**: Simpan/load state tab, window, dan percakapan AI.
- **Extensions Support**: Dukungan untuk ekstensi custom (ad blocker, dark mode, dll.).
- **Cross-Platform**: Kompatibel dengan Windows, Linux, dan macOS.

---

## 2. Arsitektur
```
musgo-os-2in1/apps/browser/
├── core/                  # Inti browser (rendering, networking, security)
│   ├── engine/            # CEF/WebKit, HTTP stack
│   ├── session/           # Tab, session, cookie
│   ├── security/          # CSP, sandbox, integrity guard
│   └── ai/                # Prompt optimizer, chatbot
├── ui/                    # Antarmuka pengguna (React/TypeScript)
│   ├── components/        # TabBar, Sidebar, AIChatPanel
│   ├── styles/            # CSS/SCSS
│   └── themes/            # Dark/light mode
├── extensions/            # Ekstensi browser
│   ├── extension_loader/  # Pemuatan ekstensi
│   ├── sandbox/           # Isolasi ekstensi
│   └── api/               # API untuk ekstensi
├── sdk/                   # SDK (Python & TypeScript)
│   ├── python/            # BrowserClient, AIClient
│   └── typescript/        # Type definitions, utilities
└── tests/                 # Unit, integration, E2E tests
```

---

## 3. Setup & Instalasi

### Prerequisites:
- Python 3.10+
- Node.js 18+
- CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) untuk rendering
- Redis (opsional, untuk session caching)

### Instalasi:
```bash
# Clone repository
git clone https://github.com/musgo-os-2in1/browser.git
cd browser

# Install Python dependencies
pip install -r requirements.txt

# Install TypeScript dependencies
cd ui
npm install
npm run build
```

### Konfigurasi:
1. Salin file konfigurasi default:
   ```bash
   cp configs/browser_settings.json.example configs/browser_settings.json
   cp configs/security_policies.json.example configs/security_policies.json
   ```
2. Sesuaikan konfigurasi sesuai kebutuhan.

---

## 4. Core Components

### 4.1 TabManager
- **Fungsi**: Manajemen tab (buat, tutup, switch, duplicate, dll.).
- **File**: `core/session/tab_manager.py`
- **Contoh Penggunaan**:
  ```python
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.session.tab_manager import TabManager
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings

  settings = BrowserSettings()
  tab_manager = TabManager(settings)

  # Buat tab baru
  tab = tab_manager.create_tab(url="https://example.com", title="Contoh Tab")
  print(f"Tab created: {tab.tab_id}")

  # Pindah tab
  tab_manager.switch_tab(tab.tab_id)
  ```

### 4.2 SecurityEnforcer
- **Fungsi**: Penegakan kebijakan keamanan (CSP, blocked domains, injection prevention).
- **File**: `core/security/security_enforcer.py`
- **Contoh Penggunaan**:
  ```python
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.core.security.security_enforcer import SecurityEnforcer
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings, SecurityPolicy

  settings = BrowserSettings()
  policy = SecurityPolicy()
  enforcer = SecurityEnforcer(settings, policy)

  # Cek request
  request = {"url": "https://example.com", "headers": {}}
  allowed, violation = enforcer.enforce(request)
  if not allowed:
      print(f"Request blocked: {violation.details}")
  ```

### 4.3 PromptOptimizer
- **Fungsi**: Optimasi prompt sebelum dikirim ke AI.
- **File**: `ai/prompt_optimizer.py`
- **Contoh Penggunaan**:
  ```python
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.prompt_optimizer import PromptOptimizer, OptimizationLevel
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings

  settings = BrowserSettings()
  optimizer = PromptOptimizer(settings)

  # Optimalkan prompt
  result = optimizer.optimize(
      "mungkin jelaskan tentang python",
      optimization_level=OptimizationLevel.ADVANCED,
  )
  print(f"Original: {result.original_prompt}")
  print(f"Optimized: {result.optimized_prompt}")
  ```

### 4.4 ChatbotIntegration
- **Fungsi**: Integrasi chatbot AI.
- **File**: `ai/chatbot_integration.py`
- **Contoh Penggunaan**:
  ```python
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.chatbot_integration import ChatbotIntegration
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings, AIConfig
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.ai.prompt_optimizer import PromptOptimizer

  settings = BrowserSettings()
  ai_config = AIConfig()
  optimizer = PromptOptimizer(settings)
  chatbot = ChatbotIntegration(settings, ai_config, optimizer)

  # Buat percakapan
  conversation = chatbot.create_conversation(title="Test Chat")

  # Kirim pesan
  with patch.object(chatbot, '_call_ai_api', return_value="Ini adalah respons AI"):
      message = chatbot.send_message(conversation.conversation_id, "Halo, AI!")
  print(f"AI Response: {message.content}")
  ```

---

## 5. UI Components (TypeScript/React)

### 5.1 TabBar
- **Fungsi**: Menampilkan dan mengelola tab.
- **File**: `ui/components/TabBar.tsx`
- **Props**:
  ```typescript
  interface TabBarProps {
    tabs: Tab[];
    activeTabId: string;
    onTabClick: (tabId: string) => void;
    onTabClose: (tabId: string) => void;
    onNewTab: () => void;
    onTabReorder: (newOrder: string[]) => void;
    settings: BrowserSettings;
    theme: Theme;
  }
  ```

### 5.2 Sidebar
- **Fungsi**: Sidebar untuk bookmark, history, dan AI chat.
- **File**: `ui/components/Sidebar.tsx`
- **Props**:
  ```typescript
  interface SidebarProps {
    tabs: Tab[];
    activeTabId: string;
    conversations: Conversation[];
    onTabClick: (tabId: string) => void;
    onConversationClick: (conversationId: string) => void;
    theme: Theme;
  }
  ```

### 5.3 AIChatPanel
- **Fungsi**: Panel chat AI.
- **File**: `ui/components/AIChatPanel.tsx`
- **Props**:
  ```typescript
  interface AIChatPanelProps {
    conversation: Conversation | null;
    onSendMessage: (content: string) => void;
    theme: Theme;
  }
  ```

---

## 6. Extensions

### 6.1 Membuat Ekstensi
1. Buat folder ekstensi dengan struktur:
   ```
   my_extension/
   ├── manifest.json
   ├── background.js (opsional)
   ├── content.js (opsional)
   └── styles.css (opsional)
   ```
2. Buat `manifest.json`:
   ```json
   {
     "name": "My Extension",
     "version": "1.0.0",
     "description": "Deskripsi ekstensi",
     "manifest_version": 3,
     "permissions": ["NETWORK_ACCESS", "TAB_MANAGEMENT"],
     "background_scripts": ["background.js"],
     "content_scripts": [
       {
         "matches": ["<all_urls>"],
         "js": ["content.js"],
         "css": ["styles.css"]
       }
     ]
   }
   ```
3. Install ekstensi:
   ```python
   from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.extensions.extension_loader import ExtensionLoader
   from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser import BrowserSettings

   settings = BrowserSettings()
   loader = ExtensionLoader(settings)
   success, ext_id = loader.install_extension("/path/to/my_extension")
   ```

### 6.2 Extension API
Ekstensi dapat berinteraksi dengan browser menggunakan **ExtensionAPI**:
```javascript
// Contoh: Mendapatkan daftar tab
const tabs = await musgo.browser.tabs.list();
console.log(tabs);

// Contoh: Membuat tab baru
await musgo.browser.tabs.create({ url: "https://example.com" });
```

---

## 7. SDK (Python & TypeScript)

### 7.1 Python SDK
- **BrowserClient**: Klien untuk berinteraksi dengan Browser API.
  ```python
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.sdk.python.browser_client import BrowserClient

  client = BrowserClient(base_url="http://localhost:8000/api/browser")
  tab = client.create_tab(url="https://example.com")
  print(f"Created tab: {tab['tab_id']}")
  ```

- **AIClient**: Klien untuk berinteraksi dengan AI API.
  ```python
  from musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.sdk.python.ai_client import AIClient

  ai_client = AIClient(base_url="http://localhost:8000/api/ai")
  conversation = ai_client.create_conversation(title="Test")
  response = ai_client.send_message(conversation.conversation_id, "Halo!")
  print(response.content)
  ```

### 7.2 TypeScript SDK
- **BrowserClientImpl**: Implementasi klien browser.
  ```typescript
  import { BrowserClientImpl } from './sdk/typescript/browser';

  const client = new BrowserClientImpl('http://localhost:8000/api/browser');
  const tab = await client.createTab('https://example.com');
  console.log(`Created tab: ${tab.tabId}`);
  ```

- **AIClientImpl**: Implementasi klien AI.
  ```typescript
  import { AIClientImpl, AIModel } from './sdk/typescript/ai';

  const aiClient = new AIClientImpl('http://localhost:8000/api/ai');
  const conversation = await aiClient.createConversation('Test Chat', AIModel.MUSGO_OPTIMIZED);
  const response = await aiClient.sendMessage(conversation.conversationId, 'Halo!');
  console.log(response.content);
  ```

---

## 8. Testing

### 8.1 Unit Tests
Jalankan unit tests:
```bash
python -m unittest discover -s tests/unit
```

### 8.2 Integration Tests
Jalankan integration tests:
```bash
python -m unittest discover -s tests/integration
```

### 8.3 E2E Tests
Jalankan E2E tests (memerlukan browser headless):
```bash
python -m unittest discover -s tests/e2e
```

---

## 9. Deployment

### 9.1 Build
```bash
# Build Python package
python setup.py sdist bdist_wheel

# Build TypeScript
cd ui
npm run build
```

### 9.2 Run
```bash
# Jalankan server browser
python -m musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser

# Atau gunakan FastAPI
uvicorn musgo_os_2in1.apps.browser.api:app --reload
```

---
## 10. Troubleshooting

| **Masalah**                          | **Solusi**                                                                 |
|--------------------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| CEF tidak terinstall                 | `pip install cefpython3`                                                 |
| Error "Sandbox not initialized"      | Pastikan `enable_sandboxing=True` di settings                              |
| AI API tidak merespons               | Cek `ai_config.api_endpoint` dan `api_key`                                |
| Ekstensi tidak dimuat                | Pastikan `manifest.json` valid dan permissions terpenuhi                  |
| Tab tidak bisa dibuka                | Cek `max_tabs` di settings dan batasan resource                           |

---

1. Fork repository.
2. Buat branch (`git checkout -b feature/nama-fitur`).
3. Commit perubahan (`git commit -m "Tambah fitur X"`).
4. Push ke branch (`git push origin feature/nama-fitur`).
5. Buat Pull Request.
