```markdown
# MusGo-OS 2in1 - Browser API Reference
=======================================

## 1. Overview
API Reference untuk **Browser Module** di **MusGo-OS 2in1**. API ini memungkinkan interaksi dengan browser melalui **HTTP/REST** atau **SDK (Python/TypeScript)**.

---

## 2. Base URL
```
http://localhost:8000/api/browser
```

---
## 3. Authentication
Semua request memerlukan **API Key** di header:
```http
Authorization: Bearer YOUR_API_KEY
```

---
## 4. Endpoints

---
### 4.1 Tabs
| **Endpoint**               | **Method** | **Deskripsi**                     | **Request Body**                          | **Response**                          |
|----------------------------|------------|-----------------------------------|------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------|
| `/tabs`                    | POST       | Buat tab baru                     | `{ url, window_id, title, is_pinned }`   | `{ tab_id, url, title, ... }`          |
| `/tabs/{tab_id}`           | GET        | Dapatkan info tab                 | -                                        | `{ tab_id, url, title, ... }`          |
| `/tabs`                    | GET        | Dapatkan semua tab                | -                                        | `{ tabs: [...] }`                     |
| `/tabs/{tab_id}`           | PUT        | Update tab                        | `{ url, title, is_pinned, ... }`         | `{ success: true }`                    |
| `/tabs/{tab_id}`           | DELETE     | Tutup tab                         | -                                        | `{ success: true }`                    |
| `/tabs/{tab_id}/switch`    | POST       | Pindah ke tab                     | -                                        | `{ success: true }`                    |
| `/tabs/{tab_id}/navigate`  | POST       | Navigasi ke URL                  | `{ url }`                                | `{ success: true }`                    |
| `/tabs/{tab_id}/reload`    | POST       | Reload tab                       | -                                        | `{ success: true }`                    |
| `/tabs/{tab_id}/back`      | POST       | Kembali ke halaman sebelumnya     | -                                        | `{ success: true }`                    |
| `/tabs/{tab_id}/forward`   | POST       | Maju ke halaman berikutnya        | -                                        | `{ success: true }`                    |

**Contoh Request (POST /tabs):**
```http
POST /api/browser/tabs HTTP/1.1
Authorization: Bearer YOUR_API_KEY
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "url": "https://example.com",
  "title": "Contoh Tab",
  "is_pinned": false
}
```

**Contoh Response:**
```json
{
  "tab_id": "tab_1_0",
  "url": "https://example.com",
  "title": "Contoh Tab",
  "window_id": "window_1",
  "state": "ACTIVE",
  "is_pinned": false,
  "is_muted": false,
  "zoom_level": 1.0
}
```

---
### 4.2 Windows
| **Endpoint**          | **Method** | **Deskripsi**               | **Request Body** | **Response**               |
|-----------------------|------------|-----------------------------|------------------|----------------------------|
| `/windows`            | POST       | Buat window baru            | -                | `{ window_id, tabs: [] }`  |
| `/windows/{window_id}`| GET        | Dapatkan info window        | -                | `{ window_id, tabs: [...] }`|
| `/windows`            | GET        | Dapatkan semua window       | -                | `{ windows: [...] }`        |
| `/windows/{window_id}`| DELETE     | Tutup window                | -                | `{ success: true }`         |

---
### 4.3 Sessions
| **Endpoint**               | **Method** | **Deskripsi**               | **Request Body** | **Response**               |
|----------------------------|------------|-----------------------------|------------------|----------------------------|
| `/sessions/{session_id}/save` | POST     | Simpan session              | -                | `{ success: true }`         |
| `/sessions/{session_id}`    | GET        | Muat session                | -                | `{ tabs: [...], windows: [...] }` |
| `/sessions/{session_id}`    | DELETE     | Hapus session               | -                | `{ success: true }`         |

---
### 4.4 AI Integration
| **Endpoint**                          | **Method** | **Deskripsi**                     | **Request Body**                          | **Response**                          |
|---------------------------------------|------------|-----------------------------------|------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------|
| `/ai/conversations`                   | POST       | Buat percakapan AI                | `{ title, model }`                        | `{ conversation_id, title, model }`    |
| `/ai/conversations/{conversation_id}` | GET        | Dapatkan percakapan AI            | -                                        | `{ conversation_id, messages: [...] }` |
| `/ai/conversations/{conversation_id}` | DELETE     | Hapus percakapan AI               | -                                        | `{ success: true }`                    |
| `/ai/conversations/{conversation_id}/messages` | POST | Kirim pesan ke AI | `{ content, mode }` | `{ message_id, content, model }` |
| `/ai/prompt/optimize`                  | POST       | Optimalkan prompt                 | `{ prompt, optimization_level }`         | `{ original_prompt, optimized_prompt }` |

**Contoh Request (POST /ai/conversations/{id}/messages):**
```http
POST /api/browser/ai/conversations/conv_1/messages HTTP/1.1
Authorization: Bearer YOUR_API_KEY
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "content": "Jelaskan tentang Python",
  "mode": "standard"
}
```

**Contoh Response:**
```json
{
  "message_id": "msg_1",
  "content": "Python adalah bahasa pemrograman serbaguna yang digunakan untuk...",
  "model": "musgo-optimized",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-06T10:00:00Z"
}
```

---
### 4.5 Extensions
| **Endpoint**               | **Method** | **Deskripsi**               | **Request Body** | **Response**               |
|----------------------------|------------|-----------------------------|------------------|----------------------------|
| `/extensions`              | POST       | Install ekstensi            | `{ path }`       | `{ extension_id, success }` |
| `/extensions/{extension_id}`| GET        | Dapatkan info ekstensi      | -                | `{ name, version, ... }`    |
| `/extensions/{extension_id}`| DELETE     | Uninstall ekstensi          | -                | `{ success: true }`         |
| `/extensions/{extension_id}/enable` | POST | Aktifkan ekstensi | - | `{ success: true }` |
| `/extensions/{extension_id}/disable` | POST | Nonaktifkan ekstensi | - | `{ success: true }` |

---
### 4.6 Settings
| **Endpoint** | **Method** | **Deskripsi**               | **Request Body** | **Response**               |
|--------------|------------|-----------------------------|------------------|----------------------------|
| `/settings`  | GET        | Dapatkan settings           | -                | `{ homepage, max_tabs, ... }` |
| `/settings`  | PUT        | Update settings             | `{ homepage, ... }` | `{ success: true }` |

---
## 5. Error Codes
| **Code** | **Deskripsi**                          | **Solusi**                                  |
|----------|----------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| 400      | Bad Request (input tidak valid)        | Perbaiki input request                     |
| 401      | Unauthorized (API Key tidak valid)     | Gunakan API Key yang benar                 |
| 403      | Forbidden (izin tidak mencukupi)       | Cek permissions                            |
| 404      | Not Found (resource tidak ditemukan)    | Pastikan endpoint/ID benar                 |
| 429      | Too Many Requests (rate limit)        | Tunggu sebelum mencoba lagi                |
| 500      | Internal Server Error                  | Cek log server                             |

---
## 6. WebSocket Events
Browser Module juga mendukung **WebSocket** untuk event real-time.

**Endpoint:**
```
ws://localhost:8000/ws/browser
```

**Event Types:**
| **Event**               | **Deskripsi**                          | **Data**                          |
|-------------------------|----------------------------------------|-----------------------------------|
| `tab_created`           | Tab baru dibuat                        | `{ tab_id, url, title }`          |
| `tab_closed`            | Tab ditutup                            | `{ tab_id }`                      |
| `tab_switched`          | Tab dipindah                           | `{ tab_id }`                      |
| `url_changed`           | URL tab berubah                        | `{ tab_id, url }`                 |
| `ai_message_received`   | Pesan AI diterima                      | `{ conversation_id, content }`    |
| `security_violation`    | Pelanggaran keamanan terdeteksi        | `{ violation_type, details }`     |

**Contoh WebSocket Client (JavaScript):**
```javascript
const socket = new WebSocket('ws://localhost:8000/ws/browser');

socket.onmessage = (event) => {
  const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
  console.log(`Event: ${data.event_type}`, data.data);
};

socket.onopen = () => {
  console.log('Connected to Browser WebSocket');
};
```
